Keys (paired state moved to PairedServerStore in #295): `themeMode: Flow<ThemeMode>` (#86 — `stringPreferencesKey("theme_mode")` holding the enum's `.name`; `entries.firstOrNull { it.name == stored } ?: SYSTEM` fallback for both absent + unparseable; consumed reactively at `MainActivity`'s `setContent` root to resolve `PyrycodeMobileTheme(darkTheme = …)` and inside `composable(Routes.SETTINGS)` to drive the Theme row subtitle), `useWallpaperColors: Flow<Boolean>` (#88 — `booleanPreferencesKey("use_wallpaper_colors")`, default `false`; collected as a sibling to `themeMode` at `setContent`'s root and forwarded into `PyrycodeMobileTheme(dynamicColor = …)`, whose internal `Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= S` gate at `Theme.kt:275` handles the pre-S fall-through to the brand palette; since #89 also collected inside `composable(Routes.SETTINGS)` via `SettingsViewModel.useWallpaperColors` for the visible Appearance switch row, with `setUseWallpaperColors(...)` wired from the row's `onCheckedChange` → `SettingsViewModel.onToggleUseWallpaperColors`). The "Defaults for new conversations" schema landed in #231 added four more keys (schema-only — no UI/consumer wiring yet): `defaultModel: Flow<Model>` (`stringPreferencesKey("default_model")` holding `.name`; tolerant-unknown fallback `Model.entries.firstOrNull { it.name == stored } ?: Model.OPUS_4_7` mirroring `themeMode`'s shape — sibling enum `Model { OPUS_4_7, SONNET_4_6, HAIKU_4_5 }` in `data/preferences/Model.kt`, originally identifier-only with no `.label()` extension since the first UI consumer owns display strings per the Compose Multiplatform `data/`-portability constraint — the decision was **partially walked back in [#253](codebase/253.md)** when two UI consumers materialised in the same release window (the [`ThreadStatusRow`](features/thread-status-row.md) label derivation in #253 + the [#254](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/254) Status Sheet radio group); a top-level `fun Model.label(): String` extension was co-located with the enum (returning vendor product names `"Opus 4.7"` / `"Sonnet 4.6"` / `"Haiku 4.5"` as Kotlin literals, not `stringResource` lookups — the names don't localise). The walk-back is **scoped to `Model`**; `Effort` and `ThemeMode` keep the original first-UI-consumer-owns-the-labels convention. First read consumer of `defaultModel` itself also landed in #253 — `ThreadViewModel.selectedModelFlow` pre-combines `appPreferences.defaultModel` with an in-memory `MutableStateFlow<Model?>` per-conversation override), `defaultEffort: Flow<Effort>` (same shape, fallback `Effort.HIGH`; sibling enum `Effort { LOW, MEDIUM, HIGH, XHIGH, MAX }`), `defaultYolo: Flow<Boolean>` (`booleanPreferencesKey("default_yolo")`, default `false`), `defaultWorkspace: Flow<String>` (`stringPreferencesKey("default_workspace")`, default `DEFAULT_SCRATCH_CWD` imported from `de.pyryco.mobile.data.model` — deliberately not a `sealed Workspace { Scratch; Bound(cwd) }`, reusing the existing string-sentinel convention `Conversation.cwd` already encodes). First *read* consumer landed in #240 — [`ChannelListViewModel.CreateDiscussionTapped`](features/channel-list-viewmodel.md) reads `defaultWorkspace` via `.first()` inside the existing `viewModelScope.launch { … }` and passes it to `repository.createDiscussion(workspace = …)`; the long-press FAB (`WorkspacePicked`) keeps bypassing the read since an explicit user pick always overrides the default. First *write* consumer landed in [#235](codebase/235.md) — the Settings "Default workspace" row opens the reused `WorkspacePicker` host and persists the pick via `SettingsViewModel.onSelectDefaultWorkspace(...)`, closing the write→read loop with #240. The first **Notifications** key `notificationsEnabled: Flow<Boolean>` landed in [#268](codebase/268.md) (`booleanPreferencesKey("notifications_enabled")`, **default `true`** — the only `?: true` getter in the file, Figma-sourced from node `17:2`'s ON toggle; getter/setter mirror the `defaultYolo` block with the default deliberately inverted; consumed by the Settings "Push notifications when claude responds" switch via `SettingsViewModel.onTogglePushNotifications`; write-live / read-dead until Phase 4 notification delivery). Still pending: the new-conversation materialiser that reads `defaultModel` / `defaultEffort` / `defaultYolo` together, and the StatusSheet override stubs in #228 (model override) and #229 (effort override) that read `defaultModel` / `defaultEffort` read-only. Key-count threshold (`~5 → consider split`) crossed, now at **9**; the split's named trigger — the first non-defaults Phase-3 key (notifications) — landed at [#268](codebase/268.md) and the split was **not** taken (#268 added `notificationsEnabled` inline as XS plumbing mirroring `defaultYolo`), so the seam has now been reached and passed unevaluated. The four defaults still form one logical group; revisit a split only if the Notifications group grows past one key. The Phase 4 FCM push key `pushToken: Flow<String?>` landed in [#364](codebase/364.md) (`stringPreferencesKey("push_token")`) — the **first key in the file with no fallback default**: the getter is the bare `dataStore.data.map { prefs -> prefs[PUSH_TOKEN] }` (no `?: …` elvis), so absence reads as `null`, the load-bearing "dormant / nothing to register yet" signal the connect-time re-registration reader branches on. `suspend fun setPushToken(token)` overwrites last-write-wins (the rotation path #361's Firebase `onNewToken` drives). The FCM token is a low-value, rotating wake *address* (not a credential — pushing also needs the project's FCM server key on the daemon), so it lives in plain `DataStore`, **not** Keystore under `data/crypto/` — classified by value × revocability, the first deliberate non-secret token here. **Dormant**: origin is the #361 Firebase slice (`onNewToken`), reader is the connect-time re-registration hook ([#365](codebase/365.md), landed) that calls [`RemoteConversationRepository.registerPushToken`](features/remote-conversation-repository.md) ([#359](codebase/359.md)); never logged (AC #5, grep-verified, not unit-tested). Count is now **9**.
- [ConversationRow](features/conversation-row.md) — stateless M3 `ListItem`-based row consuming `Conversation`; auto-name fallback branched on `isPromoted` and kotlinx-datetime relative-time bucketing (`just now` / `Nm ago` / `Nh ago` / `Yesterday` / `Nd ago` / abbreviated date). Locked to Figma node `15:8` since #154 as a single-line "avatar + name + sleep-dot + timestamp" row: the headline `Row` is `name → 8.dp outline-coloured Box (only when isSleeping) → trailing time`, no workspace label, no supporting-content preview. The pre-#154 `lastMessage: Message?` parameter and the workspace label (#19) / leading-sleep-dot (#20) / supporting-message-preview (#17) decorations were all walked back against the locked spec. Optional `onLongClick: (() -> Unit)? = null` (#25) switches the modifier chain from `Modifier.clickable` to `Modifier.combinedClickable` so callers can attach long-press affordances without fanning a screen-specific concept into the shared primitive. Since #68 the `ListItem` carries a `leadingContent = { ConversationAvatar(conversation) }` slot. Shared between the channel list (#46/#68), discussions drilldown (#24), and archived discussions (#94). Since #270 the gesture modifier carries `.semantics(mergeDescendants = true) { contentDescription = …; role = Role.Button }` (mirroring `SeeAllDiscussionsRow`), collapsing avatar + name + dot + time into one focusable TalkBack node labelled `<name>, <time>` via `cd_conversation_row` — and `<name>, idle, <time>` via `cd_conversation_row_idle` when `isSleeping`, the spoken equivalent of the otherwise-silent sleeping dot (the AC #2 gap closed). The merge lives on the row itself, so all three consumers inherit it (the #270 spec's "single consumer" claim was wrong); `relativeTime` is hoisted to one `val` shared by the label and `trailingContent`. First instrumented tests for the row landed in #270 (`ConversationRowTest`, androidTest — merged label, idle announcement, 48dp touch target; compile-verified only, no device CI).
- [ConversationAvatar](features/conversation-avatar.md) — 40dp leading bubble for `Conversation`-backed rows (#68); deterministic hash-based palette selection via `Math.floorMod(name.hashCode(), 3)` across `primary`/`secondary`/`tertiary` `*-container` pairs, 2-letter lowercase initials from `deriveInitials(name, fallback = conversation.id)` (first letter of first two words after splitting on `[^\p{L}\p{N}]+`; single-word and null/blank fallbacks pad with `'?'`). `paletteIndexFor` and `deriveInitials` are `internal` non-`@Composable` helpers so `./gradlew test` covers them as plain JUnit. Decorative — no `contentDescription`.
- [DiscussionPreviewRow](features/discussion-preview-row.md) — stateless preview-row primitive for the inline Recent-discussions section (#69); `Column` of titleMedium title (auto-fallback `R.string.untitled_discussion`), 2-line bodyMedium body rendering `lastMessage.content` when non-null and omitted entirely when null (since #162 — the `Arrangement.spacedBy(2.dp)` collapses naturally because Compose only emits the spacer between actually-rendered slots, so title sits directly above meta with a single 2 dp gap when the body is absent), and a single-line `labelSmall` meta `Text` rendering the bare `formatRelativeTime(conversation.lastUsedAt)` on `colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant @ 0.70f alpha`. Signature gained `lastMessage: Message?` (no default — private-package composable with one production caller, named-arg call sites stay clearer) immediately after `conversation` in #162; the body source flipped from `stringResource(R.string.discussion_preview_placeholder_body)` to `lastMessage.content` and the placeholder string resource was deleted. The data path arrived in #161 (`ChannelListUiState.recentDiscussionLastMessages: Map<String, Message>` with absent-key = no-message semantics, `mapNotNull`-filtered upstream so the map only holds non-null entries) and the consumer slice landed in #162 — `RecentDiscussionsSection` reads `lastMessages[conversation.id]` per row. Locked to Figma node `15:8` since #158 — the pre-#158 `<workspace> · <relative-time>` shape (`AnnotatedString` + `SpanStyle(fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace)` on the workspace fragment, `"scratch"` fallback for `DefaultScratchCwd`) was walked back along with the `discussionPreviewWorkspaceLabel` helper and the `R.string.discussion_preview_workspace_scratch` resource. Sibling-walked-back precedent: `ConversationRow` lost its workspace label in #154 (same "match Figma exactly" justification). Three `@Preview`s reshaped in #162: light + populated body (Figma 15:8 verbatim copy), dark + populated body, light + null lastMessage (the pre-#162 `NamedCwdPreview` renamed to `NoMessageLightPreview` and switched to `cwd = DEFAULT_SCRATCH_CWD` + `lastMessage = null` since the "named cwd" framing is obsolete post-#158); no dark + null permutation because color slots don't change between null and non-null states. Sole call site is `RecentDiscussionsSection` inside `ChannelListScreen`; shares the `formatRelativeTime` helper at `ui/conversations/components/RelativeTime.kt` (`internal`, same package). One spotless gotcha caught in a follow-up commit (#162's `21a9617`): the `Role as MessageRole` alias import — used inside previews because the `data.model.Role` enum clashes with `androidx.compose.ui.semantics.Role` used by `clickable(role = Role.Button, …)` — must sort *after* all non-aliased imports, not alphabetically into the middle of the block, or ktlint's import-ordering rule fails CI. Since #270 the `Column` also carries `.semantics(mergeDescendants = true) { contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_discussion_preview_row, name, time) }` between `.clickable` and `.padding`, exposing the row as one focusable TalkBack node labelled `<name>, <time>` (the message preview is intentionally excluded from the *announced* label per AC #3 but stays visible and merged into the node's `text`); `relativeTime` is hoisted to one `val` shared by the label and the meta `Text`. No idle state on discussion rows (no sleeping dot to mirror). First instrumented tests landed in #270 (`DiscussionPreviewRowTest`, androidTest — merged label for with/without-message, 48dp touch target; compile-verified only).
- [ChannelListViewModel](features/channel-list-viewmodel.md) — first project ViewModel; since #69 `combine`s `observeConversations(Channels)` + `observeConversations(Discussions)` into a hot `StateFlow<ChannelListUiState>` via `stateIn(WhileSubscribed(5_000), initial = Loading)` with sealed `Loading` / `Empty(recentDiscussions, recentDiscussionsCount, recentDiscussionLastMessages)` / `Loaded(channels, recentDiscussions, recentDiscussionsCount, recentDiscussionLastMessages)` / `Error(message)` variants and a `.catch { }` → `Error` terminal branch (throws on any upstream collapse — three since #161). `recentDiscussions = discussions.take(3)` and `recentDiscussionsCount = discussions.size` derive from one upstream emission inside the `combine` body — collapsed the prior `.map { it.size }` projection. Since #161 a third combined input `recentDiscussionLastMessages: Map<String, Message> = emptyMap()` carries the per-row last-message keyed by conversation id; sourced via `lastMessagesFlow = recentIdsFlow.flatMapLatest { ids -> if (ids.isEmpty()) flowOf(emptyMap()) else combine(ids.map { observeLastMessage(it).map(it to _) }) { mapNotNull-then-toMap } }` over `recentIdsFlow = discussionsFlow.map { it.take(3).map(::id) }.distinctUntilChanged()`; `distinctUntilChanged` prevents re-subscription churn on stable id sets, `flatMapLatest` cancels obsolete per-row subscriptions when the slice shifts, `mapNotNull` keeps map values non-nullable so absent-key = null-last-message by construction. Default `= emptyMap()` on both data-class fields kept the 14 existing construction sites (previews + screen/VM tests) untouched. First `onEvent` reducer landed in #22 — handles `CreateDiscussionTapped` by calling `repository.createDiscussion()` and emitting a one-shot `ChannelListNavigation.ToThread(id)` via a `Channel<…>(BUFFERED)` exposed as `navigationEvents`; project-wide pattern for suspend-shaped side effects whose nav target depends on the suspend's return value. `RowTapped`, `SettingsTapped`, and `RecentDiscussionsTapped` (#26) sit on a shared `Unit` arm — pure-navigation events routed at the destination. Since #221 a fourth combined input — a private `MutableStateFlow<Boolean>` (`pendingWorkspacePicker`) — projects onto a `workspacePickerVisible: Boolean = false` field on `Loaded`/`Empty`; combine arity widened from 3 to 4 (kotlinx-coroutines ships `combine` overloads up to 5 args, no infrastructure change). Same shape as #78's `pendingPromotion` (and the `SaveAsChannelDialog` arm from #142) — bare `Boolean` rather than a nullable-payload wrapper because the picker has no per-target payload (no "which discussion are we promoting" equivalent). `Error` and `Loading` do not carry the field — FAB renders only in `Loaded`/`Empty`, so the picker cannot be reached from those states; the VM's internal `pendingWorkspacePicker.value` survives across an `Error` transition (unobservable while there, re-applied when upstream recovers — acceptable Phase 0 behaviour, fix with a `LaunchedEffect(state is Error)` if auto-reappearance ever reads as surprising). `onEvent` widened in #221 from one VM-consumed variant to four: `LongPressFab` flips the flag to `true` (one-liner), `is WorkspacePicked(workspace)` clears the flag **synchronously before** `viewModelScope.launch { repository.createDiscussion(workspace = event.workspace); navigationChannel.send(ToThread(c.id)) }` (same shape as #22's `CreateDiscussionTapped` but with the picked path; sheet's exit animation starts immediately instead of waiting for the suspend — same discipline as #78's `confirmPromotion`), `WorkspacePickerDismissed` flips the flag to `false` (one-liner — fires when the user dismisses the sheet via close icon, scrim tap, drag-down, back-press; the host's `onDismiss` callback contract is documented in [#220](codebase/220.md)'s "AC #2g"). The three-variant event split (open / confirm-with-payload / cancel) is the canonical shape for transient-modal events — the "dismissed without picking" path is distinct from "picked a path" and must NOT trigger the side effect, mirrors #78's three-variant split. Two new unit tests in #221: `longPressFab_setsWorkspacePickerVisibleToTrue` (`stubRepo`-shaped because the flag-flip does no repository work) and `workspacePicked_createsDiscussionWithPickedWorkspace_emitsNavigation_andClearsVisibility` (`FakeConversationRepository()` because `createDiscussion` must actually run; snapshots `observeConversations(Discussions).first()` before, pre-arranges `LongPressFab` so the clear-visibility assertion is meaningful, then `async { vm.navigationEvents.first() }` *before* `WorkspacePicked("pyry-workspace/my-folder")`; asserts the new discussion's `cwd == "pyry-workspace/my-folder"`, the captured event is `ToThread(created.id)`, and `state.value.workspacePickerVisible == false` after the launch completes — same test-pattern split as #22, state-derivation via stub, side-effect via real fake). Existing `createDiscussionTapped_*` tests cover the AC's regression requirement verbatim; not duplicated. Since #240 the VM's constructor takes a second parameter `appPreferences: AppPreferences` (Koin: `viewModel { ChannelListViewModel(get(), get()) }`) and `CreateDiscussionTapped` reads `appPreferences.defaultWorkspace.first()` inside the existing `viewModelScope.launch { … }` before calling `repository.createDiscussion(workspace = <that value>)` — first consumer of the #231 `defaultWorkspace` schema. The `WorkspacePicked` long-press path is unchanged — explicit user pick still overrides the persisted default, bypassing the `.first()` read entirely. Incidental side effect: an unconfigured user's `cwd` goes from `""` (pre-#240, fake's `workspace ?: ""` on the no-arg call) to `DEFAULT_SCRATCH_CWD` post-#240; both forms hit `FakeConversationRepository.bumpWorkspace`'s `cwd.isEmpty() || cwd == DEFAULT_SCRATCH_CWD` early-return, so no recent-workspaces pollution. Two new tests in #240 (`shortPressFab_usesPersistedDefaultWorkspace` and `longPressPicker_overridesDefaultWorkspace`) drive `FakeConversationRepository` end-to-end with a `prefs.setDefaultWorkspace(...) + advanceUntilIdle()` seed and assert `created.cwd == "~/projects/my-thing"` / `created.cwd == "~/projects/user-pick"` respectively. The `makeVm` test helper from #239 spent its `@Suppress("UNUSED_PARAMETER")` seam — the helper body now threads `prefs` into `ChannelListViewModel(repository, prefs)` and the 18 pre-#240 call sites stay untouched (their default `prefs` produces `DEFAULT_SCRATCH_CWD` which keeps the assertions invisible).
- [SettingsViewModel](features/settings-viewmodel.md) — thin VM at the `settings` route (#87, extended #89/#164/#233); three parallel hot preference projections — `themeMode: StateFlow<ThemeMode>` via `appPreferences.themeMode.stateIn(viewModelScope, WhileSubscribed(5_000L), ThemeMode.SYSTEM)`, (since #89) `useWallpaperColors: StateFlow<Boolean>` via `appPreferences.useWallpaperColors.stateIn(viewModelScope, WhileSubscribed(5_000L), false)`, and (since #233) `defaultEffort: StateFlow<Effort>` via `appPreferences.defaultEffort.stateIn(viewModelScope, WhileSubscribed(5_000L), Effort.HIGH)` (initial value matches the upstream's tolerant-unknown fallback from #231), each paired with a fire-and-forget setter forward (`fun onSelectTheme(mode)` / `fun onToggleUseWallpaperColors(enabled)` / `fun onSelectDefaultEffort(effort)`) — plus a read-only derived projection `archivedDiscussionCount: StateFlow<Int>` (#164) via `conversationRepository.observeConversations(ConversationFilter.Archived).map { it.count { !it.isPromoted } }.catch { emit(0) }.stateIn(viewModelScope, WhileSubscribed(5_000L), 0)` that mirrors `ArchivedDiscussionsViewModel`'s filter exactly so the Settings Storage row's `"N archived"` line and the destination screen's list cannot disagree. The `.catch { emit(0) }` is a deliberate divergence from `ArchivedDiscussionsViewModel.state` (which surfaces `Error(message)`) and establishes the rule **supportive-metadata projections swallow upstream errors; primary-content projections surface them**. `STOP_TIMEOUT_MILLIS = 5_000L` is shared across all four flows via a single `private companion object` constant. **The "lift to sealed `SettingsState` on the third real persisted preference" threshold has now been crossed** — #87 set the rule, and #233's `defaultEffort` is the third writable preference (after `themeMode` and `useWallpaperColors`; #164's archived-count projection is read-only and does not count toward the threshold). #233 deferred the lift for one more slice because (a) the AC mandated a mechanical copy of the existing `(StateFlow<X>, fun onSelectX)` shape, (b) the sibling #232 (model picker) was in flight at architect time and lifting under #233 would force #232 to either rebase onto a new envelope or land its own mechanical-copy pair and rebase later, and (c) the lift, when it happens, should restructure all three at once. The next defaults consumer (YOLO switch row or workspace picker, whichever lands first after #232 + #233 settle) is the right re-evaluation point — see [`codebase/233.md`](codebase/233.md) § Lessons learned. Constructor `(private val appPreferences: AppPreferences, conversationRepository: ConversationRepository)` — the repo is a constructor parameter, not `private val`, because only the field-initializer for `archivedDiscussionCount` reads it; promote to a property when an instance method needs it. Koin registration `viewModel { SettingsViewModel(get(), get()) }` in `AppModule.kt`. Tested at `app/src/test/.../SettingsViewModelTest.kt` against a real `AppPreferences` over a `TemporaryFolder`-backed `PreferenceDataStoreFactory` plus a hand-rolled `ConversationRepository` stub driven by a `MutableSharedFlow<List<Conversation>>(replay = 0)` — 15 methods since #164 covering fresh-default emit, pre-write mirror on construction, per-`ThemeMode` round-trip, `themeMode` re-emission across two `onSelectTheme` calls, the three `useWallpaperColors` tests (fresh-default emits `false`, pre-write `true` mirrors on construction, `false → true → false` walk asserts both persistence and flow re-emission), plus the six new `archivedDiscussionCount_*` tests (initial-zero, three-archived-unpromoted, excludes-promoted-archived via `[archivedDiscussion("disc-1"), archivedChannel("chan-1"), archivedChannel("chan-2")] → count == 1`, archive transition, unarchive transition, plus a filter-capture test verifying the VM passes `ConversationFilter.Archived` to `observeConversations`). Hand-rolled stub chosen over the real `FakeConversationRepository` precisely because the filter-capture assertion needs a `captureFiltersInto: MutableList<ConversationFilter>` knob. Pre-#164 nine tests stay one-arg via a `makeVm(prefs, repo = stubRepo()) = SettingsViewModel(prefs, repo)` helper. Tests that read `.value` `launch` a no-op collector + `advanceUntilIdle()` first to keep the `WhileSubscribed(5_000L)` upstream subscribed. Sibling root collectors `MainActivity.setContent { … appPreferences.themeMode.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(...) ; appPreferences.useWallpaperColors.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(...) }` (#86 + #88) drive `PyrycodeMobileTheme(darkTheme = …, dynamicColor = …)` from the same upstream flows, so a single `setThemeMode` / `setUseWallpaperColors` write fans out to both the global theme and the matching Settings row's reflected state; `archivedDiscussionCount` has no composition-root sibling — it's a Settings-screen-local read.
- [DiscussionListViewModel](features/discussion-list-viewmodel.md) — sibling of `ChannelListViewModel` for the unpromoted tier (#24); since #78 a `combine(observeConversations(Discussions), pendingPromotion: MutableStateFlow<PendingPromotion?>)` projection into the sealed `Loading` / `Empty` / `Loaded(discussions, pendingPromotion)` / `Error` UiState, behind the same `stateIn(WhileSubscribed(5_000), initial = Loading)` lifecycle and the same `Channel<…>(BUFFERED) → receiveAsFlow()` one-shot nav seam. Consumed events: `RowTapped` (sends `ToThread(id)` — the VM-side emit exists so the unit test can assert the nav target), `SaveAsChannelRequested(id)` (#25 — since #78 sets `pendingPromotion` via `openPromotionDialog`, which guards against unknown ids by reading `state.value`'s `Loaded.discussions`), `PromoteConfirmed` (#78 — clears `pendingPromotion.value` *before* `viewModelScope.launch { repository.promote(id, derivedChannelName(sourceName), workspace = null) }` so a double-tap finds a `null` snapshot and short-circuits), `PromoteCancelled` (#78 — single-line `pendingPromotion.value = null`; also fired by scrim taps and back-press via M3 `AlertDialog`'s default `onDismissRequest`), and `BackTapped` (`Unit`; back handled at the composable side via `popBackStack()`). Two file-scope `internal` helpers — `derivedChannelName(sourceName) = sourceName?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "Untitled channel"` (persisted as `Conversation.name` post-promote) and `displayLabel(sourceName) = sourceName?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "Untitled discussion"` (dialog body identifier; matches `ConversationRow.displayName`). The combine body inline-clears `pendingPromotion` when the upstream collapses to `emptyList()` so a later `Loaded` emission cannot revive a stale dialog.
- [DiscussionListScreen](features/discussion-list-screen.md) — drilldown for the unpromoted tier (#24), mounted at the `discussions` route; same `Scaffold + LazyColumn + when (state)` skeleton as `ChannelListScreen` but with a back-arrow `navigationIcon` (`Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowBack`, `R.string.cd_back`), no FAB, and rows wrapped with `Modifier.alpha(0.65f)` at the call site to signal the secondary tier. Each row in `Loaded` is the private `DiscussionRow(...)` composable (#25) that pairs `SwipeToDismissBox` (right-to-left swipe → "Save as channel…") and a `DropdownMenu` anchored to an inner `Box` (long-press → same action); both gestures emit `DiscussionListEvent.SaveAsChannelRequested(id)`, which since #78 opens a minimal M3 `AlertDialog` overlay (`PromotionConfirmationDialog`, rendered outside the `Scaffold` body via a `(state as? Loaded)?.pendingPromotion` safe-cast). Confirm wires `onConfirm = { onEvent(PromoteConfirmed) }`; `onDismiss = { onEvent(PromoteCancelled) }` is passed to `dismissButton` *and* `onDismissRequest`, so scrim taps and back-press also emit `PromoteCancelled` by construction. Four new strings (`promote_dialog_{title,body,confirm,cancel}`); the body interpolates `displayLabel(pending.sourceName)` via a `%1$s` placeholder so unnamed discussions read as `Save "Untitled discussion" as a channel?`. Three `@Preview`s (#24/#25 — full Loaded state, long-press menu open via `menuInitiallyExpanded = true`, side-by-side channel-row-vs-discussion-row) plus a new instrumented `DiscussionListScreenTest.kt` since #78 (project's first for this screen) — six methods covering dialog appears/does-not-appear, Confirm/Cancel button → event emission, and body string interpolation with `sourceName` present and null; drives the screen via `state` + `events::add` directly (no VM, no Koin), mirroring `ChannelListScreenTest`'s conventions. Route reached from the channel-list inline "See all discussions (N) →" link (#69).
- [Settings screen](features/settings-screen.md) — sectioned stub at the `settings` route, locked to Figma node `17:2` (#64); since #271 the `public` signature is `SettingsScreen(themeMode, useWallpaperColors, archivedDiscussionCount, defaultModel, defaultEffort, defaultYolo, onSelectTheme, onToggleUseWallpaperColors, onSelectDefaultModel, onSelectDefaultEffort, onToggleDefaultYolo, onBack, onOpenArchivedDiscussions, onOpenAbout, modifier)` — parameter ordering groups state values first (#164 inserted `archivedDiscussionCount: Int`, #232 `defaultModel: Model`, #233 `defaultEffort: Effort`, #234 `defaultYolo: Boolean` into that group), then event callbacks (their writable counterparts `onSelectDefaultModel` / `onSelectDefaultEffort` / `onToggleDefaultYolo` join here), then nav callbacks (`onBack`, `onOpenArchivedDiscussions`, and `onOpenAbout` since #271), then `modifier`; all fourteen leading parameters are required (the prior `themeMode = ThemeMode.SYSTEM` default from #86 was dropped once the row became interactive in #87; the `useWallpaperColors` pair from #89 follows the same rule; `archivedDiscussionCount` from #164 has no default — every call site must wire the real count so a forgotten preview can't render a stale "0 archived" while the live value is non-zero; `onOpenAbout` from #271 carries no default per the nav-callback rule; the `onOpenLicense` parameter from #91 was dropped in #163 once the License row became text-only — #271's `onOpenAbout` is its structural successor). M3 `TopAppBar` ("Settings" + back-arrow `IconButton`) over a `Column(verticalScroll)` body of seven `SettingsSectionHeader` (`labelLarge` / `colorScheme.primary`, padding `16/16/16/4`) + `SettingsRow` (M3 `ListItem` with explicit `containerColor = colorScheme.surface` to defeat the M3 4.x `surfaceContainer` default; `internal` since #271 so the same-package [`AboutScreen`](features/about-screen.md) reuses it) groupings, ~15 rows in total since #271 dropped the four inline About rows. Four trailing-affordance shapes — 20dp `KeyboardArrowRight`, M3 `Switch`, borderless `TextButton` "Add" pill with 16dp leading `+`, or none (the 18dp `ic_open_in_new` per-file vector left Settings with the About rows in #271 — now in [`AboutScreen`](features/about-screen.md); `material-icons-extended` is still banned project-wide). **No** `remember { mutableStateOf(...) }` placeholder switches remain inside the `Scaffold` content lambda as of #268 — the last one (Push notifications) became VM-backed; Default YOLO had gone VM-backed in #234 and `materialYou` was deleted in #89. What's left in the body is dialog open-state only: `showThemeDialog` (#87), `showModelDialog` (#232), `showEffortDialog` (#233), plus a `dynamicColorSupported = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S` constant (#89) — none `rememberSaveable`. String ids in `strings.xml`: `settings_title`, `cd_add_memory_plugin`, `settings_theme_dialog_title` since #87, `settings_model_dialog_title` since #232, `settings_effort_dialog_title` since #233, `settings_use_wallpaper_colors` + `settings_use_wallpaper_colors_unsupported` since #89, `archived_discussions_count_supporting` = `"%1$d archived"` since #164 (the live Storage row supporting line), `about_settings_row` = "About" since #271 (the navigable About entry headline — sibling `about_title` lives with [`AboutScreen`](features/about-screen.md)), plus the pre-existing `cd_back`; the `license_title` id from #91 was deleted in #163. Remaining row headlines / subtitles stay hardcoded inline as Phase-3-replaceable scaffolding placeholders. The About section is a single navigable entry since #271 — `SettingsRow(headline = R.string.about_settings_row, trailing = { ChevronIcon() }, onClick = onOpenAbout)` under a kept `SettingsSectionHeader("About")` — that opens the dedicated [About screen](features/about-screen.md). The four former inline rows (Version + `BuildConfig.GIT_SHA` build SHA from #90/#165 with its `GitShaValueSource` Gradle wiring, Open-source row launching the platform browser via `Intent.ACTION_VIEW` from #90, inert Privacy policy, text-only "License: MIT" from #163) were extracted verbatim into `AboutScreen`, along with the `LocalContext` capture, the `ExternalLinkIcon` composable, and the `SOURCE_REPO_URL` const — see [About screen](features/about-screen.md) for the moved content and its #90/#163/#165 history. Appearance → Theme row subtitle reads `"System"` / `"Light"` / `"Dark"` via the `internal ThemeMode.label()` extension (SYSTEM arm shortened from `"System default"` in #163 to match Figma); taps open the M3 single-choice [`ThemePickerDialog`](features/settings-screen.md#themepickerdialog-sibling-file) (#87, `internal` composable in sibling `ThemePickerDialog.kt`) — confirm calls `onSelectTheme(pending)` → [`SettingsViewModel.onSelectTheme`](features/settings-viewmodel.md) → `AppPreferences.setThemeMode`, repainting both the row subtitle and the root-level `PyrycodeMobileTheme` from one write. Appearance → "Use Material You dynamic color" switch row (since #89; headline updated #163 from the prior "Use wallpaper colors" to match Figma) is a VM-backed `Switch` whose `checked` mirrors `AppPreferences.useWallpaperColors` and whose `onCheckedChange` routes through `SettingsViewModel.onToggleUseWallpaperColors` → `AppPreferences.setUseWallpaperColors`; SDK-gated `enabled = dynamicColorSupported` with `supporting` text "Requires Android 12 or newer" on the disabled-pre-S branch (structurally unreachable today since Min SDK 33 > S, but per AC), no row-level `onClick`. Defaults → "Default effort" row subtitle reads lowercase `"low"` / `"medium"` / `"high"` / `"xhigh"` / `"max"` via the `internal Effort.label()` extension at file scope in the sibling `EffortPickerDialog.kt` (#233 — diverges from `ThemeMode.label()`'s placement in `SettingsScreen.kt`; both are same-package `internal` so the call-site shape is identical, the file housing the extension is a cost-of-file-growth tradeoff); taps open the M3 single-choice `EffortPickerDialog` (five `Effort.entries` rows, mechanical copy of `ThemePickerDialog`'s shape with OK / Cancel `TextButton`s using `android.R.string.{ok,cancel}` and the same `var pending by remember(selected) { mutableStateOf(selected) }` pending pattern) — confirm calls `onSelectDefaultEffort(pending)` → [`SettingsViewModel.onSelectDefaultEffort`](features/settings-viewmodel.md) → `AppPreferences.setDefaultEffort`, repainting the row subtitle from one write. The other Defaults rows are all wired now — `Default model` opens the `ModelPickerDialog` (#232), `Default YOLO` is a VM-backed `Switch` (#234), `Default workspace` opens the reused `WorkspacePicker` host (#235). #268 then added the **Notifications** section's "Push notifications when claude responds" VM-backed `Switch` (`checked = pushNotifications` ← `AppPreferences.notificationsEnabled`, `onCheckedChange` → `SettingsViewModel.onTogglePushNotifications`; **defaults ON** — the only `?: true` preference, Figma-sourced from node `17:2`; no supporting text) and removed the screen's last placeholder-switch local. **The signature listing above predates #235/#268** — the current 21-param form (adding `pushNotifications` / `defaultWorkspace` / `workspacePickerVisible` to the state group and `onTogglePushNotifications` / `onDefaultWorkspaceTapped` / `onSelectDefaultWorkspace` / `onWorkspacePickerDismissed` to the callbacks) is documented in [Settings screen](features/settings-screen.md); this one-liner is overdue a full #232→#268 reconciliation. Storage → "Archived discussions" row carries a live `"N archived"` supporting line since #164 — `supporting = stringResource(R.string.archived_discussions_count_supporting, archivedDiscussionCount)` where `archivedDiscussionCount: Int` comes from [`SettingsViewModel.archivedDiscussionCount`](features/settings-viewmodel.md) (a `Flow<List<Conversation>>` from `ConversationRepository.observeConversations(ConversationFilter.Archived)` mapped to `count { !it.isPromoted }`, mirroring `ArchivedDiscussionsViewModel`'s filter so the row's count and the destination screen's list cannot disagree); `0` renders as `"0 archived"` per Figma 17:2 empty-state. Every other non-switch row tap is still a no-op `onClick = {}` (ripple-only). Replaces the Phase 1 `SettingsPlaceholder` private composable in `MainActivity.kt`. First `createComposeRule()` instrumented test in the project lives at `app/src/androidTest/.../SettingsScreenTest.kt` since #90; #271 cut it from six methods to three by moving the four About-content tests (`versionRow_rendersBuildConfigVersionName`, `versionRow_rendersSupportingTextWithGitSha` from #165, `openSourceRow_hasClickAction`, `licenseRow_hasNoClickAction` from #163) into the new `AboutScreenTest` with the rows they cover. What remains: `wallpaperColorsRow_rendersMaterialYouLabel` (#163), `archivedDiscussionsRow_rendersSupportingTextWithCount` (#164 — sets `archivedDiscussionCount = 11`, scrolls, asserts `hasText("11 archived", substring = true)`), and the new `aboutRow_navigatesOnClick` (#271 — recording `onOpenAbout`, matcher `hasText("About") and hasClickAction()` to disambiguate the clickable row from the non-clickable `SettingsSectionHeader("About")`, then `performScrollTo()` → `performClick()` → assert fired once); all three `performScrollTo()` before asserting. The three surviving `setContent` blocks pass `themeMode` + `onSelectTheme` since #87, `useWallpaperColors` + `onToggleUseWallpaperColors` since #89 (the latter patched during the docs pass after the developer commit shipped a broken `compileDebugAndroidTestKotlin` — see [`codebase/89.md`](codebase/89.md) § Lessons learned), `archivedDiscussionCount` since #164, `defaultEffort` + `onSelectDefaultEffort` since #233, `defaultModel` + `onSelectDefaultModel` + `defaultYolo` + `onToggleDefaultYolo` since #234 (which bundled the params #232 left unthreaded), and `onOpenAbout` since #271; #163 dropped the `onOpenLicense = {}` argument. Pure-Kotlin `SettingsLabelsTest.kt` (`app/src/test/...`, #163) pins each `ThemeMode.label()` return value.
- [Archived Discussions screen](features/archived-discussions-screen.md) — recovery surface for archived conversations at the `archived_discussions` route (#94); since #176 the screen is locked to Figma `18:2` as a 2-tab segmented archive (Channels / Discussions) with live count badges; since #177 the body renders the new public [`ArchiveRow`](features/archived-discussions-screen.md#archiverow-since-177) (40dp [`ConversationAvatar`](features/conversation-avatar.md) + `titleMedium` headline + `bodySmall` "Archived <relative>" subtitle on `onSurfaceVariant @ alpha 0.75f` + trailing 40dp `IconButton` carrying a 22dp `Icons.Filled.Refresh` glyph; no row-level alpha dimming, no long-press menu — `ArchiveRow.kt` lives in `ui/conversations/components/` and is `public` because both archive tabs consume it; chose `Refresh` over the spec's `Restore`/`Replay`/`History`/`Undo` because all four ship only in the project-banned `material-icons-extended`, see `codebase/177.md`). Stateless `ArchivedDiscussionsScreen(state, onEvent, modifier, effects: Flow<ArchivedDiscussionsEffect> = emptyFlow())` over a `Scaffold` + back-arrow `TopAppBar` (`archived_title` = "Archived" since #176 — replaces the pre-#176 `archived_discussions_title` = "Archived discussions" / `cd_back` reused) + `snackbarHost = { SnackbarHost(snackbarHostState) }` (since #177 — first `SnackbarHost` in the codebase) with `Loading` / `Error` / `Loaded` branches; `Loaded` renders the file-private `LoadedBody` = `Column { SecondaryTabRow + (LazyColumn | CenteredText) }`. Snackbar wiring: `val snackbarHostState = remember { SnackbarHostState() }` + `val resources = LocalResources.current` + `LaunchedEffect(effects, snackbarHostState) { effects.collect { is RestoreSucceeded -> snackbarHostState.showSnackbar(resources.getString(R.string.restored_snackbar, effect.displayName)) } }` (since #177; `LocalResources.current` chosen over the spec's `LocalContext.current` — one accessor shorter, functionally equivalent for `getString`). The tab row uses `selectedTabIndex = state.selectedTab.ordinal` against the top-level `enum class ArchiveTab { Channels, Discussions }`; each `Tab`'s label reads `stringResource(R.string.archived_tab_<variant>, state.<list>.size)` (`"Channels (%1$d)"` / `"Discussions (%1$d)"`) so counts stay live without re-collection on tab switch; `SecondaryTabRow`'s default 2dp `colorScheme.primary` underline matches Figma so the architect's drafted explicit `SecondaryIndicator(...)` override was dropped; `PrimaryTabRow` was rejected because its rounded-pill indicator doesn't match the flat underline spec. Per-tab empty body (`archived_empty_channels` / `archived_empty_discussions` — both new in #176) renders **below** the tab header so the header + counts stay visible (AC §5). Each row's `displayName` argument and the `RestoreRequested(id, displayName)` payload both read from a file-private `Conversation.displayName(): String` extension that mirrors `ConversationRow`'s inline fallback (`name?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: if (isPromoted) "Untitled channel" else "Untitled discussion"`); deliberately duplicated per the #177 spec ("do not refactor `ConversationRow`"). Backed by `ArchivedDiscussionsViewModel` (`ui/settings/`, constructor signature unchanged from #94): sealed `Loading` / `Loaded(channels, discussions, selectedTab)` / `Error(message)` UiState (the pre-#176 top-level `Empty` is gone — per-tab emptiness is `Loaded.<list>.isEmpty()` because the chrome needs both counts at all times); built from `combine(repository.observeConversations(ConversationFilter.Archived), selectedTab: MutableStateFlow<ArchiveTab> = MutableStateFlow(ArchiveTab.Discussions)) { conversations, tab -> Loaded(channels = conversations.filter { it.isPromoted }, discussions = conversations.filter { !it.isPromoted }, selectedTab = tab) as ArchivedDiscussionsUiState }.catch { Error(...) }.stateIn(viewModelScope, WhileSubscribed(5_000L), Loading)` — the `as ArchivedDiscussionsUiState` widening cast is **required** (single-arm lambda types as `Loaded`; without the cast the chained `.catch { emit(Error(...)) }` doesn't typecheck — see `codebase/176.md`'s Lessons learned). Default tab is Discussions per AC §3 (preserves the pre-#176 landing surface; Figma 18:2 illustrates Channels-active for layout reference only); the `!isPromoted` post-filter that #94 applied client-side is gone — #176 keeps both partitions because the screen now needs them, and the repository's `Archived` filter from #93 was always `isPromoted`-agnostic on purpose. New `sealed interface ArchivedDiscussionsEffect { data class RestoreSucceeded(val displayName: String) : ArchivedDiscussionsEffect }` since #177 with a private `_effects = Channel<ArchivedDiscussionsEffect>(Channel.BUFFERED)` field exposed as `val effects: Flow<...> = _effects.receiveAsFlow()` — first non-nav effect channel in the codebase (sibling shape to the established `navigationEvents` channels in `ChannelListViewModel` / `DiscussionListViewModel`; keep both shapes separate per `codebase/177.md` § Patterns established). `RestoreRequested(conversationId, displayName)` gained a `displayName: String` field in #177 so the resolved fallback flows end-to-end from row → VM → snackbar without re-derivation; handled by `viewModelScope.launch { runCatching { repository.unarchive(event.conversationId) }.onSuccess { _effects.send(RestoreSucceeded(event.displayName)) } }` — the `runCatching` swap from the spec's bare-throw is deliberate so Phase 4's Ktor failures don't leak through `viewModelScope`'s uncaught handler; snackbar contract ("on success only") is preserved (`codebase/177.md` § Lessons learned). `TabSelected(tab)` is handled by `selectedTab.value = event.tab` (non-suspending — `MutableStateFlow.value` setter is not `suspend`, so no `viewModelScope.launch`); `BackTapped` is a `Unit` arm so the screen carries one uniform `onEvent` lambda, with `popBackStack()` owned by the NavHost destination block. No `navigationEvents` channel. `MainActivity.PyryNavHost` `Routes.ARCHIVED_DISCUSSIONS` block gained an `effects = vm.effects` argument in #177 at `MainActivity.kt:234`; the route key (`Routes.ARCHIVED_DISCUSSIONS = "archived_discussions"`), `koinViewModel<ArchivedDiscussionsViewModel>()`, `collectAsStateWithLifecycle()`, and the three-arm `onEvent` `when` are untouched. Koin binding stays one-arg (`viewModel { ArchivedDiscussionsViewModel(get()) }`). Strings post-#177: `archived_title` + `archived_tab_channels` + `archived_tab_discussions` + `archived_empty_channels` + `archived_empty_discussions` (#176) plus `archived_relative_subtitle` = `"Archived %1$s"` + `restored_snackbar` = `"Restored %1$s"` + `cd_restore_archive` = `"Restore %1$s"` (#177); the pre-#176 `archived_discussions_title` + `archived_discussions_empty` and the pre-#177 `restore_action` = `"Restore"` (sole consumer was the deleted `DropdownMenuItem`) all deleted; `archived_discussions_settings_row` (Settings copy kept the pre-#176 wording — deliberately separate string id so the two surfaces can diverge) and `archived_discussions_count_supporting` (Settings live count since #164) unchanged; `cd_back` reused. Tab selection lives in the VM (`MutableStateFlow<ArchiveTab>`), not in `rememberSaveable`, so unit tests can assert tab-selection deterministically — this also means selection resets to `Discussions` after the `WhileSubscribed(5_000L)` grace expires when leaving and re-entering the screen (acceptable in Phase 0; revisit when Phase 4's real backend introduces durable session state). Tested by 15-method `ArchivedDiscussionsViewModelTest` (#177 grew it from #176's 13): the existing `restoreRequested_callsUnarchive_withConversationId` constructor-call updated to pass the new `displayName`; new `restoreRequested_emitsRestoreSucceededEffect_afterUnarchive` (`async { vm.effects.first() }` started before the event dispatches so the collector is subscribed when `_effects.send` runs) + `restoreRequested_doesNotEmitEffect_whenUnarchiveThrows` (`withTimeoutOrNull(100.milliseconds) { vm.effects.first() }` against a new `throwingUnarchiveRepo` helper — also the regression guard for the `runCatching` swap, would fail if a future refactor reverted to bare-throw); helper set grew from 5 to 6 with `throwingUnarchiveRepo`. Five `@Preview`s post-#177 (`ArchivedDiscussionsScreen.kt`: Discussions-tab + Channels-tab + Discussions-empty; `ArchiveRow.kt`: light + dark) — the pre-#177 `ArchivedDiscussionsRowMenuPreview` deleted with the dropdown it was rendering. Still no instrumented Compose test; the `Snackbar` text is one obvious follow-up candidate but verifying it requires `composeTestRule.waitUntil { … }` against the live `SnackbarHostState`, which has no prior art in this codebase. "Archive date" trailing slot still renders `formatRelativeTime(lastUsedAt)` — `Conversation` has no `archivedAt: Instant?` field yet; follow-up adds it when the 30-day auto-archive worker ticket lands. Settings-row count divergence note: since #164 the Settings entry reads `count { !it.isPromoted }` so it counts archived discussions only — archived channels are first-class in this screen since #176 but invisible on the Settings entry; deliberate, documented in `archived-discussions-screen.md`'s Edge cases. `ArchivedDiscussionsScreen` keeps its name and signature — the #176 spec offered an `ArchiveScreen` rename as optional and the developer chose keep-name (would have churned `MainActivity`, the Koin binding, and three test files for zero behavior gain); the surface itself now covers both tiers but the type name and the package (`ui/settings/`) are historical artefacts of the #94 origin. **[#557](codebase/557.md)** closed the mirror gap on the restore side: `RestoreRequested`'s handler was rewritten from `runCatching { unarchive(id) }.onSuccess { send(RestoreSucceeded) }` (silent no-op on failure, an explicit deferral comment since #177) to an explicit try/catch — `CancellationException` rethrown first (JVM: it extends `IllegalStateException`), then `RelayErrorException` (server error, reachable because `unarchive` is request/reply, #549) and `IllegalStateException` (disconnected) both `send`ing a new payload-free `ArchivedDiscussionsEffect.RestoreFailed` — rendered by a new third `when` branch on the screen's existing `effects` collector as a fixed-string `restore_failed` snackbar (never the caught exception's message). No new channel, no `MainActivity` edit — `RestoreFailed` rides the same `_effects` `Channel` `RestoreSucceeded` already used since #177. Twin of #556 (archive-from-thread) but strictly smaller since the effect channel already existed. `unarchive` still sits behind the `mutationsSupported` family gate against the real relay — untouched by this ticket.
- [About screen](features/about-screen.md) — dedicated, navigable About screen at the `about` route (#271); a **static** sub-screen (no ViewModel, no repository, no backend — `BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME` / `GIT_SHA` are the only dynamic data, both compile-time `const`) under `ui/settings/`, reached from the Settings About-section "About" row via the new `onOpenAbout` callback. Promotes the four About rows that rendered inline at the bottom of [`SettingsScreen`](features/settings-screen.md) from #90–#271 (non-clickable `"Version <name>"` / `"build <sha>"` row; clickable `"Open source · github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile"` row → platform browser via `Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse(SOURCE_REPO_URL))`, unguarded per #90; clickable-but-inert `"Privacy policy"` row, `onClick = {}`; text-only `"License: MIT"` row, no viewer) into a verbatim extraction — no row text or click behavior changed. Public `@Composable fun AboutScreen(onBack: () -> Unit, modifier: Modifier = Modifier)`: `Scaffold(topBar = TopAppBar(title = stringResource(R.string.about_title), navigationIcon = back-arrow IconButton(Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowBack, contentDescription = R.string.cd_back)))` over a `Column(Modifier.padding(inner).fillMaxSize().verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()))` holding the four rows — a scrollable `Column` not `LazyColumn` (four fixed rows, matches how they rendered in Settings), and **no inner `SettingsSectionHeader`** (the TopAppBar title "About" replaces it). `val context = LocalContext.current` captured at the top of the body for the Open source `onClick`. Mirrors the locked [`ArchivedDiscussionsScreen`](features/archived-discussions-screen.md) sub-screen chrome but **drops the VM / `collectAsStateWithLifecycle` / `onEvent` / effects / snackbar** that a static screen doesn't need — the first sub-screen to demonstrate the no-state shape. Reuses `SettingsRow` (bumped `private` → `internal` in `SettingsScreen.kt` by #271 so this same-package screen single-sources the locked row treatment without redefining it); the `private ExternalLinkIcon()` composable (18dp `painterResource(R.drawable.ic_open_in_new)`, `material-icons-extended` banned) and `private const val SOURCE_REPO_URL = "https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile"` were **moved into `AboutScreen.kt`** from `SettingsScreen.kt` with the rows that use them. Mounted as the minimal `composable(Routes.ABOUT) { AboutScreen(onBack = { navController.popBackStack() }) }` (`Routes.ABOUT = "about"`) in [`PyryNavHost`](features/navigation.md); both TopAppBar back arrow and system back return to Settings via the default `popBackStack()` (no `BackHandler`). Two new strings: `about_title` = "About" (TopAppBar) and `about_settings_row` = "About" (the Settings entry headline — separate id so the two surfaces can diverge); the four content rows keep their hardcoded literals (verbatim extraction). Two `@Preview`s (light + dark, `widthDp = 412`), `AboutScreen(onBack = {})`. Tested by 5-method instrumented `AboutScreenTest` (`app/src/androidTest/.../ui/settings/AboutScreenTest.kt`): `versionRow_rendersBuildConfigVersionName`, `versionRow_rendersSupportingTextWithGitSha` (both substring-match the live `BuildConfig` constant so they stay correct on dev builds with a real SHA and CI builds where `GIT_SHA == "unknown"`), `openSourceRow_hasClickAction`, `licenseRow_hasNoClickAction` (all four ported verbatim out of `SettingsScreenTest`), plus the new `backArrow_invokesOnBack` (recording `onBack`, click `onNodeWithContentDescription("Back")`); each asserts after a `performScrollTo()`. Code review PASS with one non-blocking NIT — the body `Column` deliberately omits the `.padding(bottom = 32.dp)` that `SettingsScreen`'s scroll column carries (four fixed rows won't scroll; kept intentional). Privacy policy row stays inert (wiring it is unscheduled); no license viewer (the in-app `LicenseScreen` from #91 was deleted in #163 and not revived). `connectedAndroidTest` + manual verification (tap About → screen opens; Open source → browser; back-arrow + system back → Settings) were device-gated and pending at merge, consistent with #90/#93. Blocks #270 (a11y pass — covers this screen). No Figma frame of its own — content specced inside the Settings frame `17:2`, chrome follows the Archive frame `18:2`. See [`codebase/271.md`](codebase/271.md).
- [Warning color slot](features/warning-color.md) — `MaterialTheme.colorScheme.warning` extension (#119); Material 3's `ColorScheme` is `final`, so a `warning` semantic for non-error attention states (token-budget approaching limit, progress fills approaching threshold) is grafted on via the Compose extension pattern — `@Immutable data class WarningColors(val warning: Color)` (single field today; can grow `onWarning` / `warningContainer` / `onWarningContainer` when a 4-slot consumer needs them), `internal LocalWarningColors: ProvidableCompositionLocal<WarningColors>` created via `staticCompositionLocalOf { error("WarningColors not provided. Wrap content in PyrycodeMobileTheme.") }` (fail-fast on missing provider, never silent `Color.Unspecified`), and `val ColorScheme.warning: Color @Composable @ReadOnlyComposable get() = LocalWarningColors.current.warning` so call sites read `MaterialTheme.colorScheme.warning` — same surface as a real slot. Six per-mode `warning*` color vals in `Color.kt` (`warningLight` `#5C4C1E`, `warningLightMediumContrast` `#403309`, `warningLightHighContrast` `#251C00`, `warningDark` `#D8B85A`, `warningDarkMediumContrast` `#E5CB80`, `warningDarkHighContrast` `#F3DDA8`, sourced from Figma `Schemes/Warning` variable `VariableID:72:2` in `material-theme` collection, authored 2026-05-15) follow the existing `errorLight` / `errorDark` mode-suffix convention rather than the AC's `Warning80` / `WarningMC80` tone-number naming so they sort with their sibling `error*` blocks. Six `private val *WarningColors(...)` instances in `Theme.kt` parallel the six existing `*ColorScheme` instances; `PyrycodeMobileTheme` destructures `val (colorScheme, warningColors) = when { … }` and wraps `MaterialTheme(...)` in `CompositionLocalProvider(LocalWarningColors provides warningColors)` — the dynamic-color (wallpaper) branches fall back to `darkWarningColors` / `lightWarningColors` since the wallpaper-derived scheme carries no warning hint, keeping the resolved color non-null on every composition path. Medium/high-contrast `WarningColors` instances exist but aren't selected — parallels the existing un-routed M3 HC/MC `ColorScheme` instances and will get wired by whichever ticket eventually threads accessibility contrast through `PyrycodeMobileTheme`. Two-method instrumented test at `app/src/androidTest/.../theme/WarningColorSlotTest.kt` under `createComposeRule()` (not `createAndroidComposeRule<...>` — no activity binding needed) asserts `warningLight` / `warningDark` are reachable via a private `CaptureWarning { captured = it }` composable. Foundational only — no call site consumes the slot yet; the existing raw `#D8B85A` literals are migrated by downstream tickets #145 (Status Sheet) / #146 (Conversation Thread token-budget chip). Project's first non-Material custom color slot; the pattern (extension property + private `CompositionLocal` + provider inside `PyrycodeMobileTheme`) is the precedent for any future ones (`success`, `info`, etc.). The architect's spec drafted a 4-field `WarningColors` and 24 color vals (warning + on + container + onContainer × 6 modes); the developer shipped the minimal-AC shape (1 field, 6 vals, 1 extension property) — when AC and spec diverge on scope-shape, follow the AC and let the extension points carry the further growth.
- [Success color slot](features/success-color.md) — a green `success` ("up") semantic colour slot on `MaterialTheme.colorScheme` ([#397](codebase/397.md)), the **second** custom colour slot and the first realization of the `success`/`info` slots the [warning slot](features/warning-color.md) ([#119](codebase/119.md)) anticipated. New `ui/theme/SuccessColors.kt` mirrors `WarningColors` **one-for-one** — `@Immutable SuccessColors(val success: Color)` + an `internal LocalSuccessColors` via `staticCompositionLocalOf { error("…not provided…") }` (fail-fast, never a silent `Color.Unspecified`) + a `val ColorScheme.success @Composable @ReadOnlyComposable` extension so call sites read `MaterialTheme.colorScheme.success` — threaded through the same three `Theme.kt` sites and provided for light+dark; palette literals `successLight #316B2B` / `successDark #A6D388` beside the `warning*` ones in `Color.kt`. **A sibling holder, not a `WarningColors` field** (a holder named `WarningColors` owning a green value would be semantically muddled) — the rule for the next slot (`info`, …): own holder, never widen an existing one. Values are **design-owed placeholders** (no Figma `Schemes/Success`; conceived after the design lock, same resolution as #343) isolated in `Color.kt` (two-line change to reconcile); no medium/high-contrast variants (matches `WarningColors`' selected scope). Sole consumer = the [connection status line](features/connection-status-line.md)'s `Up → success` dot.
- [Thread screen](features/thread-screen.md) — outer shell at the `conversation_thread/{conversationId}` route (#126); TopAppBar promoted from placeholder to Figma-matched chrome in #139. `ThreadScreen(state, onBack, modifier, onTitleClick = {}, onOverflowClick = {})` over an `@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)` `Scaffold` whose `topBar` calls the new public stateless `ThreadTopAppBar(title, onBack, onTitleClick, onOverflowClick, modifier)` in its own file (`ui/conversations/thread/ThreadTopAppBar.kt`, #139) — back-arrow `IconButton` (`Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowBack`, reuses `R.string.cd_back`), `Text(title, Modifier.clickable(onClick = onTitleClick).semantics { role = Role.Button })` title (clickable on the `Text` not a wrapping `Row` so the ripple aligns with text bounds — a tradeoff #141 may revisit if rename moves to "tap anywhere in the bar"), trailing `IconButton(onOverflowClick) { Icon(Icons.Filled.MoreVert, R.string.cd_more_actions) }`; **`TopAppBar` defaults preserved** — no `colors = TopAppBarDefaults.topAppBarColors(...)` override (`colorScheme.surface` already matches Figma `Schemes/surface`), no `scrollBehavior`, no `windowInsets` override; new string `cd_more_actions = "More actions"` introduced in #139 (named generically so non-thread overflows can reuse). Body shape evolved across slices: #126 baked in `reverseLayout = true` with an empty `items(emptyList<Unit>()) { }` placeholder; #246 filled the slot with `items(state.items.asReversed(), key = { item -> when (item) { is ThreadItem.MessageItem -> "msg:${item.message.id}"; is ThreadItem.SessionBoundary -> "boundary:${item.previousSessionId}->${item.newSessionId}" } }) { item -> when (item) { is ThreadItem.MessageItem -> MessageBubble(message = item.message); is ThreadItem.SessionBoundary -> SessionBoundaryDelimiter(boundary = item) } }` — dispatching at the `ThreadItem` sealed-interface level only (no role re-dispatch at the screen; `MessageBubble` owns `Role.User`/`Role.Assistant`/`Role.Tool` selection internally) and reversing the chronologically-ascending source list via the Kotlin stdlib O(1) `asReversed()` view because `reverseLayout = true` draws index 0 at the bottom. Stable composite keys with `"msg:"` / `"boundary:"` namespace prefixes prevent collisions between message ids and session ids that share a string. Backed by `ThreadViewModel(savedStateHandle, repository: ConversationRepository)` post-#139 whose `state: StateFlow<ThreadUiState>` is now `repository.observeConversations(ConversationFilter.All).map { list -> ThreadUiState(conversationId, list.firstOrNull { it.id == conversationId }?.displayName() ?: conversationId) }.stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5_000), initialValue = ThreadUiState(conversationId, displayName = conversationId))` — the pre-#139 `MutableStateFlow(initial).asStateFlow()` synchronous shape upgraded in place to a `stateIn(WhileSubscribed)` cold-flow share (same lifetime as `ChannelListViewModel` / `DiscussionListViewModel`); the `initialValue` falls back to the raw id so the placeholder matches the steady-state fallback, and the fake's `MutableStateFlow.map` chain emits synchronously so the placeholder is invisible in practice. **`ThreadUiState` widened to `data class ThreadUiState(conversationId: String, displayName: String)`** — `displayName` always non-null (`firstOrNull { it.id == conversationId }?.displayName() ?: conversationId` three-layer fallback: real `name`, `"Untitled channel"` / `"Untitled discussion"` kind-aware fallback, then the raw id when the lookup misses); still a `data class`, not a sealed envelope — there's no `Loading` / `Error` distinction worth modelling (the lookup falls back to the id rather than failing). **`ThreadScreen` still takes `onBack: () -> Unit` directly, not `(state, onEvent)`** — all three lambdas (`onBack`, `onTitleClick`, `onOverflowClick`) are pure-navigation hooks owned by the destination, not VM-owned events; the signature widens to `(state, onBack, onEvent)` or the three callbacks fold into a then-introduced `ThreadEvent` when the first VM-owned event lands (#133's composer send is the most likely first). `onTitleClick` / `onOverflowClick` are `() -> Unit = {}` defaults at the screen signature (not hoisted lambdas at `MainActivity` — keeps the destination block at `MainActivity.kt:193-203` byte-identical to its #126 form; #141 wires `onTitleClick` to the rename dialog; [#252](codebase/252.md) later renamed `onOverflowClick` to `onOverflowEvent` in place, hoisted `var overflowExpanded by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }` on `ThreadScreen`, mounted the [`ThreadOverflowMenu`](features/thread-overflow-menu.md) inside `ThreadTopAppBar`'s `actions` slot, and bound `onOverflowEvent = vm::onOverflowEvent` at the destination). #139 picked **plumbing option (c)** of three (filter the existing `observeConversations(All)` in the VM) over (a) second nav arg `displayName` (URL-encoding scatter + no live-rename re-emission) and (b) new `observeConversation(id)` interface method (≥5 `.kt` files, busts S-budget) — option (c) hit 4 `.kt` files and **gets free rename re-emission**: when #141 calls `repository.rename(...)`, the fake updates its `MutableStateFlow<State>`, the upstream re-emits, the `.map { … }` recomputes `displayName`, and the title updates without #141 touching `ThreadViewModel`; the `state_displayName_reemitsOnRename` test pins this contract; the ticket-body Out-of-Scope "plumbing a live rename back into the title" is **already wired** post-#139. `ConversationFilter.All` (not `Channels` / `Discussions`) because the thread screen is reachable from the archive screen and future deep links; restricting would silently break those paths. The `firstOrNull` per-emission scan is `O(n)` over the conversation list — acceptable at Phase 0 fake-cardinality (~5 records); a follow-up introduces `observeConversation(id): Flow<Conversation?>` when the Phase 4 Ktor-backed impl makes the cost real. `private fun Conversation.displayName(): String = name?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: if (isPromoted) "Untitled channel" else "Untitled discussion"` is **re-declared at file scope** in `ThreadViewModel.kt` (duplicate of the same private extension in `ArchivedDiscussionsScreen.kt:164-166` since #176) — deliberately not extracted to a shared helper because promotion to `internal fun` in a new file would have pushed the spec to a 5-`.kt`-file diff (busting the S-budget) and two occurrences is below the canonical extract-on-third-use threshold; the third caller (likely #140's overflow menu's "Rename …" label) is the ticket that extracts. Fallback strings are Kotlin literals (not `stringResource(R.string.untitled_discussion)`) because ViewModels have no `Context` and the existing private extension has been shipping with literals since #176 — revisit when the app needs localization beyond English. Koin binding `viewModel { ThreadViewModel(get(), get()) }` (post-#139 — second `get()` resolves to the `FakeConversationRepository` singleton already bound at `AppModule.kt:28`). The `Routes.CONVERSATION_THREAD = "conversation_thread/{conversationId}"` constant, the `navArgument("conversationId") { type = NavType.StringType }` declaration, the four pre-existing call sites at `MainActivity.kt:142-143, 151-152, 169-170, 178-179`, and the destination block at `MainActivity.kt:193-203` are **all unchanged** post-#139 — subsequent `MainActivity` edits add VM-bound callbacks one at a time ([#252](codebase/252.md) added `onOverflowEvent = vm::onOverflowEvent`; #141 will add `onTitleClick = …` for the rename dialog). The pre-#126 `backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("conversationId").orEmpty()` inline parse remains gone since #126 (the `SavedStateHandle` route through Koin owns the extraction). No new dependencies, no `gradle/libs.versions.toml` edits, no Compose-Material classpath additions; `R.string.cd_back` reused for the back-arrow `contentDescription`, `R.string.cd_more_actions` added for the overflow. Two `@Preview`s seeded with `ThreadUiState(conversationId = "seed-channel-personal", displayName = "kitchenclaw refactor")` (light + dark — neither preview passes `onTitleClick` / `onOverflowClick`; the defaults apply). Tested by seven JUnit methods in `ThreadViewModelTest.kt` post-#139 (upgraded from three plain-JUnit tests to the `Dispatchers.setMain(UnconfinedTestDispatcher()) + resetMain()` / `runTest { launch collector; advanceUntilIdle(); … }` idiom from `ChannelListViewModelTest:1-60` because `stateIn(viewModelScope, …)` requires a `Main` test dispatcher): `state_initialValue_isConversationIdPlaceholderBeforeSubscription` (pins the `stateIn` initialValue contract — synchronous read returns the id-as-displayName placeholder without a launched collector), `state_resolvedTitle_isChannelNameForSeededChannel` (`seed-channel-personal` → `"Personal"`), `state_resolvedTitle_isUntitledDiscussionForUnnamedDiscussion` (`seed-discussion-a` → `"Untitled discussion"`), `state_resolvedTitle_isUntitledChannelForUnnamedChannel` (file-scope `fixedRepo(listOf(Conversation(name = null, isPromoted = true, …)))` stub → `"Untitled channel"`), `state_resolvedTitle_fallsBackToConversationIdWhenConversationMissing` (`fixedRepo(emptyList())` + `conversationId = "ghost-id"` → `"ghost-id"`), **`state_displayName_reemitsOnRename`** (pins the load-bearing free-re-emission contract #141 inherits), `state_collapsesAbsentConversationIdToEmptyString` (the `.orEmpty()` narrowing). The `fixedRepo(conversations)` helper is an anonymous `object : ConversationRepository { … }` with 10 `TODO("not used")` overrides + `flowOf(conversations)`-backed `observeConversations` — kept local; not extracted yet because each test's bespoke conversation shape would force a builder-shaped helper that doesn't pay for itself. No instrumented Compose test — codebase has no `androidTest` infrastructure for thread/list screens; AC5 smoke verification is by `./gradlew assembleDebug` + `./gradlew installDebug` + manual tap from channel-list / discussion-list / FAB-create-discussion paths (channel name renders, discussion fallback renders, back pops, title-tap and overflow-tap are no-op stubs with TalkBack announcing role + content description). Post-#201 the body slot is `Column { ConnectionBanner(connectionState, onRetry); LazyColumn(weight=1f, reverseLayout=true) { … } }` — banner sits between TopAppBar and message list, structurally (not overlay), pushing the list down under disconnected states and collapsing to zero height under steady-state `Connected`; `Modifier.padding(inner)` moved from `LazyColumn` to the outer `Column` so the inset wraps both children (banner-under-status-bar trap on edge-to-edge devices). VM grew one constructor arg `private val connectionStateSource: ConnectionStateSource` (third Koin `get()` at `AppModule.kt:36`, resolves to the `FakeConnectionStateSource` singleton bound by #196), one new `val connectionState: StateFlow<ConnectionState> = connectionStateSource.observe().stateIn(viewModelScope, WhileSubscribed(5_000), ConnectionState.Connected)` (same lifetime as `state`; separate flow rather than folded into `ThreadUiState` — avoids `combine(...)` ceremony and churn to the seven existing tests, and the destination block's two `collectAsStateWithLifecycle()` calls match the canonical `SettingsViewModel` four-flow shape at `MainActivity.kt:218-225`), and one new non-suspend `fun retry() { viewModelScope.launch { connectionStateSource.retry() } }` (same VM-owns-the-launch posture as #188's `sendMessage`; UI binds `vm::retry` directly to the `() -> Unit` slot). `ThreadScreen` signature widened to `(state, onBack, onSendMessage, connectionState, onRetry, modifier, onTitleClick = {}, onOverflowClick = {})` — still flat callbacks (no `ThreadEvent` fold), `connectionState` is *state* not event so it stays a flat param even when the fold eventually lands; `LazyColumn` now carries `Modifier.fillMaxWidth().weight(1f)` (not `.fillMaxSize()` — inside a `Column`, `fillMaxSize` ignores `weight` semantics and over-claims). Destination block at `MainActivity.kt:197-211` adds one `val connectionState by vm.connectionState.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()` + two named args (`connectionState = connectionState, onRetry = vm::retry`). No `try/catch` around `source.retry()` (Phase-2 fake can't throw; per `ConnectionStateSource` KDoc Phase-4 failures route through state transitions, not exceptions). Test file added a `private fun makeVm(handle, repository, source = FakeConnectionStateSource())` helper (same shape as `ChannelListViewModelTest.makeVm` from #239) — every pre-#201 `ThreadViewModel(handle, repository)` site (seven) `grep`-replaced to `makeVm(handle, repository)` mechanically; three new tests `connectionState_initialValue_isConnected` (synchronous read, no collector, pins `Connected` initialValue), `connectionState_reemitsOnSourceChange` (`FakeConnectionStateSource.emit(Offline)` → `vm.connectionState.value == Offline`), `retry_invokesSourceRetry` (uses file-private `RecordingConnectionStateSource` test double with `var retryCallCount` counter — fake's `retry()` is a no-op so an assertion-of-effect through it would have nothing to observe; the recording double is ~10 lines and stays test-local because no other consumer needs it). Previews keep steady-state `ConnectionState.Connected` only — the four-state matrix already lives in `ConnectionBannerPreviewMatrix` from #200; duplicating here would add no fidelity. Post-#246 the `LazyColumn` body iterates `state.items.asReversed()` with sealed-interface dispatch onto `MessageBubble` / `SessionBoundaryDelimiter` (see body-shape paragraph above); `ThreadUiState` widened to its seventh field `items: List<ThreadItem> = emptyList()`, populated from the same `combine` arm that already drives `hasMessages`. New test `state_items_reflectsObserveMessagesStream` boots the VM on `seed-channel-personal` and asserts the seeded messages reach `vm.state.value.items.first() as ThreadItem.MessageItem` — runs through the `runTest` + `UnconfinedTestDispatcher` + collector-cancel scaffold inherited from #139. Previews now seed `isPromoted = true, items = previewItems()` via a file-private `previewItems(): List<ThreadItem>` helper (one user / assistant / tool / `SessionBoundary(Clear)`, timestamps via `Instant.parse("2026-05-17T14:32:00Z")` + 10s/20s/60s offsets); `isPromoted = true` is load-bearing because `hasMessages` is a *raw* field on a hand-constructed `ThreadUiState` (no VM reducer, no `items.any { … }` derivation), so without it the `WorkspaceChip` gate `!isPromoted && !hasMessages` would light. Downstream slices remaining (each replaces or layers over a body slot, not the chrome): [#136](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/136) above-delimiter opacity (composes `Modifier.alpha(0.5f)` over rows above each `SessionBoundary`; was `blocked-by` #246), #137 / #138 empty states (both landed — #137 wires the [`WorkspaceChip`](features/workspace-chip.md), #138 wires the centered [`EmptyThreadState`](features/empty-thread-state.md) prompt in the `weight(1f)` slot when `!hasMessages`), [#252](codebase/252.md) overflow-menu mount (renames `onOverflowClick` → `onOverflowEvent`, hoists `overflowExpanded` via `rememberSaveable`, binds `vm::onOverflowEvent`), #141 rename dialog (wires `onTitleClick`; live re-emission already free), #145 status row, generic scroll-to-bottom on every message append (`LazyListState` + `LaunchedEffect(state.items.size) { animateScrollToItem(0) }`; still not ticketed and distinct from [`#185`](codebase/185.md)'s streaming-only auto-anchor). Streaming auto-scroll landed in [`#185`](codebase/185.md): inside the `state.hasMessages == true` arm, a hoisted `rememberLazyListState()` is threaded as `state =` on the `LazyColumn`; a `remember(state.items) { derivedStateOf { state.items.any { it is ThreadItem.MessageItem && it.message.isStreaming } } }` gates a `LaunchedEffect(hasStreamingMessage, listState)` whose body early-returns when the flag is false (AC4) and otherwise runs `snapshotFlow { listState.layoutInfo.visibleItemsInfo.firstOrNull { it.index == 0 }?.size ?: 0 }.distinctUntilChanged().collect { if (!userScrolledAway) listState.scrollToItem(0) }` — re-anchoring on every layout-pass size change of item 0 as the [`StreamingAssistantBody`](features/message-bubble.md) reveals new characters and `MarkdownText` re-flows. User drag/fling is detected via a `NestedScrollConnection` filtered on `source == NestedScrollSource.UserInput && available.y != 0f` (attached via `Modifier.nestedScroll(autoScrollNestedScroll)` on the `LazyColumn`); a second `LaunchedEffect(listState)` on `snapshotFlow { firstVisibleItemIndex == 0 && firstVisibleItemScrollOffset == 0 }` clears the yield flag when the user manually returns to the bottom so auto-follow resumes for the remainder of the reveal. `scrollToItem(0)` (not `animateScrollToItem`) is the chosen primitive — instant, O(1) when already pinned, and it does **not** dispatch through `NestedScrollSource.UserInput`, so the programmatic re-anchor cannot recursively trip its own yield flag (whereas `animateScrollToItem`'s `SideEffect`-source dispatch would, plus its overlapping animations would visibly stutter at the streaming cadence). When `isStreaming` flips false the `LaunchedEffect` re-launches and the early-return cancels the collector (AC3); non-streaming threads start no collector (AC4). No `ThreadViewModel` / `MessageBubble` / `StreamingAssistantBody` change — the auto-scroll is purely screen-internal, consuming the same `Message.isStreaming` contract [#184](codebase/184.md) seeded. Reverse-layout's bottom anchor is `firstVisibleItemIndex == 0 && firstVisibleItemScrollOffset == 0`; both effects rely on that. Phase 0's static seed never flips `isStreaming = false` so AC3's cancel path is verifiable only by code-review or local seed flip; Phase 4's WS feed exercises it naturally. The [#184](codebase/184.md) off-screen-disposal trade-off (`StreamingAssistantBody`'s `revealedLength` resets on re-entry) is sidestepped in the auto-pin happy path because the bubble stays in the viewport; if the user yields by scrolling away during streaming, the Phase-4 hoist-into-VM fix from [#184](codebase/184.md) is the proper remedy.
- [Thread input bar](features/thread-input-bar.md) — bottom-anchored composer for [Thread screen](features/thread-screen.md), landed in [#188](codebase/188.md) atop the `sendMessage` mutator from [#187](codebase/187.md). Public composable in `ui/conversations/thread/ThreadInputBar.kt` with the **stateful + stateless overload pair** pattern (stateful holds `var text by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }` and delegates; stateless takes `(text, onTextChange, onSend, modifier)`; previews call the stateless overload directly — sets the precedent for future composer-shaped composables that need both a VM-mount entry point and a hoisted-state preview entry point). Layout (Figma subframe `16:61` of [`16:8`](https://www.figma.com/design/g2HIq2UyPhslEoHRokQmHG?node-id=16-8)): outer `Column` with `Modifier.fillMaxWidth().background(surface).imePadding()` (IME-lift lives **only on the input bar**, not the `Scaffold` body or screen root — putting it on the screen would shift the top app bar too); 1dp `HorizontalDivider(color = outlineVariant)` at the top of the `Column` (picked over the spec's `Modifier.drawBehind` alternative for simplicity); composer `Row` with padding `start = 12.dp, end = 12.dp, top = 8.dp, bottom = 12.dp`, `Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)`, `verticalAlignment = Alignment.Bottom` so the pill grows upward while the send button anchors. Pill `Surface(shape = RoundedCornerShape(28.dp), color = surfaceContainerHigh, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).heightIn(min = 48.dp))` wraps a nested `Row` (padding `start = 16.dp, end = 4.dp, vertical = 4.dp`, 4dp gap) holding `BasicTextField` left + 40dp `IconButton(Icons.Outlined.Mic, 22dp, tint = onSurfaceVariant)` right. `BasicTextField` over `TextField` because the pill `Surface` already supplies container styling — material `TextField` would have to override `TextFieldDefaults.colors` to transparent on every slot; non-default settings are `cursorBrush = SolidColor(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary)` (BasicTextField's default cursor is solid black and is invisible against dark theme — **load-bearing fix, document this for the next `BasicTextField` consumer**), `singleLine = false, maxLines = 5` (visible-line cap, no character cap), `KeyboardOptions(imeAction = ImeAction.Send) + KeyboardActions(onSend = { onSend() })` (IME `Send` action invokes the same path as the button — load-bearing because some keyboards fire `Send` even when the field is `isBlank()`, which is why the VM's `if (text.isBlank()) return` second-checks), `decorationBox = { innerTextField -> Box { if (text.isEmpty()) Text(R.string.thread_input_placeholder, color = onSurfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.6f)); innerTextField() } }` — placeholder uses `text.isEmpty()` not `isBlank()` so a leading space doesn't clobber the placeholder mid-typing. Mic stub: `val context = LocalContext.current` + `val voiceToast = stringResource(R.string.voice_input_toast)` captured once at composable scope; `onClick = { Toast.makeText(context, voiceToast, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }` — no ViewModel involvement (VMs have no `Context`; one-shot side effect; gets replaced by real voice input in Phase 6). Toast literal `"Voice input — Phase 6"` uses an **em dash**, not hyphen (the exact string matters; lives in `R.string.voice_input_toast` rather than as a Kotlin literal at the call site — sets the precedent that user-facing side-effect copy goes on the translation surface even when there's no plan to localize before Play Store launch).
