Send button: `FilledTonalIconButton(onClick = onSend, enabled = text.isNotBlank(), shape = CircleShape, colors = IconButtonDefaults.filledTonalIconButtonColors(containerColor = surfaceContainerHigh), modifier = Modifier.size(48.dp)) { Icon(Icons.Outlined.ArrowUpward, R.string.cd_send_message, 22dp) }` — **two non-default overrides** because the M3 defaults disagree with Figma: (1) `shape = CircleShape` (M3 default is rounded rectangle; Figma is a 48dp circle) and (2) `containerColor = surfaceContainerHigh` (M3 default is `secondaryContainer` which renders as a tinted teal/lavender under Material You dynamic color, breaking the visual cluster with the pill — any future `FilledTonalIconButton` against a neutral surface needs the same override). `enabled = text.isNotBlank()` is the UI half of belt-and-suspenders blank rejection; the VM half is `if (text.isBlank()) return` at the top of `ThreadViewModel.sendMessage` — **don't remove either**; they defend different code paths (UI disables the button, VM second-checks for the IME-`Send`-on-blank case). The stateful overload clears `text` to `""` on send **only when `text.isNotBlank()`**, so the IME `Send` action on a blank field is a true no-op, not a clear. `ThreadViewModel.sendMessage(text: String)`: `if (text.isBlank()) return; viewModelScope.launch { repository.sendMessage(state.value.conversationId, text) }` — fire-and-forget (return value discarded; UI re-renders from the future #128 `observeMessages` subscription). Required the trivial promotion `repository` → `private val repository` in the constructor (one-token edit; Koin binding at `di/AppModule.kt:28` unchanged). `ThreadScreen` grew one new required parameter `onSendMessage: (String) -> Unit` (no default) and a new `bottomBar = { ThreadInputBar(onSend = onSendMessage) }` slot on the `Scaffold`; the screen signature **stays a flat callback list** rather than folding into a sealed `ThreadEvent` (the #139 doc predicted the fold would happen when "the first VM-owned event lands (#133's composer send is the most likely first)" — that prediction was wrong by one ticket; flat-callbacks for one event arm is cheaper than scaffolding a sealed envelope for one arm; the fold is deferred to whichever ticket lands the *second* VM-owned event, #140 overflow or #141 rename). `MainActivity.kt:206` edit is one named argument `onSendMessage = vm::sendMessage` — method-reference syntax over `{ text -> vm.sendMessage(text) }` avoids a new lambda allocation per recomposition of the destination block. Four new `R.string.*` entries in `res/values/strings.xml`: `thread_input_placeholder = "Message"`, `cd_voice_input = "Voice input"`, `cd_send_message = "Send message"`, `voice_input_toast = "Voice input — Phase 6"`. Test surface: two new `ThreadViewModelTest` methods (`sendMessage_blankText_isNoOp` pins the `if (isBlank()) return` short-circuit; `sendMessage_nonBlankText_appendsToConversation` pins the VM → repository forwarding end-to-end against the live `FakeConversationRepository`, asserting `content = "Hello world"`, `role = Role.User`, `sessionId = "seed-session-personal"`); reuses the existing `Dispatchers.setMain(UnconfinedTestDispatcher())` + `runTest { launch collector; advanceUntilIdle(); … collector.cancel() }` scaffold from #139. Mic / Toast intentionally untested (Toast is a system service, would need Robolectric or instrumented test; Phase 6 replaces the stub anyway). Four `@Preview`s at the bottom of the file all calling the stateless overload (`InputBar — Light, Empty`, `InputBar — Light, Filled`, `InputBar — Dark, Empty`, `InputBar — Dark, Filled`); preview naming `<Component> — <Theme>, <Variant>` is the established pattern. No new Gradle entries — `material-icons-extended` (`Icons.Outlined.Mic`, `Icons.Outlined.ArrowUpward`) has been on classpath since #129. Edge cases: no error surfacing (Phase 0; coroutine failure is silent; `IllegalArgumentException` on unknown id cannot happen at this call site because the VM only ever passes its own observed id), no optimistic echo at the screen level (sending appears as a cleared field with nothing visible above it until #128 wires `observeMessages`), no undo for the cleared field, no slash-command plumbing, no character cap on `text`. **Replaces #133 in the downstream-of-thread-screen list** — #133 was the original composer ticket that got split into #187 (repository mutator) + #188 (UI); both have now shipped and #133 itself can be closed as fulfilled.
- [ChannelListScreen](features/channel-list-screen.md) — first stateless `(state, onEvent)` screen; M3 `Scaffold` with a `TopAppBar` (since #68: 28dp `ic_pyry_logo` `navigationIcon` tinted `colorScheme.primary` inside a 40dp `Box` + `app_name` title + trailing settings-gear `IconButton`, #21) and a trailing `FloatingActionButton` (#22, rendered only in `Loaded`/`Empty` — `Icons.Default.Add`) wraps the four `ChannelListUiState` variants. The private `RecentDiscussionsSection` composable gained a `lastMessages: Map<String, Message>` parameter in #162 — both `Empty` and `Loaded` branches forward `state.recentDiscussionLastMessages` (the data path from #161) through to the section, and the section's `forEach` passes `lastMessages[conversation.id]` into each `DiscussionPreviewRow` so per-row bodies render real `Message.content` instead of the retired `R.string.discussion_preview_placeholder_body` literal. Screen-level previews still rely on `recentDiscussionLastMessages`'s `= emptyMap()` default so each section row collapses to the no-body shape there; populated-body coverage lives in `DiscussionPreviewRow`'s own previews. `Loaded` is a `Column` of a file-local `ChannelsSectionHeader()` ("Channels", `labelLarge` on `onSurfaceVariant @ 0.85f`, #68) → `LazyColumn` of `ConversationRow`s plus a trailing `item(key = "recent-discussions-section")` hosting `RecentDiscussionsSection` (#69); `Empty` renders the same section above the centred `"Tap + to start a conversation"` copy. Centred-`Text` for `Loading` / `Error` (no section, no FAB). Dispatches `ChannelListEvent.RowTapped` / `SettingsTapped` / `RecentDiscussionsTapped` (resolved directly to `navController.navigate`) and `CreateDiscussionTapped` (forwarded into `vm.onEvent`) via the destination-level `onEvent` lambda. Destination collects `vm.navigationEvents` in `LaunchedEffect(vm)` for one-shot nav. Introduces the `koinViewModel<…>()` + `collectAsStateWithLifecycle()` call-site shape at the `channel_list` NavHost destination, and pulls `androidx.compose.material:material-icons-core` onto the classpath for `Icons.Default.Settings` / `Icons.Default.Add` / `Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowForward`. Six previews (Loaded + Loaded+discussions + Empty + Empty+discussions light; Loaded + Empty dark — reshaped in #69). The inline section is composed of two file-private composables `RecentDiscussionsSection` (divider + header + up-to-3 `DiscussionPreviewRow`s + See-all link; guarded with `if (discussions.isEmpty()) return` so empty discussions collapse the entire block — "no orphan header") and `SeeAllDiscussionsRow` (label + 18dp `Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowForward`, both `colorScheme.primary`; merged-descendants `semantics` carries `contentDescription` + `Role.Button`). The previously-shipped `RecentDiscussionsPill` and its two strings were deleted in #69. Instrumented Compose tests since #99 — six-method `ChannelListScreenTest` at `app/src/androidTest/.../conversations/list/ChannelListScreenTest.kt` covers `TopAppBar` title + settings gear, each channel name in `Loaded`, the three event dispatches (`RowTapped(id)`, `SettingsTapped`, `CreateDiscussionTapped`), and the `Empty` placeholder; constructs `ChannelListUiState` instances directly with a local `channel(id, name)` fixture builder and captures events into a `mutableListOf::add`, skipping the VM / Koin / `FakeConversationRepository` entirely. Since #221 the M3 `FloatingActionButton` widget is replaced by a file-private `ChannelListFab(onTap, onLongPress, onTapLabel, onLongPressLabel)` — a manually-composed non-clickable `Surface(modifier = Modifier.size(56.dp).combinedClickable(onClick, onLongClick, onClickLabel, onLongClickLabel, role = Role.Button), shape = FloatingActionButtonDefaults.shape, color = primaryContainer, contentColor = onPrimaryContainer, tonalElevation = 6.dp, shadowElevation = 6.dp) { Box(fillMaxSize(), Alignment.Center) { Icon(Icons.Default.Add, contentDescription = null) } }` (the inner `Box` carries the centring the M3 widget did internally; the inner `Icon.contentDescription = null` because `combinedClickable.onClickLabel` / `onLongClickLabel` carry the a11y descriptions — duplicating would announce twice). The rewrite was forced because the M3 widget composes its own inner `Surface(onClick = ...)` which installs a `Modifier.clickable` on a leaf node that consumes pointer events before they can propagate up to any outer `combinedClickable` — same shadowing failure mode as [#25](codebase/25.md)'s `ConversationRow.ListItem.clickable` (see [`codebase/221.md`](codebase/221.md)'s "Lessons learned" for the full reasoning). The non-clickable `Surface { content }` overload (no `onClick` parameter) has no inner clickable, so `combinedClickable` becomes the sole pointer-input subscriber. Tradeoff: M3's "lower elevation while held" press-animation is lost (we don't replicate the internal `InteractionSource` plumbing); the `combinedClickable` default ripple via `LocalIndication.current` covers the user-facing feedback. Accepted Phase 0 tradeoff — revert to the native widget if a future Compose Material 3 BOM exposes `onLongClick` natively. Three new `ChannelListEvent` variants (#221): `LongPressFab` (data object — opens the picker), `WorkspacePicked(workspace: String)` (data class — confirm with payload, triggers `repository.createDiscussion(workspace = path)` then `ToThread(id)`), `WorkspacePickerDismissed` (data object — closes without creating). Three-variant split mirrors #78's `PromoteConfirmed`/`PromoteCancelled` rationale — "dismissed without picking" must NOT trigger the side effect. [`WorkspacePicker`](features/workspace-picker.md) host is composed as a sibling of the `Scaffold` (not inside its content lambda — the `ModalBottomSheet`'s window-level scrim would otherwise conflate with the body's `LazyColumn` layout; same shape as #78's `SaveAsChannelDialog`), gated on a `pickerVisible` derived from an exhaustive `when (state)` block mapping `Loaded`/`Empty` to their `workspacePickerVisible` field and `Loading`/`Error` to `false`. `MainActivity`'s destination `when (event)` comma-groups the four VM-forwarded variants (`CreateDiscussionTapped, LongPressFab, is WorkspacePicked, WorkspacePickerDismissed -> vm.onEvent(event)`) — one body, four variants, keeps the nav-vs-VM split visually obvious. New string `R.string.cd_long_press_fab_pick_workspace` ("Pick a workspace for the new discussion"); existing `cd_new_discussion` reused as the `onClickLabel`. Combined, TalkBack announces "New discussion, button, double-tap to activate, long-press to pick workspace." No new androidTest in #221 — mirrors #22's "no UI test for the FAB" decision; [#220](codebase/220.md)'s `WorkspacePicker` androidTest already covers the host, and the screen-level wiring is a hoisted `visible` prop + `onPicked`/`onDismiss` pass-through. The previously-deferred "Phase 2 — Workspace picker is part of `ConversationThread` design" decision from #22 was walked back: the picker host (#220) is reusable across screens, the FAB's gesture surface was the natural entry point, and the Thread's own picker access points ([#137](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/137), [#208](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/208)) consume the same host independently.
- [MarkdownText](features/markdown-text.md) — CommonMark renderer (#129) for assistant-message content; `@Composable fun MarkdownText(markdown: String, modifier: Modifier = Modifier)` parses with `org.jetbrains:markdown` 0.7.3 via `remember(markdown) { MarkdownParser(CommonMarkFlavourDescriptor()).buildMarkdownTreeFromString(markdown) }` then walks the AST through `MarkdownBlock(node, source, uriHandler)` (a `when (node.type)` over `MarkdownElementTypes` dispatching ATX_1 → `headlineSmall`, ATX_2 → `titleLarge`, ATX_3 → `titleMedium`, PARAGRAPH → `bodyMedium`, UNORDERED_LIST → `Column { … Row { Text("•"); Spacer(ListItemIndent = 8.dp); Column { recurse } } … }`, ORDERED_LIST → same with `"${index + 1}."` marker, BLOCK_QUOTE → `Row(IntrinsicSize.Min) { Box(BlockquoteBarWidth = 4.dp, fillMaxHeight, background = outlineVariant); Spacer(BlockquoteContentIndent = 12.dp); Column { italic bodyMedium paragraphs } }`, CODE_FENCE / CODE_BLOCK → `Surface(RoundedCornerShape(CodeBlockCornerRadius = 8.dp), surfaceContainer) { Text(monospace bodyMedium, padding = horizontal 12.dp vertical 8.dp) }`, else → `Text(node.getTextInNode(source).trim(), style = bodyMedium)` graceful fallback) wrapped in `Column(modifier, verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(ParagraphSpacing = 8.dp))`. Inline content goes through `appendInline(node, source, uriHandler, codeSpanBg, linkColor)` building an `AnnotatedString` with EMPH → `withStyle(SpanStyle(fontStyle = Italic))`, STRONG → `withStyle(SpanStyle(fontWeight = Bold))`, CODE_SPAN → `withStyle(SpanStyle(fontFamily = Monospace, background = surfaceContainer)) { append(trim('\`')) }` (background paints at glyph-rect bounds — Compose's text API has no per-span horizontal padding option; acceptable for #129, follow-up shape if it becomes tight is `InlineTextContent` per span), INLINE_LINK → `withLink(LinkAnnotation.Url(url, styles = TextLinkStyles(SpanStyle(color = primary, textDecoration = Underline)), linkInteractionListener = …)) { append(linkText) }`, EOL → `append(" ")` (CommonMark soft-break = space), leaf / recurse fallback for everything else. Public signature is **deliberately minimal — no `style`, no `color`, no `onLinkClick` parameters**: text styles resolve from `MaterialTheme.typography.<slot>` by element kind (AC5 "no `TextStyle` or `.sp` literals" holds by construction), text colour inherits from `LocalContentColor.current` (the [`MessageBubble`](features/message-bubble.md) assistant variant supplies `MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface` via `CompositionLocalProvider(LocalContentColor provides …) { MarkdownText(...) }` — keeps the renderer reusable for future surfaces like system messages or tool cards without growing its signature), links open via `LocalUriHandler.current.openUri(url)` directly. **Security:** `#129` carries the `security-sensitive` label; the mitigation is file-private `isSafeLinkScheme(url): Boolean = url.substringBefore(':', missingDelimiterValue = "").lowercase() in setOf("http", "https", "mailto")` consulted by the `LinkInteractionListener` before calling `uriHandler.openUri(url)` — rejected URLs no-op silently (the underlined span remains drawn, the tap does nothing), allowlist over denylist to be robust against case / whitespace tricks (`InTeNt://`, `\tjavascript:`) and against future scheme additions; new safe schemes (e.g. `tel:` for click-to-dial) are an explicit one-line addition. HTML / images / non-allowed schemes never reach an execution path — `HTML_BLOCK` / `HTML_INLINE` / image markdown fall through to raw-text `bodyMedium` rendering, so there's no script execution and no SSRF surface. Six file-private spacing constants at the top of the file (`ParagraphSpacing`, `ListItemIndent`, `BlockquoteBarWidth`, `BlockquoteContentIndent`, `CodeBlockCornerRadius`, `CodeBlockPadding`) — same shape as [`MessageBubble`](features/message-bubble.md)'s file-private constants. Pure stateless composable — no `LaunchedEffect`, no coroutines, no flows; parsing is synchronous CPU-only (sub-millisecond for typical assistant messages). Streaming (#132) is not specially handled here — partial markdown strings flow through unchanged, the `remember(markdown)` re-parses per token batch which stays sub-millisecond; incremental-parse is a #132-scope optimisation if traces ever show pressure, not pre-paid here. **Renderer is total** — the JetBrains parser produces an AST for any input string, unsupported AST kinds (tables, HTML, horizontal rules, strikethrough, task lists, images, autolinks beyond `[text](url)`) hit the `else` fallback so the message is never blank. Fenced code blocks gained syntax highlighting in #130 via `dev.snipme:highlights` 1.1.0 ([ADR 0003](decisions/0003-syntax-highlighter-library.md)): same flat `Surface(surfaceContainer)` (no outline border — deliberate divergence from Figma `16:45`–`16:50` which shows `surface` + `outline-variant`), `CodeBlock(content, language)` widens to accept the fence info string and renders an optional top-right `labelSmall` / `onSurfaceVariant` language label aligned `Alignment.TopEnd` outside the `horizontalScroll` chain; `Modifier.horizontalScroll(rememberScrollState())` + `Text(softWrap = false)` handle long lines; `buildHighlightedCode` walks the library's `CodeStructure` and applies `SpanStyle`s bound to M3 slots (`keywords`/`annotations` → `tertiary` + Medium weight, `strings` → `secondary`, `literals` → `primary`, `comments`/`multilineComments` → `onSurfaceVariant` + italic, default → inherited `LocalContentColor.current`) so AC6 "no hardcoded hex" holds by construction; `resolveSyntaxLanguage` maps `kotlin`/`kt`/`kts` → `KOTLIN`, `bash`/`sh`/`shell`/`zsh` → `SHELL`, `json` → `JAVASCRIPT` (subset substitute — no dedicated JSON lexer), `markdown`/`md` and unknown languages → `null` plain-monospace fallback; `tokeniseCode` wraps the library call in `runCatching { … }.getOrNull()` and `applySpan` clamps `PhraseLocation` ranges to `(0, contentLength)` before `addStyle` — renderer stays total even on library regressions. The `MARKDOWN_PREVIEW_FIXTURE` in `MessageBubble.kt` was extended in #130 with JSON / Bash / Markdown fences alongside the existing Kotlin one so the same two preview functions cover AC7 (Kotlin / JSON / Bash / Markdown in light + dark) — no new `@Preview` functions added. Library wire-up is the minimum possible diff: one `[versions]` line + one `[libraries]` line in `gradle/libs.versions.toml` (`jetbrainsMarkdown = "0.7.3"` / `jetbrains-markdown = { group = "org.jetbrains", name = "markdown", version.ref = "jetbrainsMarkdown" }`) + one `implementation(libs.jetbrains.markdown)` line in `app/build.gradle.kts` — no KSP, no kapt, no proguard rules, no Gradle plugin entry (pure-Kotlin reflection-free library). Library-choice rationale recorded in [ADR 0002 — markdown renderer library](decisions/0002-markdown-renderer-library.md): rejects `dev.jeziellago:compose-markdown` (a Markwon-on-`AndroidView` wrapper that would introduce an Android-View interop seam the codebase doesn't have today, and whose heading sizes are relative multipliers on a single base style — couldn't satisfy AC5's "each heading maps to a distinct M3 typography slot" without writing a custom Markwon plugin roughly the same size as a hand-rolled visitor walk). The canonical preview pair lives in [`MessageBubble.kt`](features/message-bubble.md) (`MessageBubbleMarkdownLightPreview` / `MessageBubbleMarkdownDarkPreview`, both `widthDp = 412`, exercising every AC element through the assistant variant), not in `MarkdownText.kt` itself — `MarkdownText`'s only consumer today is the [`MessageBubble`](features/message-bubble.md) assistant variant. No unit tests, no instrumented Compose UI tests — same precedent as #126 / #128; codebase has no `ComposeTestRule` / Robolectric infrastructure and building it for a pure-rendering 332-line component is over-scope; visual verification is the previews + `./gradlew assembleDebug` + `./gradlew lint` passing.
- [ToolCallRow](features/tool-call-row.md) — stateless row primitive (#131) for `Role.Tool` messages in the conversation thread, routed from [`MessageBubble`](features/message-bubble.md)'s `Role.Tool` arm via `message.toolCall?.let { ToolCallRow(toolCall = it, modifier = modifier) }` (the `?.let` absorbs the `Message.toolCall` non-null-iff-`Role.Tool` invariant silently — identical posture to the pre-#131 `Role.Tool -> Unit`, no defensive throw); routing happens inside `MessageBubble` rather than `ThreadScreen` because the dispatcher already owns the `Role`-fanout. `@Composable fun ToolCallRow(toolCall: ToolCall, modifier: Modifier = Modifier)` carries one piece of internal state — `var expanded by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }` (survives configuration changes; no `key` parameter; lost on `LazyColumn` item disposal — Phase-0 acceptable, ViewModel-side hoisting keyed on `Message.id` is the fix when users complain) — and delegates to a private stateless `ToolCallRowContent(toolCall, expanded, onToggle, modifier)` so previews can pin both `expanded = false` and `expanded = true` snapshots without driving the toggle through Android Studio's preview runtime (same stateful-wrapper + stateless-content split as [#184](codebase/184.md)'s `StreamingAssistantBody` / `StreamingAssistantBodyView`). Outer `Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(bottom = MessageRowVerticalSpacing = 12.dp))` wraps a `Surface(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clickable(onClick = onToggle), shape = RoundedCornerShape(ToolCallCornerRadius = 12.dp), color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceContainerHigh)` with inner `Column(padding(horizontal = ToolCallHorizontalPadding = 12.dp, vertical = ToolCallVerticalPadding = 8.dp))` containing the always-present `CollapsedHeaderRow(toolCall)` and the conditional `if (expanded) ExpandedBody(toolCall)`. Three deliberate Figma deviations from `16:28`–`16:31` recorded by the architect spec — (1) background `surfaceContainerHigh` with no border (vs Figma's `surface-container` + 1dp `outline-variant` outline; the flat tonal slot matches the assistant variant from [#128](codebase/128.md) so the `Role.User` `primaryContainer` / tool card `surfaceContainerHigh` / session-delimiter transparent form a three-step contrast ladder legible without an outline stroke); (2) leading 18dp `Icon(imageVector = iconForTool(toolCall.toolName), contentDescription = null, tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)` added per AC5 (Figma has no icon; `contentDescription = null` because the adjacent `Text` carries the semantic content — a TalkBack user hears `"Read · path/to/file.kt"`, not `"Read · path/to/file.kt, document icon"`); (3) tap-to-toggle on the whole `Surface` via `Modifier.clickable(onClick = onToggle)` with the default M3 ripple, no chevron / caret rendered (the entire surface is the click target — a chevron is design-for-hypothetical-future). Two-tone summary preserved verbatim from Figma — `buildSummaryAnnotated(toolCall)` is a `@Composable` builder constructing an `AnnotatedString` of the form `"$toolName · $primaryArg"` with the tool-name span styled `SpanStyle(color = tertiary, fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace)` (matches Figma's `Roboto Mono Regular`) and the separator + arg span styled `SpanStyle(color = onSurfaceVariant)` proportional (matches Figma's `body-small`); the `Text` renders with `MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium`, `maxLines = 1`, `overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis`, and `Modifier.weight(1f)` so long primary-args ellipsise rather than wrap (AC4). Primary-arg derivation is `private fun primaryArg(toolCall: ToolCall): String = toolCall.input.trim()` — one statement, no parser, no regex, no first-param lookup; given [`ToolCall(toolName, input, output)`](features/data-model.md) from [#127](codebase/127.md) the `input` field carries the path (for `Read` / `Edit`) or the command (for `Bash`) directly as a flat string, so the AC's three-way branching collapses to a one-liner — don't pre-bake a JSON parser or branch table; when [#191](codebase/191.md)'s eventual structured payload replaces `input: String` the helper gains real branching and the public `ToolCallRow` signature stays unchanged. Icon mapping is case-insensitive on the trimmed name (`"read"` → `Icons.Outlined.Description`, `"edit"` → `Icons.Outlined.Edit`, `"bash"` → `Icons.Outlined.Terminal`, else → `Icons.Outlined.Build`), `Outlined` family because passive metadata reads better than filled against `surfaceContainerHigh`. Expanded body is `Column(padding(top = 8.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp))` of two `ExpandedSection(label, content)` calls — `"Input"` then `"Output"` (English-only Phase-0 copy) — each section is a `labelSmall` caption in `onSurfaceVariant` on top of the body, with the body dispatched by `isCodeIsh(content) = content.contains('\n') || content.length > CODE_ISH_LENGTH_THRESHOLD = 80` — code-ish content routes through `CodeBlock(content, language = null)` from [`MarkdownText`](features/markdown-text.md) (widened from `private` to `internal` by this ticket — one-keyword edit, no body change; cross-file reuse pattern where the smallest visibility widening wins over duplicating the rounded-monospace surface boilerplate), plain content renders as `Text(content, style = bodyMedium.copy(fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace), color = onSurface)` with no nested surface wrapper. Eight file-private spacing `val`s + one `CODE_ISH_LENGTH_THRESHOLD` const at the top of the file (no `.dp` literal at any call site); `MessageRowVerticalSpacing = 12.dp` is redeclared here rather than imported from `MessageBubble.kt` — duplication is intentional, the size-S budget doesn't include a cross-component refactor, rule-of-three triggers extraction to `Spacing.kt` or `LocalSpacing` when a third component declares the same constant. New `androidx.compose.material:material-icons-extended` library dependency (one `[libraries]` line in `gradle/libs.versions.toml`, one `implementation(libs.androidx.compose.material.icons.extended)` line in `app/build.gradle.kts`, BOM-managed) required for `Description`/`Terminal`/`Build` icons (`Edit` was already in `material-icons-core`); ~3–4 MB debug-APK bloat from the ~7000-icon set, R8 tree-shakes unreferenced icons in release builds so production cost is bounded to the three actually called. Two `@Preview` functions (`ToolCallRowLightPreview` `darkTheme = false`, `ToolCallRowDarkPreview` `darkTheme = true` + `uiMode = Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES`) both delegate to a shared `ToolCallRowPreviewMatrix()` that renders 6 cells (3 tools × 2 states) by invoking `ToolCallRowContent` directly with pinned `expanded` values — 12 visual cells total across 2 preview functions, AC7 satisfied. Three file-private preview fixtures (`PreviewReadToolCall`, `PreviewEditToolCall`, `PreviewBashToolCall`) live at the bottom of `ToolCallRow.kt`, not added to [`FakeConversationRepository`](features/conversation-repository.md) — keeping previews self-contained means changing the rendering doesn't ripple into the data-layer fake. No unit tests, no Compose UI tests — same precedent as [#128](codebase/128.md) / [#129](codebase/129.md) / [#130](codebase/130.md) / [#184](codebase/184.md); the three helpers (`primaryArg`, `isCodeIsh`, `iconForTool`) are trivially correct by inspection, visual verification by previews + `./gradlew assembleDebug` + `./gradlew lint`. **[#388](codebase/388.md) added the live status affordance** (supersedes the stale "leading 18dp Icon" / "two ExpandedSection" / "6 preview cells" / "no Compose UI tests" claims above): the single leading `Icon` became a private `ToolCallStatusIcon(toolCall)` — an exhaustive `when (toolCall.status)` ([`ToolCallStatus`](features/data-model.md), populated live by [#387](codebase/387.md)) at a fixed `ToolCallIconSize = 18.dp` footprint (so a `Running → Done/Failed` flip never reflows) — `Running` → indeterminate `CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth = ToolCallSpinnerStrokeWidth = 2.dp)` carrying `cd_tool_running` via `Modifier.semantics`, `Done` → the existing per-tool `Icon` moved **verbatim** (settled Figma `16-28`, unchanged), `Failed` → `Icons.Outlined.ErrorOutline` tinted `colorScheme.error` carrying `cd_tool_failed`; `ExpandedBody` now gates the `"Output"` `ExpandedSection` behind `if (toolCall.status != Running)` (output is `""` mid-run, filled on the correlated `tool_result`) while `"Input"` always renders; statelessness preserved (pure function of `status`, no `remember`/`derivedStateOf` over it, the only local state is the orthogonal `expanded` toggle); two new `strings.xml` content descriptions (`cd_tool_running`/`cd_tool_failed`, `cd_*` convention); the preview matrix grew to 10 cells (added `PreviewRunningToolCall` status=Running output="" + `PreviewFailedToolCall` status=Failed, each collapsed+expanded) across the unchanged Light+Dark `@Preview` pair (AC#4); a new instrumented `ToolCallRowTest` (5 scenarios, mirrors `ThinkingIndicatorTest`) compiles but `connectedAndroidTest` not run (no device). No signature change, no caller edit ([`MessageBubble.kt:55`](features/message-bubble.md) already passes the whole `ToolCall`); running/failed glyphs are **design-owed** (not in Figma `16-28`, follow the M3 progress idiom mirroring [`ThinkingIndicator`](features/thinking-indicator.md)). Size:s, **not** security-sensitive (render-only; the output-encoding hand-off [#387](codebase/387.md) named is satisfied by rendering the server strings through inert Compose `Text`/`CodeBlock`). Code review **PASS**, zero blocking ([PR #411](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/pull/411)).
- [MessageBubble](features/message-bubble.md) — stateless row primitive (#128) for the [Thread screen](features/thread-screen.md) `LazyColumn`; `@Composable fun MessageBubble(message: Message, modifier: Modifier = Modifier)` dispatches `message.role` through an exhaustive `when` with three arms — `Role.User` → file-private `UserMessageBubble(content, modifier)` (outer `Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.End)` pinning a `Surface(modifier = Modifier.widthIn(max = UserBubbleMaxWidth = 320.dp), shape = UserBubbleShape = RoundedCornerShape(topStart = 20.dp, topEnd = 20.dp, bottomEnd = 6.dp, bottomStart = 20.dp), color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primaryContainer)` wrapping a `Text(content, modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = BubbleHorizontalPadding = 14.dp, vertical = BubbleVerticalPadding = 12.dp), style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onPrimaryContainer)` — inner padding lives on the `Text`, not the `Surface`, so the painted `primaryContainer` background extends under the padding band; the asymmetric `bottomEnd = 6.dp` corner is the "tail" pointing toward the user side; the `widthIn(max = 320.dp)` cap is the 4dp-grid-aligned approximation of Figma `16:23` `w-[330px]` on the 412dp canvas); `Role.Assistant` → file-private `AssistantMessage(content, modifier)` (`Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth())` wrapping a left-aligned [`MarkdownText(markdown = content, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth())`](features/markdown-text.md) inside `CompositionLocalProvider(LocalContentColor provides MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface) { … }` since #129 — pre-#129 was a plain `Text(content, style = bodyMedium, color = onSurface)` — **no `Surface`, no `Modifier.background(...)`, no shape, no inner padding** — the assistant message flows in the column with the screen's surface color behind it, **deliberately diverging from Figma `16:25` / `16:32` / `16:42` / `16:54`** which show a bubbled assistant on `surface-container-high` with mirrored `RoundedCornerShape(topStart = 20.dp, topEnd = 20.dp, bottomEnd = 20.dp, bottomStart = 6.dp)` — the ticket body's Context paragraph names this "Claude-style — flat assistant, bubbled user" and AC3 mandates the flat treatment; the spec records "AC wins, Figma reconciliation is a downstream design call" and the project escalation path for reviewer pushback is `needs-rework:po`, not in-PR debate); and `Role.Tool -> Unit` (renders nothing — **deliberate no-op**, not a TODO; the framework contract is that ThreadScreen routes `Role.Tool` to the yet-to-land tool-card composable from #131 before the list reaches this dispatcher, so a defensive `error("Tool role not yet handled")` would crash exactly the path the contract guarantees never happens and a debug placeholder `Text("[tool]")` would leak into production builds where the contract isn't yet in force; the absence of a default `else ->` arm keeps Kotlin's compiler enforcing exhaustiveness so a future fourth `Role` value, e.g. `Role.System`, is flagged at this site as a hard compile-time decision rather than silently absorbed into a fallback). Each variant's outer container carries `Modifier.padding(bottom = MessageRowVerticalSpacing = 12.dp)` so the per-row vertical rhythm lives on the component, not the eventual `LazyColumn` consumer — alternative considered (no padding inside `MessageBubble`; consumer uses `verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(MessageRowVerticalSpacing)`) but rejected to keep AC4 satisfied at the component level today, when no consumer is part of this ticket; bottom-only direction avoids accumulating an extra leading gap above the topmost row, and the trailing 12dp below the last message is acceptable padding before the (eventual) composer / status row. AC4 ("Vertical spacing between consecutive messages uses M3 spacing tokens (not raw `.dp` literals)") is satisfied via **file-private named `val`s at the top of `MessageBubble.kt`**, **not** a `LocalSpacing` `CompositionLocal` — M3 doesn't ship a spacing scale, the codebase has no ambient design-token plumbing, and introducing one to satisfy a single component is infrastructure for hypothetical future requirements; other components in `ui/conversations/components/` (`ArchiveRow.kt:43`, `ConversationRow.kt:53`) still use bare `.dp` literals and **must not be back-filled** with this pattern until they grow their own naming pressure; the first cross-component spacing pressure (e.g. `MessageRowVerticalSpacing` shared with the #135 session-boundary delimiter) is the trigger for extracting to an `internal val` peer file in the same package, mirroring [`RelativeTime.kt`](features/discussion-preview-row.md#shared-relativetime-helper)'s shape. Pure stateless composable — no `remember`, no `LaunchedEffect`, no coroutines; `Message` is a `data class` with all stable fields (`String`, `Role` enum, `kotlinx.datetime.Instant`, `Boolean`) so Compose's stability inference skips recompositions on identity-equal and `equals`-equal inputs without any `@Stable` / `@Immutable` annotation. `Message.timestamp` is ignored in this ticket (per-bubble timestamp surfacing is handled elsewhere); `Message.isStreaming` is consumed by the assistant arm since #184 — a private `StreamingAssistantBody(content, modifier)` derives `revealedLength` via `produceState(initialValue = 0, key1 = content)` (one character per 20 ms tick → 50 chars/sec, restarts on `content` change so Phase 4's token-batch growth replays from 0) and `caretVisible` via `produceState(initialValue = true, key1 = Unit)` (toggle every 500 ms → conventional terminal caret cadence), appends `STREAMING_CARET_GLYPH = "▎"` (U+258E LEFT ONE QUARTER BLOCK, matching Figma node `16:56`) to the revealed prefix when the caret is on, and passes the result through [`MarkdownText`](features/markdown-text.md) unchanged so the caret rides as inline text — inherits `LocalContentColor.current` (pinned to `onSurface` by the surrounding `CompositionLocalProvider`) and lands pixel-adjacent to the last revealed character regardless of which block kind the prefix ends in; a sibling pure-rendering composable `StreamingAssistantBodyView(revealedText, caretVisible, modifier)` exists so previews can pin a deterministic half-revealed mid-state without depending on coroutine timing (Android Studio `@Preview` renders `produceState`-driven composables but the captured snapshot of fast animations is non-deterministic). The static `MarkdownText(message.content, ...)` path is preserved when `isStreaming = false` — zero animation cost for the typical historical-message case. `FakeConversationRepository`'s seed pipeline carries the flag through `SeedMessage`/`seedMsg(..., isStreaming = false)` so existing call sites are unchanged; the last assistant message of `seed-channel-pyrycode-mobile`'s current session opts in with substantive multi-paragraph markdown content so the animation is visible end-to-end in the running app and in the extended `MessageBubbleMarkdownPreviewBody`. Markdown rendering (#129 — landed; routes the assistant variant through [`MarkdownText`](features/markdown-text.md), see [ADR 0002](decisions/0002-markdown-renderer-library.md)), code-block styling (#130), streaming caret + animation (#184 — landed; see [`codebase/184.md`](codebase/184.md)), and tool-role rendering (#131) extend this component additively, with #131 either replacing the `Role.Tool -> Unit` arm with a real composable call or filtering tool messages out of `state.messages` upstream in `ThreadScreen`; #185 layers auto-scroll behaviour on top of #184's `isStreaming` contract at the `ThreadScreen` layer without changing `MessageBubble`'s API. Public API is a single `Message` parameter (not pre-split `(text, isUser)`) because the eventual call site is `items(state.messages) { MessageBubble(it) }` and consumers don't pre-split the list by role; **no `onClick`** (message-level interactions like long-press for "Copy" / "Reply" aren't in AC and aren't in Figma `16:8` — design-for-hypothetical-future per the project rules), **no `SelectionContainer`** (if long-press-to-copy lands later, the right place is a screen-level wrap of the `LazyColumn` body, not per-bubble selection); RTL handled automatically by `Arrangement.End`, `Modifier.fillMaxWidth()`, and `RoundedCornerShape(bottomEnd = …)` (all direction-aware — in RTL locales the user bubble pins to the left, the corner asymmetry flips correctly). Reads only M3 default slots `colorScheme.primaryContainer` / `colorScheme.onPrimaryContainer` / `colorScheme.onSurface` and `typography.bodyMedium` — no custom slots, no theme overrides, no new strings, no `gradle/libs.versions.toml` edits inside `MessageBubble.kt` itself (the assistant-variant pipeline picks up `org.jetbrains:markdown` transitively via [`MarkdownText`](features/markdown-text.md) since #129). Four `@Preview`s at the bottom of `MessageBubble.kt`, all `widthDp = 412`. Pair one (`MessageBubbleLightPreview` / `MessageBubbleDarkPreview`) renders a shared `MessageBubblePreviewSequence()` of four messages (User → Assistant → User → Assistant; the last assistant message is a long multi-sentence string to exercise multi-line wrap) inside `Surface { Column(Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) { … } }` so the assistant variant's transparent background renders against the theme's surface color (matching the eventual `LazyColumn` runtime) and the user bubble has a 16dp column inset to pin against instead of the raw preview viewport edge — `darkTheme = false` and `darkTheme = true` + `uiMode = Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES` respectively, pairing shape mirrors [`DiscussionPreviewRow`](features/discussion-preview-row.md)'s previews; since #129 this pair also serves as the no-markdown-syntax verification path through [`MarkdownText`](features/markdown-text.md). Pair two added in #129 (`MessageBubbleMarkdownLightPreview` / `MessageBubbleMarkdownDarkPreview`) renders a single assistant-variant `MessageBubble` whose content is a file-private `MARKDOWN_PREVIEW_FIXTURE` raw triple-quoted string exercising every supported markdown element (h1/h2/h3, bold, italic, inline code, link to `https://pyryco.de`, unordered list, ordered list, blockquote, fenced Kotlin code block) — satisfies #129's AC6. `previewMessage(role, content)` is a file-private factory fixing `id = "preview-${role.name}"`, `sessionId = "preview-session"`, `timestamp = Clock.System.now()`, `isStreaming = false` to keep the four call sites in the sequence focused on the varying fields. No preview for the `Role.Tool` arm (would be a blank surface with no visual signal). No unit tests and no instrumented Compose UI tests — the composable is pure rendering with no logic beyond the `when` over `Role` (branch coverage visible in the previews) and the codebase has no `androidTest` infrastructure for thread/list components beyond existing fixtures, same precedent as #126's `ThreadScreen`; visual verification is via the two `@Preview`s rendering in Android Studio plus `./gradlew assembleDebug` + `./gradlew lint` passing. Ships the component only — `ThreadScreen.kt` is **not** touched (architect spec's scope contract: "component only, no wiring"); the eventual `items(state.messages) { MessageBubble(it) }` wiring and the corresponding `ThreadUiState` widening to sealed `Loading | Loaded(messages) | Error` wait for the ticket that consumes #127's fake `ConversationRepository.observeMessages(conversationId)` repository — the prediction in [`thread-screen.md`](features/thread-screen.md) that #128 itself would do that widening was wrong; the widening moves to whichever ticket wires the consumer.
- [ConnectionBanner](features/connection-banner.md) — stateless one-line banner primitive (#200) for the connection state, mounted at `ui/conversations/components/ConnectionBanner.kt`; `@Composable fun ConnectionBanner(state: ConnectionState, onRetry: () -> Unit, modifier: Modifier = Modifier)` resolves a per-state quartet (`text`, `containerColor`, `contentColor`, `onClick`) in a single exhaustive `when` over the four [`ConnectionState`](features/connection-state.md) cases from #196 — `Connected` short-circuits with an early `return` (zero composition, AC-1 zero-height baseline); `Connecting` → `"Connecting…"` on `surfaceContainerHigh` with `onClick = {}`; `Reconnecting(secondsRemaining)` → `"Reconnecting in ${state.secondsRemaining}s"` (lowercase `s`, no space) on `surfaceContainerHigh` with `onClick = {}`; `Offline` → `"Offline — tap to retry"` (literal U+2014 em-dash, not `--`) on `errorContainer` with `onClick = onRetry` — then delegates to a private stateless `ConnectionBannerContent(text, containerColor, contentColor, onClick, modifier)` that renders `Surface(modifier.fillMaxWidth().clickable(onClick = onClick), color = containerColor, contentColor = contentColor) { Text(text, padding(BannerHorizontalPadding = 16.dp, BannerVerticalPadding = 12.dp), bodyMedium) }`. The public / `*Content` split mirrors [`ToolCallRow`](features/tool-call-row.md)'s shape but its inner takes already-resolved primitives (`String` + `Color` + lambda) rather than the unresolved sealed value, because the projection is one-shot and trivial — the `when` lives in the public entry so the `Connected` short-circuit and the smart-cast `state.secondsRemaining` read both happen there. `Modifier.clickable` is attached **unconditionally** on the `Surface`; the "ignore taps in `Connecting` / `Reconnecting`" contract is routed through the no-op `{}` lambda assigned in the same `when` that resolves the copy, not through `Modifier.clickable(enabled = state is Offline, ...)` or a conditional modifier chain — same uniform-`Surface`-shape idiom as [`ToolCallRow`](features/tool-call-row.md), keeps the M3 default ripple consistent across all three visible states (ripple fires in `Connecting` / `Reconnecting` too, even though the lambda is a no-op; acceptable — ripple is the tap-registered signal, the *behaviour* of "nothing happens" is the contract; swap is `enabled = state is Offline` if a future a11y review wants to suppress). Text colour inherits from `LocalContentColor.current` via `Surface(contentColor = contentColor)` — **no `Text(color = ...)` argument** — the M3-idiomatic inheritance vehicle, keeps the `on*` slot at the `Surface` boundary so future leading icons or secondary `Text`s wouldn't each need their own `tint` / `color` override (same `LocalContentColor`-inheritance shape as [`MarkdownText`](features/markdown-text.md)'s assistant-variant path). Copy strings are Kotlin literals — **no `stringResource` indirection** (same convention as [`ToolCallRow`](features/tool-call-row.md)'s `"Input"` / `"Output"` labels and [`MessageBubble`](features/message-bubble.md)'s preview fixture; first-localisation-pass migrates everything together rather than ad-hoc per-component). Exhaustive `when` over the sealed type carries no `else ->` arm — a future fifth `ConnectionState` case is flagged at compile time as a hard decision, same posture as [`MessageBubble`](features/message-bubble.md)'s `Role`-fanout. Pure stateless projection — no `remember`, no `LaunchedEffect`, no flow collection, no coroutines; `ConnectionState` is a stable sealed class (`data object` × 3 + `data class` × 1), and `onRetry` stability is the caller's responsibility (project convention is to declare event lambdas as plain literals at the destination block; lifting to `remember` is reserved for hot recomposition sites). Two file-private spacing `val`s at the top of the file (`BannerVerticalPadding = 12.dp`, `BannerHorizontalPadding = 16.dp`), **no cross-file dedup** with [`MessageBubble`](features/message-bubble.md) / [`ToolCallRow`](features/tool-call-row.md)'s constants — these are layout-specific to the banner and don't share intent with the message-row tokens; promote to a shared `Spacing.kt` peer file only when a third component declares the same value. No new dependencies, no `gradle/libs.versions.toml` edits, no `strings.xml` entries, **no other Kotlin files touched** — `MessageBubble.kt`, `ThreadScreen.kt`, `ThreadViewModel.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `AppModule.kt` are all unchanged; the scope contract from the architect spec ("component only, no wiring") held without compromise. Two `@Preview`s (light + dark, `widthDp = 412`, `showBackground = true`, dark adds `uiMode = Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES`, annotation shape copied from `ToolCallRow.kt`) each delegating to a shared private `ConnectionBannerPreviewMatrix()` that calls the **public** `ConnectionBanner(state, onRetry = {})` four times — once per `ConnectionState` case — wrapped in `PyrycodeMobileTheme(darkTheme = ...) { Surface { ... } }`; calling the public entry (not `ConnectionBannerContent`) is deliberate because the preview is the regression artifact for the `Connected` short-circuit and must exercise the public surface — divergence from [`ToolCallRow`](features/tool-call-row.md) / [#184](codebase/184.md)'s `StreamingAssistantBody`, where previews drove the stateless inner because the public-entry branching there was preview-incompatible state machinery (`rememberSaveable` / `produceState`) rather than load-bearing behaviour. No unit tests, no Compose UI tests — same precedent as [#128](codebase/128.md) / [#129](codebase/129.md) / [#130](codebase/130.md) / [#131](codebase/131.md) / [#184](codebase/184.md); the component is pure projection of a 4-case sealed type and the only branching is the exhaustively-typed public-entry `when`. The tap-gating contract (`onRetry` fires only in `Offline`) is asserted in the follow-up wiring slice's integration test, where the surrounding `ThreadScreen` and a fake `ConnectionStateSource` exist; pre-staging that test here would couple this slice to a not-yet-existing consumer. Edge cases: `Reconnecting(secondsRemaining = 0)` renders `"Reconnecting in 0s"` verbatim (no "Reconnecting now…" special-case — model boundary owns the transition to `Connecting` / `Connected` when the countdown elapses); negative `secondsRemaining` also renders verbatim because the model deliberately omits `require(secondsRemaining >= 0)` per [Connection state](features/connection-state.md) — clamp at the model boundary if Phase 4 ever emits negatives, not here. The follow-up wiring slice landed in [#201](codebase/201.md) (split from #197): the banner mounts between `ThreadTopAppBar` and the message `LazyColumn` inside a `Column` wrapper on [`ThreadScreen`](features/thread-screen.md); `ThreadViewModel` exposes `connectionState: StateFlow<ConnectionState>` (separate flow, not folded into `ThreadUiState`) via `connectionStateSource.observe().stateIn(viewModelScope, WhileSubscribed(5_000), ConnectionState.Connected)` and a non-suspend `fun retry()` that launches `source.retry()` on `viewModelScope`; the destination block at `MainActivity.kt:197-211` adds one `collectAsStateWithLifecycle()` and binds `onRetry = vm::retry`. The screen's `LazyColumn` carries `.fillMaxWidth().weight(1f)` (not `.fillMaxSize()` — `weight` inside a `Column` ignores `fillMaxSize`). No `ThreadEvent` fold yet — `connectionState` is *state* and stays flat even when the fold eventually lands. `AnimatedVisibility` show/hide animation, copy localisation, and a11y `onClickLabel = "Retry connection"` / `role = Role.Button` on the `Offline` arm remain deferred open follow-ups; Phase-4 swap of `FakeConnectionStateSource` to a real Ktor-backed source is a one-line binding edit at `AppModule.kt:31`.
- [SessionBoundaryDelimiter](features/session-boundary-delimiter.md) — stateless composable (#135) at `ui/conversations/components/SessionBoundaryDelimiter.kt` rendering a single [`ThreadItem.SessionBoundary`](features/conversation-repository.md) marker as a horizontal-rule delimiter for the future `ThreadScreen` `LazyColumn` switch (`MessageItem` → [`MessageBubble`](features/message-bubble.md); `SessionBoundary` → this); `@Composable fun SessionBoundaryDelimiter(boundary: ThreadItem.SessionBoundary, modifier: Modifier = Modifier)` resolves `TimeZone.currentSystemDefault()` + default `Locale` + `LocalUriHandler.current` and delegates to file-internal `SessionBoundaryDelimiterContent(boundary, uriHandler, modifier)` whose body is a `Column(fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = DelimiterVerticalPadding = 12.dp))` containing `HorizontalDivider(color = outlineVariant)` → 8.dp spacer → centered `Text(boundaryLabel(...), labelSmall, onSurfaceVariant)` → 4.dp spacer → `FlowRow(Arrangement.Center) { Text("Claude doesn't remember messages above this line. Install a memory plugin to preserve context. ", bodySmall, onSurfaceVariant) ; TextButton(onClick = { uriHandler.openUri(MEMORY_PLUGIN_DOCS_URL) }, contentPadding = PaddingValues(0.dp)) { Text("Install") } }`. Reason → label mapping in `internal fun boundaryLabel(boundary, timeZone, locale): String` over `BoundaryReason` (defined in #3, authored on the marker in #192): `Clear` → `"New session — $time"`; `WorkspaceChange` → `"Workspace changed to ${boundary.workspaceCwd!!} — $time"` (`!!` is intentional fail-fast on the repository invariant "`workspaceCwd != null` iff `reason == WorkspaceChange`" asserted in `FakeConversationRepositoryTest`; pinned by a `@Test(expected = NullPointerException::class)`); `IdleEvict` → `"Idle session ended — $time"`; em-dash is the literal U+2014 character, not `--`. `$time` from `internal fun formatShortTime(instant, timeZone, locale): String` via the `kotlinx-datetime` → `java.time` interop bridge `instant.toLocalDateTime(timeZone).time.toJavaLocalTime()` + `DateTimeFormatter.ofLocalizedTime(FormatStyle.SHORT).withLocale(locale).format(localTime)` — renders `"16:32"` on 24-hour locales (`de_DE` / `en_GB`) and `"4:32 PM"` on 12-hour (`en_US`); min SDK 33 means `java.time` is on the platform classpath, no `coreLibraryDesugaring`. Did **not** import `formatRelativeTime` from `RelativeTime.kt` despite the Figma sample showing relative time (`— 2 hours ago`) — AC's locale short time is canonical. `Install` URL is a file-internal `const val MEMORY_PLUGIN_DOCS_URL = "https://pyryco.de/docs/memory-plugins"` — placeholder (Phase 1–2) consistent with the `pyryco.de` host already used in `MessageBubble.kt`'s `MARKDOWN_PREVIEW_FIXTURE`; Phase 3+ deep-link swap is a one-line constant change. `internal` visibility (not `private`) is load-bearing so the androidTest can assert the forwarded URL against the constant rather than hard-coding a literal that would silently desync from a future swap. The injectable-`UriHandler` worker split mirrors [`MarkdownText`](features/markdown-text.md)'s pattern (#129/#131) and lets the androidTest exercise the click → URL side effect via `CompositionLocalProvider(LocalUriHandler provides capturing) { SessionBoundaryDelimiter(...) }` rather than mocking the local. Four file-private spacing `val`s (`DelimiterVerticalPadding = 12.dp`, `DelimiterHorizontalPadding = 16.dp`, `LabelTopSpacing = 8.dp`, `ExplanationTopSpacing = 4.dp`) — no raw `.dp` literal inside the worker; the 4.dp gap between label and explanation isn't in the architect spec, added during preview when the two crowded. `FlowRow` (not `Row`) is the load-bearing layout choice so the long sentence wraps cleanly at narrow widths and the button reflows with the trailing text — `@OptIn(ExperimentalLayoutApi::class)` on the worker only. Three `@Preview` entries (not two) — Light 412dp, Dark 412dp, **Narrow 320dp**; the Narrow preview is the visual regression artifact for `FlowRow` wrap behaviour at typical narrow phone widths, a class of bug not caught by ComposeRule and not visible at 412dp. All three delegate to the same `SessionBoundaryDelimiterPreviewMatrix()` that stacks the three boundary fixtures (`Clear` with `workspaceCwd = null`, `WorkspaceChange` with `workspaceCwd = "~/Workspace/Projects/KitchenClaw"` matching the Figma example, `IdleEvict` with `workspaceCwd = null`) in a `Column(Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp))` wrapped in `PyrycodeMobileTheme { Surface { ... } }`; all use a fixed `Instant.parse("2026-05-17T14:32:00Z")` so previews are deterministic. Tests: 5 JVM unit tests at `src/test/.../SessionBoundaryDelimiterTest.kt` (three label scenarios against `TimeZone.of("Europe/Berlin")` + `Locale.GERMANY` asserting exact `"… — 16:32"` strings since UTC 14:32 → Berlin 16:32; one `WorkspaceChange + workspaceCwd = null → NullPointerException` fail-fast pin; one `formatShortTime × Locale.US` scenario asserting `contains("4:32") && contains("PM")` separately rather than a literal `"4:32 PM"` because recent JDK CLDR emits a narrow no-break space U+202F before `PM`) + 5 androidTest scenarios at `src/androidTest/.../SessionBoundaryDelimiterTest.kt` (explanatory sentence + `Install` button present for `Clear`; each reason renders its label prefix via `hasText(..., substring = true)`; tapping `Install` appends exactly `MEMORY_PLUGIN_DOCS_URL` to a captured list — each test installs a per-test capturing `UriHandler` via the bottom-of-file `setContentWithCapturingUriHandler(boundary, opened)` helper). Stateless — no `remember`, no `LaunchedEffect`, no coroutine scope; pure `ThreadItem.SessionBoundary` → rendered Column. No new dependencies, no string resources, no theme tokens added. Above-delimiter opacity treatment ([#136](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/136)), wiring into `ThreadScreen`'s `LazyColumn` (currently `items(emptyList<Unit>())`), and a11y review on the `Install` affordance (`onClickLabel = "Open memory plugin docs"` is the obvious hook) remain deferred open follow-ups.
- [WorkspacePickerSheet](features/workspace-picker-sheet.md) — first **sheet** composable (#212) in `ui/conversations/components/` (siblings are all rows + bubbles); stateless renderer for Figma `20:2`, mounted at `WorkspacePickerSheet.kt`. `internal @Composable fun WorkspacePickerSheet(recent: List<String>, onPick: (String) -> Unit, onCreateNew: () -> Unit, onDismiss: () -> Unit, modifier: Modifier = Modifier, sheetState: SheetState = rememberModalBottomSheetState(skipPartiallyExpanded = true))` is the `ModalBottomSheet(onDismissRequest = onDismiss, sheetState = sheetState)` shell that delegates into an `internal` sibling `WorkspacePickerSheetContent(recent, onPick, onCreateNew, onDismiss)` — the modal-shell-plus-`*Content`-body split is the canonical M3-sheet shape established here, load-bearing for two reasons: (a) the modal scrim + animation machinery don't render in the IDE preview pane so previews target `*Content` directly inside a `PyrycodeMobileTheme { Surface(color = surfaceContainerLow, contentColor = onSurface) { Column(padding(top = 12.dp)) { … } } }` wrap that simulates the runtime sheet's container colour + drag-handle gap, and (b) `androidx.compose.ui.test.createComposeRule()` attaches to a synthetic `ComposeView` host with no `Activity`, so the five Compose UI tests in `androidTest/.../WorkspacePickerSheetTest.kt` also target `WorkspacePickerSheetContent` (same `M3-sample` pattern Material 3 docs use). `sheetState` is defaulted but **exposed** so the host (#207) can drive `sheetState.hide()` for animated-close *before* invoking `onDismiss`; `skipPartiallyExpanded = true` because this is a short content list, not a half-sheet. Body is a single `Column(fillMaxWidth)` with five children — `TitleRow(onClose = onDismiss)` (a `Row` with `Text("Choose workspace", titleLarge, onSurface)` weighted `1f` + trailing `IconButton(Icons.Filled.Close, contentDescription = "Close", tint = onSurfaceVariant)`, padding `start = 16, end = 4, top = 4, bottom = 12` per Figma `20:5`), an optional Recent section gated on `recent.isNotEmpty()` (the AC empty-state contract — empty `recent` suppresses the `"Recent"` header entirely, no placeholder text), `SectionHeader("Other")` always rendered, `CreateNewRow(onClick = onCreateNew)` always rendered, trailing `Spacer(height = 24.dp)` per Figma `pb=24`. `SectionHeader(text)` is `Text(text, labelLarge, onSurfaceVariant, padding(start = 24, end = 16, top = 12, bottom = 4))`. `RecentRow(path, onClick)` is `Row(fillMaxWidth.clickable(onClick).padding(horizontal = 16, vertical = 12), arrangement = spacedBy(16), verticalAlignment = CenterVertically)` with leading `Icons.Outlined.Folder` 24dp tinted `primary` + `contentDescription = null` (the adjacent `Text` carries the semantic content — TalkBack hears the path, not `"path, folder icon"`) and a weighted `Text(path, bodyMedium, FontFamily.Monospace, color = onSurface, maxLines = 1, TextOverflow.Ellipsis)`. `CreateNewRow(onClick)` shares the row shape with leading `Icons.Outlined.CreateNewFolder` and `Text("Create new folder under pyry-workspace…", bodyLarge, onSurface)` — the trailing ellipsis is the literal U+2026 character (not three ASCII dots), same typographic-codepoint posture as [`ConnectionBanner`](features/connection-banner.md)'s U+2014 em-dash. All rows attach `Modifier.clickable` unconditionally — same uniform-row idiom as [`ConnectionBanner`](features/connection-banner.md) / [`ToolCallRow`](features/tool-call-row.md), the tap target is the whole row, no chevron / trailing affordance. Three deliberate Figma deviations carried from the architect spec — (1) `RecentRow` renders the **full path** as the only mono primary line and **drops the `"Last used X ago"` subtitle** (AC's `"basename primary + full-path subtitle"` proposal was a speculation with explicit `"(architect to confirm)"` license; Figma `20:2` actually shows the full path as primary + relative time as subtitle, and the [`recentWorkspaces()`](features/conversation-repository.md) data surface from [#209](codebase/209.md) carries paths only with no per-entry timestamp — implementing the subtitle would require reshaping the repository contract to `Flow<List<RecentWorkspaceEntry>>` with `(path, lastUsedAt: Instant)`, out of scope for an S-sized stateless-composable ticket; recoverable later); (2) **no `default` chip on any row** (Figma shows a `secondary-container`-backed `default` pill on the scratch row, scratch is filtered out upstream by the host per the Technical Notes so the chip never renders and is not implemented); (3) inline `"Close"` `contentDescription` literal (no `R.string` extraction per Technical Notes — first-localisation-pass migrates all literals together). Colour + typography map 1:1 from Figma `Schemes/*` → `MaterialTheme.colorScheme.*` and Figma `Static/*` → `MaterialTheme.typography.*` (sheet bg `surfaceContainerLow`, title + row text `onSurface`, headers + close-icon tint `onSurfaceVariant`, leading folder icons `primary`; title `titleLarge`, section headers `labelLarge`, Create label `bodyLarge`, recent paths `bodyMedium` + `FontFamily.Monospace`) — no hex, no custom text styles. `Icons.Outlined.Folder` + `Icons.Outlined.CreateNewFolder` are inherited from the `material-icons-extended` dependency added by [#131](codebase/131.md), no new `gradle/libs.versions.toml` or `app/build.gradle.kts` edit. `SAMPLE_RECENTS` (four entries) is a file-private preview fixture, **not added to `FakeConversationRepository`** — same self-contained-fixtures posture as [#131](codebase/131.md)'s `Preview*ToolCall` constants, keeps preview-only data out of the runtime seed pipeline. Two `@Preview`s (light + dark, `widthDp = 412`, `showBackground = true`, dark adds `uiMode = Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES`) both target `WorkspacePickerSheetContent`; no empty-recents preview because the empty-state branch is locked down by the `recent_header_is_omitted_when_recent_is_empty` Compose UI test instead. Five Compose UI tests in `app/src/androidTest/java/de/pyryco/mobile/ui/conversations/components/WorkspacePickerSheetTest.kt` (`createComposeRule()` + `AndroidJUnit4`, matching `ConversationAvatarTest.kt` — no MockK, no Turbine, no shared fixtures; `assertEquals` + `var invoked` lambda-capture for callback verification, `hasText` / `hasContentDescription` lookups for assertion targets) cover the four interactive paths plus the empty-state branch: `recent_rows_render_full_paths`, `recent_row_tap_invokes_onPick_with_full_path` (asserts the **exact full-path string** round-trips, the regression artifact for Deviation 1's `"tap → full path, not basename"` contract), `recent_header_is_omitted_when_recent_is_empty` (`assertDoesNotExist` on `"Recent"`, `"Other"` + Create label still display), `create_new_row_invokes_onCreateNew_on_tap`, `close_icon_invokes_onDismiss_on_tap` (the inline `contentDescription = "Close"` doubles as the test selector, locking down Deviation 3). The Compose tests deviated *up* from the architect spec, which said `"not required by this ticket's AC"` and described the test shape as guidance for a defensive follow-up — shipped here because the four interactive paths plus the empty-state branch are the entire surface and the `androidTest/` infrastructure was already in place. **No other files touched** — no `MessageBubble.kt` / `ThreadScreen.kt` / `ChannelListScreen.kt` / `ChannelListViewModel.kt` / `ConversationRepository.kt` / `FakeConversationRepository.kt` / `AppModule.kt` / `gradle/libs.versions.toml` / `app/build.gradle.kts` / `strings.xml` edit; the stateless-composable contract is exactly the lever that keeps the test-fake cascade off the UI slice — when the host (#207) owns the data collection, the renderer owns nothing but the visual contract, and pre-binding here would have pulled the cascade into this ticket without changing the eventual host's surface area. **`internal` visibility on both the shell and the `*Content`** — `internal` is the smallest step that satisfies (a) cross-package use within the module by the eventual host in #207 and (b) symbol visibility from the `androidTest/` source set; `private` would hide `*Content` from the test, `public` would leak the API surface outside the module. Downstream open: **host (#207)** wires this sheet into the Channel List FAB + "Save as channel" flow, collects [`recentWorkspaces()`](features/conversation-repository.md) and dedupes / filters scratch + empty string per the [#209](codebase/209.md) contract, owns sheet-visibility + animated-close, and owns the Create Folder follow-up dialog (Figma `19:44`) that calls [`createWorkspaceFolder`](features/conversation-repository.md) from [#210](codebase/210.md) on submit and feeds the returned path back through `onPick`; `"Last used X ago"` subtitle follow-up requires the `Flow<List<RecentWorkspaceEntry>>` data-shape change first; filesystem browser entry point is deliberately deferred for MVP; re-introducing the `default` chip if a future design re-surfaces scratch wires in as a `SuggestionChip` child of `RecentRow` gated on a per-entry boolean (data-shape change is upstream-first); literal-strings localisation is out of scope until the first `<plurals>` / non-English `strings.xml` pass.
- [ChannelInfoSheet](features/channel-info-sheet.md) — second **sheet** composable (#217) in `ui/conversations/components/`, mounted at `ChannelInfoSheet.kt`; stateless renderer for Figma `20:48` (the channel-info bottom sheet — conversation-name title row + close icon, **About** section with five label/value rows (workspace path, created, last activity, session count, message count), **Memory** section with empty-state `None` text + `Install` button or right-aligned plugin list when populated, **Actions** 2×2 grid of `FilledTonalButton` cells (Rename | Change workspace, Archive | Delete), low-opacity `Channel ID: <id>` footer with long-press copy via `LocalClipboardManager` + `LocalHapticFeedback`). Carries the [`WorkspacePickerSheet`](features/workspace-picker-sheet.md) (#212) shell + `*Content` body split verbatim — public `internal @Composable fun ChannelInfoSheet(model: ChannelInfoUiModel, onRename, onChangeWorkspace, onArchive, onDelete, onInstallMemoryPlugin, onDismiss, modifier, sheetState = rememberModalBottomSheetState(skipPartiallyExpanded = true))` is the `ModalBottomSheet(onDismissRequest = onDismiss, sheetState = sheetState)` shell that delegates into an `internal` sibling `ChannelInfoSheetContent(model, six callbacks)` body so previews can target the content directly (the modal scrim doesn't render in the IDE preview pane) and any future Compose UI tests can attach via `createComposeRule()` without an `Activity`. A new colocated `internal data class ChannelInfoUiModel(conversationName, workspacePath, createdLabel, lastActivityLabel, sessionCount, messageCount, memoryPlugins: List<String>, channelId)` carries the eight pre-rendered fields — first explicit `*UiModel` projection in this package; the host computes `createdLabel`/`lastActivityLabel` via a shared relative-time helper (the one [`ConversationRow`](features/conversation-row.md) uses is the natural reuse target), the composable does **no** time / number / locale formatting. Body is a single `Column(fillMaxWidth)` with seven children: `TitleRow(title = model.conversationName, onClose = onDismiss)` (`Row(start = 16, end = 4, top = 4, bottom = 12)` with weighted `Text(titleLarge, onSurface)` + trailing `IconButton(Icons.Filled.Close, contentDescription = "Close", tint = onSurfaceVariant)`), `SectionHeader("About")` + five `AboutRow`s, `SectionHeader("Memory")` + `MemoryRow(plugins, onInstall)`, `SectionHeader("Actions")` + `ActionsGrid(four callbacks)`, `Footer(channelId)`, trailing `Spacer(height = 24.dp)` (same pattern as `WorkspacePickerSheetContent`'s trailing spacer for the M3 inset gap). `AboutRow(label, value, valueIsPath = false)` branches internally: default branch is `Row(padding(horizontal = 16, vertical = 8))` with leading `Text(label, bodyLarge, onSurface)` + weighted `Spacer` + trailing `Text(value, bodyMedium, onSurfaceVariant, TextAlign.End)`; `valueIsPath = true` drops the spacer and gives the right `Text` `Modifier.weight(1f)` so `TextOverflow.StartEllipsis` (Compose 1.7+, resolved by `composeBom = 2026.02.01`) has a constraint to bite against — start-ellipsis keeps the rightmost path segment visible (`…/KitchenClaw` on narrow widths). The workspace value renders in `bodyMedium.copy(FontFamily.Monospace, fontSize = 12.sp, lineHeight = 16.sp)`. `MemoryRow` outer `Row(SpaceBetween, padding(horizontal = 16, vertical = 8))` with leading `Text("Memory plugins", bodyLarge, onSurface)`; right branches on `plugins.isEmpty()` — empty path is `Row(spacedBy(8.dp))` with `Text("None", bodyMedium, onSurfaceVariant)` + `TextButton(onClick = onInstall, contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp))` containing `Icon(Icons.Outlined.Add, contentDescription = null, size = 16.dp)` + `Spacer(width = 4.dp)` + `Text("Install", labelLarge)` (M3 `TextButton` defaults content color to `primary`), non-empty path is `Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.End)` of plugin name `Text`s with no install affordance (AC defers "install additional plugins from this sheet" to a follow-up; the host might surface it via the existing callback once a real plugin source is wired). `ActionsGrid` is `Column(spacedBy(8.dp), padding(start = 16, end = 16, top = 4))` over two `Row(spacedBy(8.dp))`s of `ActionCell(label, onClick, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))` — weight at the call site, not inside the cell, canonical row-cell shape. `ActionCell` is `FilledTonalButton(onClick, modifier) { Text(label, labelLarge, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis) }` with M3 defaults intact (`secondaryContainer` / `onSecondaryContainer` content, pill-ish shape matching Figma `20:96`'s `rounded-[100px]` intent; the `ButtonDefaults.ContentPadding` vs Figma `py-[10px]` delta is accepted as visually negligible). **No destructive-emphasis styling on Delete in this ticket** — AC explicitly defers the destructive treatment; the confirmation dialog the host wraps around the callback is where destructive intent surfaces, not the cell itself. `Footer(channelId)` is `@OptIn(ExperimentalFoundationApi::class)` `Box(padding(start = 24, end = 24, top = 24).combinedClickable(interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }, indication = null, onClick = {}, onLongClick = { clipboard.setText(AnnotatedString(channelId)); haptics.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.LongPress) }))` wrapping `Text("Channel ID: $channelId", bodySmall.copy(FontFamily.Monospace, 11.sp, 16.sp), onSurfaceVariant, Modifier.alpha(0.5f))` — `indication = null` so the low-opacity text doesn't paint a high-contrast ripple over itself, `onClick = {}` intentional (AC restricts copy to long-press), `LocalHapticFeedback.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.LongPress)` is the user-facing confirmation that the copy fired (no toast / snackbar wired here yet). `LocalClipboardManager` over `LocalClipboard` is the spec's explicit direction — AC says "via the platform `ClipboardManager` (resolved through `LocalClipboardManager`)" and the deprecation warning the newer API generates is expected and accepted; migration is a follow-up the day the legacy API is removed, not now. Long-press shape is the [`ConversationRow.kt:7,44`](features/conversation-row.md) `combinedClickable(onClick, onLongClick)` precedent verbatim. Two `@Preview`s, one per theme (`ChannelInfoSheetPreview` light, `ChannelInfoSheetDarkPreview` dark + `uiMode = Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES`), both `widthDp = 412`, `showBackground = true`, each wrapping `ChannelInfoSheetContent(model = SAMPLE_MODEL, ...)` in the canonical `PyrycodeMobileTheme { Surface(color = surfaceContainerLow, contentColor = onSurface) { Column(padding(PaddingValues(top = 12.dp))) { … } } }` wrap that simulates the runtime `ModalBottomSheet` container colour + drag-handle gap; `SAMPLE_MODEL` is file-private with AC's literal values (`conversationName = "kitchenclaw refactor"`, `workspacePath = "~/Workspace/Projects/KitchenClaw"`, `createdLabel = "3 weeks ago"`, `lastActivityLabel = "2 hours ago"`, `sessionCount = 12`, `messageCount = 347`, `memoryPlugins = emptyList()`, `channelId = "ch_a8f3c2d1e9b7"`). No tests in this ticket — AC requires only the preview; the eventual host ticket lands `androidTest/` Compose UI tests modelled on [`WorkspacePickerSheetTest.kt`](codebase/212.md) targeting the `*Content` seam. No new dependencies — `material-icons-extended` already on the classpath via [#131](codebase/131.md) covers `Icons.Outlined.Add` / `Icons.Filled.Close` at zero marginal cost, `TextOverflow.StartEllipsis` ships with the current Compose BOM. No `gradle/libs.versions.toml` / `app/build.gradle.kts` / `AppModule.kt` / `strings.xml` edit. The follow-up host ticket (open, split from [#144](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/144) alongside this one) wires this sheet into every channel-info entry point (Channel List long-press, Thread overflow → Channel info…), owns sheet-visibility `Boolean`, computes the per-channel projection that produces a `ChannelInfoUiModel` (workspace path, session and message counts from the repository, pre-rendered relative-time labels), and routes each callback to the appropriate downstream surface (rename dialog, [`WorkspacePicker`](features/workspace-picker.md), archive confirmation, delete confirmation, memory-plugin install flow); probable shape mirrors [`WorkspacePicker`](codebase/220.md): public wrapper + `internal *Internal` seam for `androidTest/` reachability.
- [StatusSheet](features/status-sheet.md) — third **sheet** composable (shell + Model section [#254](codebase/254.md); Effort + YOLO sections [#229](codebase/229.md); Context window section [#230](codebase/230.md)) in `ui/conversations/components/`, mounted at `StatusSheet.kt`; stateless renderer for the Run-configuration bottom sheet (Figma `20:100`) shown when the user taps [`ThreadStatusRow`](features/thread-status-row.md) — `"Run configuration"` title row + trailing close icon, four sections in order: **Model** (`labelLarge`/`onSurfaceVariant` "Model" header at `start = 24, end = 16, top = 12, bottom = 4`) over a `Column(Modifier.selectableGroup())` of three `ModelRow`s in `Model.entries` source order (`OPUS_4_7`/`SONNET_4_6`/`HAIKU_4_5`), each `Row(fillMaxWidth.selectable(selected, onClick, role = Role.RadioButton).padding(horizontal = 16, vertical = 4), verticalAlignment = Alignment.Top)` carrying `RadioButton(selected, onClick = null)` + `Spacer(width = 12.dp)` + `Column { Text(model.label(), bodyLarge, onSurface); Text(model.description(), bodySmall, onSurfaceVariant) }` where `Model.description()` is a file-private extension mapping `OPUS_4_7 → "best for complex work"` / `SONNET_4_6 → "faster, cheaper"` / `HAIKU_4_5 → "fastest"` from Figma nodes `20:117/122/127`; **Effort** ([#229](codebase/229.md)) — `"Effort"` `SectionHeader` over `EffortChipRow(selectedEffort, onEffortSelected)` = `Row(fillMaxWidth.selectableGroup().padding(horizontal = 16, vertical = 4), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp))` iterating `Effort.entries` (`LOW, MEDIUM, HIGH, XHIGH, MAX`) into five `FilterChip(selected = effort == selectedEffort, onClick = { onEffortSelected(effort) }, label = { Text(effort.label()) })` with no leading/trailing icon; `FilterChipDefaults.filterChipColors()` + `filterChipBorder()` match Figma `20:131–140` exactly (selected → `secondaryContainer` bg + `onSecondaryContainer` label, unselected → 1dp `outline` border + transparent bg + `onSurfaceVariant` label); labels resolve via the public [`Effort.label()`](features/app-preferences.md#design-decision-defer-label-extensions-on-data-layer-enums) extension at `ui/settings/EffortPickerDialog.kt:77` widened from `internal` to public in #229 when this slice became the second consumer alongside the [#233](codebase/233.md) `EffortPickerDialog`; and **YOLO mode** ([#229](codebase/229.md)) — `"YOLO mode"` `SectionHeader` over `YoloRow(enabled, onToggled)` = `Row(fillMaxWidth.toggleable(value = enabled, role = Role.Switch, onValueChange = onToggled).padding(horizontal = 16, vertical = 8), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically)` with a weighted `Column { Text("Auto-accept tool calls", bodyLarge, onSurface); Text("Claude runs commands without asking for confirmation. Use carefully.", bodySmall, onSurfaceVariant) }` on the left, a 16.dp `Spacer`, and `Switch(checked = enabled, onCheckedChange = null)` on the right — same canonical M3 shape family as `ModelRow`'s `selectable + RadioButton(onClick = null)`, swapping `selectable`/`Role.RadioButton` for `toggleable`/`Role.Switch`; `onCheckedChange = null` on the inner `Switch` is required (passing the handler to both the row's `toggleable` and `Switch.onCheckedChange` causes the callback to fire twice). [#230](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/230) Context window appends the fourth (and likely final) section into the same `StatusSheetContent` `Column`; **no `Section` abstraction is introduced** in #254 or #229 per the architecture spec (revisit when the third repeated shape genuinely emerges, i.e. when #230 lands — four sections cross the threshold). Carries the [`WorkspacePickerSheet`](features/workspace-picker-sheet.md) (#212) / [`ChannelInfoSheet`](features/channel-info-sheet.md) (#217) shell + `*Content` body split verbatim — public `@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class) fun StatusSheet(selectedModel: Model, onModelSelected: (Model) -> Unit, selectedEffort: Effort, onEffortSelected: (Effort) -> Unit, yoloEnabled: Boolean, onYoloToggled: (Boolean) -> Unit, onDismiss: () -> Unit, modifier: Modifier = Modifier, sheetState: SheetState = rememberModalBottomSheetState(skipPartiallyExpanded = true))` (the four `selectedEffort` / `onEffortSelected` / `yoloEnabled` / `onYoloToggled` params appended in #229 after `onModelSelected`) is the `ModalBottomSheet(onDismissRequest = onDismiss, modifier = modifier, sheetState = sheetState)` shell delegating into `internal fun StatusSheetContent(selectedModel, onModelSelected, selectedEffort, onEffortSelected, yoloEnabled, onYoloToggled, onDismiss)` so previews + `createComposeRule()` tests target the content directly (the modal scrim/animation doesn't render in the IDE preview pane or attach without an `Activity`). Private file-local `TitleRow(title, onClose)` (`Row(fillMaxWidth.padding(start = 16, end = 4, top = 4, bottom = 12))` with weighted `Text(titleLarge, onSurface)` + trailing `IconButton(Icons.Filled.Close, contentDescription = "Close", tint = onSurfaceVariant)`) and `SectionHeader(text)` helpers are re-declared in this file — three sheets now carry identical `TitleRow` + `SectionHeader` privates byte-identical in shape (extraction candidate documented in [`254.md`](codebase/254.md); deferred until the first divergent-shape sheet lands). `Modifier.selectable(role = Role.RadioButton)` on the **row** parent + `RadioButton(onClick = null)` on the inner control is the canonical M3 shape — the parent owns click + a11y semantics so the entire row (≈56dp+ with description) is the touch target rather than the 20dp radio circle; `onClick = null` on the radio is required (passing the handler to both fires the callback twice). `Alignment.Top` on the row (not `CenterVertically`) keeps the radio anchored to the title baseline when the description wraps. Three `@Preview`s in the file, one per `Model` value (`StatusSheetOpusPreview`, `StatusSheetSonnetPreview`, `StatusSheetHaikuPreview`), all light-mode + `widthDp = 412` + `showBackground = true`, each wrapping `StatusSheetContent(...)` in the canonical `PyrycodeMobileTheme { Surface(color = surfaceContainerLow, contentColor = onSurface) { Column(padding(PaddingValues(top = 12.dp))) { ... } } }` shape that simulates the runtime `ModalBottomSheet` container + drag-handle gap.
