Five Compose UI tests in `androidTest/.../StatusSheetTest.kt` (`createComposeRule()` + `PyrycodeMobileTheme` wrapper rendering `StatusSheetContent`): renders-all-three-rows-and-descriptions, tap-sonnet-invokes-callback, tap-haiku-invokes-callback, tap-close-invokes-onDismiss, selection-state via `onNode(isSelectable() and hasAnyDescendant(hasText("Sonnet 4.6"))).assertIsSelected()` — `hasAnyDescendant` is required because `selectable` lives on the parent `Row`, not on the `Text` node. **Hosted in [`ThreadScreen`](features/thread-screen.md) as a `Scaffold` sibling** (alongside [`WorkspacePicker`](features/workspace-picker.md)) gated on `var sheetVisible by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }` (UI-local state hoisted to the screen — sheet visibility has no business meaning the VM needs to react to, so it stays off `ThreadUiState`; `rememberSaveable` survives rotation at zero VM-surface cost — same idiom as `WorkspacePicker.kt:45`); the row's `onExpandClick = { sheetVisible = true }` is wired internally; the sheet's `onModelSelected = { model -> onModelSelected(model); sheetVisible = false }` auto-closes on selection (M3 modal-bottom-sheet convention for action-completing sheets; will be revisited when [#229](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/229)/[#230](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/230) make the sheet a multi-setting surface); `onDismiss = { sheetVisible = false }` covers the scrim-tap / back-press / drag-down paths (provided by `ModalBottomSheet`). The gate is a plain `if (sheetVisible) { StatusSheet(...) }`, **not** `AnimatedVisibility` — `ModalBottomSheet` runs its own enter/exit animation; wrapping in `AnimatedVisibility` would double-animate. `ThreadScreen`'s `onExpandClick: () -> Unit = {}` parameter (introduced by [#145](codebase/145.md) as a hoisted placeholder) was **deleted** in #254 — no remaining external consumer (the four pre-existing previews never passed it; `MainActivity` was the only production caller). A new `onModelSelected: (Model) -> Unit = {}` parameter takes its slot, defaulted so the previews keep compiling; `MainActivity`'s `CONVERSATION_THREAD` destination block drops the `// TODO(#146): open Status Sheet` placeholder + the `onExpandClick = {},` line and gains `onModelSelected = vm::onModelSelected` (the [#253](codebase/253.md)-shipped synchronous `MutableStateFlow<Model?>.value` write on `ThreadViewModel.modelOverride` — does **not** mutate `AppPreferences.defaultModel`). The sheet's title literal (`"Run configuration"`) + section header (`"Model"`) + three description strings (`"best for complex work"` / `"faster, cheaper"` / `"fastest"`) + `"Close"` content description are inline Kotlin literals — no `strings.xml` extraction yet, mirroring `WorkspacePickerSheet` + `ChannelInfoSheet`'s posture; first-localisation pass migrates all three sheets together. Future Settings model-picker (the still-pending `AppPreferences.setDefaultModel(...)` write-site noted in [`app-preferences`](features/app-preferences.md)) re-uses `Model.label()` but **not** the file-private `Model.description()` extension — the descriptions are framed for the per-conversation override flow, not a global default. No new dependencies, no new Koin / `gradle/libs.versions.toml` / `AppModule.kt` / `ThreadViewModel.kt` edits — this slice is pure UI on top of the [#253](codebase/253.md) state plumbing.
- [CreateFolderDialog](features/create-folder-dialog.md) — stateless M3 `AlertDialog` (#213) sibling to [`WorkspacePickerSheet`](features/workspace-picker-sheet.md) in `ui/conversations/components/`, mounted at `CreateFolderDialog.kt`; renders Figma `19:44` for the workspace-folder-name prompt the host (#207, open) will mount after the picker's `onCreateNew` fires. `internal @Composable fun CreateFolderDialog(onCreate: (String) -> Unit, onDismiss: () -> Unit, modifier: Modifier = Modifier)` is a one-line delegate that calls a file-private `CreateFolderDialogInternal(initialValue = TextFieldValue("", TextRange.Zero), ...)` seam — the public-wrapper-plus-private-`*Internal`-seam split is the load-bearing architectural move here, and it exists for **one reason only**: to let the three `@Preview`s (`CreateFolderDialogEmptyPreview` light, `CreateFolderDialogFilledPreview` light `"my-new-workspace"` + `TextRange(0, name.length)`, `CreateFolderDialogFilledDarkPreview` dark) demonstrate both AC #3 states (enabled Create with non-empty name, disabled Create with empty name) without exposing an `initialValue` parameter on the public API. Different shape from [`WorkspacePickerSheet`](features/workspace-picker-sheet.md)'s shell/`*Content` split — there the inner exists because `ModalBottomSheet` is structurally untestable from `createComposeRule()` and unrenderable in the IDE preview pane; here the inner exists purely to inject preview state, the public composable is what tests target. Body is a standard M3 `AlertDialog` with four slots: `onDismissRequest = onDismiss` (handles outside-tap + back-press via the M3 defaults `dismissOnBackPress = true` / `dismissOnClickOutside = true`, no `DialogProperties` override), `title = { Text("Create workspace", style = headlineSmall) }` (style set explicitly to document the visual contract even though M3 defaults it), `text = { OutlinedTextField(value = fieldValue, onValueChange = { fieldValue = it }, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().focusRequester(focusRequester), label = { Text("What should this workspace be called?") }, singleLine = true, keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(capitalization = KeyboardCapitalization.None, imeAction = ImeAction.Done), keyboardActions = KeyboardActions(onDone = { if (isCreateEnabled) onCreate(trimmedName) })) }` — `label` (not `placeholder`) because Figma shows the prompt at the top of the outlined frame which is the M3 floating-label position, `KeyboardCapitalization.None` because folder paths are lowercase by convention, `singleLine = true` so Enter submits via `onDone`, `confirmButton = { TextButton(onClick = { onCreate(trimmedName) }, enabled = isCreateEnabled) { Text("Create") } }`, `dismissButton = { TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) { Text("Cancel") } }` — slot ordering renders Cancel then Create matching Figma `19:49`. Four pieces of local UI state inside the seam: `focusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }`, `fieldValue by remember { mutableStateOf(initialValue) }` (`TextFieldValue` not `String` — preserved so the seam can pre-select text via `TextRange(0, name.length)` for the Filled previews, and so a future public `initialName` parameter would be a one-line internal change), `trimmedName by remember { derivedStateOf { fieldValue.text.trim() } }`, `isCreateEnabled by remember { derivedStateOf { trimmedName.isNotEmpty() } }`; `LaunchedEffect(Unit) { focusRequester.requestFocus() }` pulls keyboard focus to the input once per dialog instance (no `LaunchedEffect(focusRequester)` indirection needed — the requester reference is stable from `remember`, Compose's `LaunchedEffect` runs after the composition tree is committed, focus work is main-thread-only which `LaunchedEffect` defaults to). Two submit paths through the same `onCreate(trimmedName)` binding gated on `isCreateEnabled`: the visible `Create` button (`enabled = isCreateEnabled` produces the disabled state per AC #2) and the keyboard `ImeAction.Done` (guarded by `if (isCreateEnabled)`, no-op when blank — no callback, no dismiss); Cancel / outside-tap / back-press all route to `onDismiss` with no data emission. Three deliberate deviations from AC carried from the architect spec — (1) **visible title is `"Create workspace"`, file / symbol stays `CreateFolderDialog`** (Figma `19:45` locks the visible label, AC #1 locks the file path / symbol; the product-naming inconsistency vs. [`WorkspacePickerSheet`](features/workspace-picker-sheet.md)'s `"Create new folder under pyry-workspace…"` row label is recorded as a PO-level decision); (2) **`OutlinedTextField` uses `label` not `placeholder`** because Figma shows the prompt above the value-text region inside the outlined frame which is the floating-label position, not the placeholder position; (3) **`ImeAction.Done` keyboard submit added beyond AC #2** because a one-field form without a keyboard-submit binding is a UX regression vs. M3 conventions and the behaviour is identical to the button tap — one-line revert if the PO disagrees. Colour + typography map 1:1 from Figma `Schemes/*` → `MaterialTheme.colorScheme.*` (dialog bg `surfaceContainerHigh`, title + input text `onSurface`, floating label `onSurfaceVariant`, field border `outline`, button labels `primary`) and Figma `Static/*` → `MaterialTheme.typography.*` (title `headlineSmall` *set explicitly on the title `Text`*, floating label `labelMedium` / input value `bodyLarge` / button labels `labelLarge` all come for free from M3 defaults — only the title is in source) — no hex, no custom text styles. Previews wrap `CreateFolderDialogInternal` directly inside `PyrycodeMobileTheme(darkTheme = …) { ... }` with **no intermediate `Surface` wrapper** because `AlertDialog`'s window machinery (scrim, surface, elevation) renders inside the IDE preview pane — unlike `ModalBottomSheet`, which doesn't (asymmetry that explains why [`WorkspacePickerSheet`](features/workspace-picker-sheet.md)'s previews need the `Surface(color = surfaceContainerLow)` wrap and this dialog's previews don't). Six Compose UI tests in `app/src/androidTest/java/de/pyryco/mobile/ui/conversations/components/CreateFolderDialogTest.kt` (`createComposeRule()` + `AndroidJUnit4`, matching the [`WorkspacePickerSheetTest.kt`](codebase/212.md) precedent — no MockK, no Turbine, no shared fixtures; `assertEquals` + `assertNull` for callback-shape verification, `hasText` / `hasSetTextAction` lookups for selectors, `performTextInput` for typing, `performClick` for tapping) — **all target the public `CreateFolderDialog`** (the seam stays file-private, tests anchor to the public API so refactoring the seam doesn't rewrite tests): `title_and_label_and_buttons_render` (locks down the Deviation-1 visible-title string `"Create workspace"` and the Deviation-2 floating-label string as test selectors), `create_button_disabled_when_input_blank` (AC #2 baseline), `create_button_enabled_after_non_blank_input` (disabled → enabled transition through `onValueChange` + `derivedStateOf` + `TextButton.enabled`), `create_button_stays_disabled_when_input_is_whitespace_only` (the regression artifact for the **`trim()`-then-`isNotEmpty()`** contract — `String.isNotEmpty()` would treat four spaces as valid; the test catches the silent simplification from `derivedStateOf { fieldValue.text.trim().isNotEmpty() }` to `derivedStateOf { fieldValue.text.isNotEmpty() }`), `tapping_create_invokes_onCreate_with_trimmed_text` (type `"  my-workspace  "`, assert `created == "my-workspace"` — the trimmed-text round-trip end-to-end), `tapping_cancel_invokes_onDismiss_without_data` (`dismissed == 1` AND `assertNull(created)` — the negative `assertNull` doing the load-bearing assertion for "Cancel emits no data"). Three AC #2 paths intentionally NOT covered by Compose tests because they are structurally unreachable from `createComposeRule()` (no `Activity`, no scrim window, no hardware back dispatch, no software IME): outside-tap (`dismissOnClickOutside = true`), back-press (`dismissOnBackPress = true`), keyboard-`Done` submit — same wall [`WorkspacePickerSheetTest.kt`](codebase/212.md) hit for outside-tap; the regression artifact for these three is source-level wiring (`onDismissRequest = onDismiss`, one line) not a test. The Compose tests deviated *up* from the architect spec, which said `"not required by AC"` and described the test shape as guidance for a defensive follow-up — shipped here because the surface is six callback / state paths and the `androidTest/` infrastructure was already in place ([`WorkspacePickerSheetTest.kt`](codebase/212.md) precedent). **No other files touched** — no `WorkspacePickerSheet.kt` / `MessageBubble.kt` / `ThreadScreen.kt` / `ChannelListScreen.kt` / `ChannelListViewModel.kt` / `ConversationRepository.kt` / `FakeConversationRepository.kt` / `AppModule.kt` / `gradle/libs.versions.toml` / `app/build.gradle.kts` / `strings.xml` edit; **no new dependencies** (`AlertDialog`, `OutlinedTextField`, `TextButton`, `FocusRequester`, `TextFieldValue`, `KeyboardOptions`, `KeyboardActions`, `ImeAction`, `KeyboardCapitalization`, `TextRange` all ship in the existing `androidx.compose.material3` / `ui` / `foundation` artifacts). **`internal` visibility** is the smallest step that satisfies (a) cross-package use by the eventual host in #207 and (b) symbol visibility from `androidTest/`; **`private` visibility on the `*Internal` seam** is deliberate — making it `internal` would tempt future code to reach into it for runtime use, which is exactly what the AC-1 "stateless contract" forbids; tests reach the dialog via the public API only. Downstream open: **host (#207)** owns the picker-sheet ↔ dialog visibility coordination, the picker → dialog handoff (the picker's `onCreateNew` opens the dialog), the repository binding (calls [`createWorkspaceFolder`](features/conversation-repository.md) from [#210](codebase/210.md) on `onCreate`), and the error contract for repo-level failures (collision, IO — natural extension is an `errorMessage: String? = null` parameter feeding an `isError` / `supportingText` slot on the `OutlinedTextField`, deferred until the host's error contract is exercised end-to-end); on success the host feeds the returned path back through the picker's `onPick`; localisation of the five inline literals (`"Create workspace"`, `"What should this workspace be called?"`, `"Cancel"`, `"Create"`) is deferred to the codebase's first `strings.xml` pass alongside [`WorkspacePickerSheet`](features/workspace-picker-sheet.md)'s literals; the file-name vs. visible-label vs. picker-row product-naming inconsistency (Deviation 1) is a PO-level decision.
- [WorkspacePicker](features/workspace-picker.md) — stateful **host** composable (#220) in `ui/conversations/components/`, mounted at `WorkspacePicker.kt`; composes [`WorkspacePickerSheet`](features/workspace-picker-sheet.md) (#212) + [`CreateFolderDialog`](features/create-folder-dialog.md) (#213) and binds them to [`ConversationRepository`](features/conversation-repository.md), so each entry-point screen only contributes a hoisted `visible: Boolean` and an `onPicked: (String) -> Unit` callback. `@Composable fun WorkspacePicker(visible: Boolean, onPicked: (String) -> Unit, onDismiss: () -> Unit, modifier: Modifier = Modifier)` is the package's external contract — **`public` (no `internal`)**, a deliberate visibility step from the two children (both `internal`) because consumer screens in sibling packages (`ui/conversations/list/`, `ui/conversations/thread/`) call this host directly; the children are only consumed inside the package via this host. The public body is a one-line gate: `if (!visible) return` (zero work when invisible — no Koin lookup, no flow subscription, no `remember`, no coroutine scope allocation; mounting the host as a permanent sibling of a screen body costs nothing when its `visible` flag is false), then `val repository = koinInject<ConversationRepository>()` (same Koin-from-composable convention as `MainActivity.kt`), then delegates to an `internal` testing seam `WorkspacePickerInternal(repository, onPicked, onDismiss, modifier)` that takes the repo as a parameter so the three `androidTest/` cases can pass a real `FakeConversationRepository()` instance without bootstrapping Koin. **The seam is `internal`, not `private`** — a deliberate visibility step from [`CreateFolderDialogInternal`](features/create-folder-dialog.md) (file-private, [#213](codebase/213.md)) because `private` would hide the seam from the `androidTest/` source set, and the test source set crosses source-set boundaries within the same module so `internal` (module-wide visibility) is the smallest step that satisfies "reachable from `androidTest/` but invisible outside the module." Three distinct seam shapes now coexist in `ui/conversations/components/` — don't conflate: (1) [`WorkspacePickerSheet`](features/workspace-picker-sheet.md)'s shell-plus-`*Content` split exists because `ModalBottomSheet` is structurally unrenderable in the IDE preview pane and untestable from `createComposeRule()`; (2) [`CreateFolderDialog`](features/create-folder-dialog.md)'s public-plus-file-private-`*Internal` seam exists purely so previews can inject `initialValue` without widening the public API (tests target the public composable); (3) this host's public-plus-`internal`-`*Internal` seam exists so `androidTest/` can bypass Koin (tests target the **seam**, not the public composable, because the seam is precisely what skips the Koin lookup the test wants to avoid). The seam body has three pieces of state: `val recents by repository.recentWorkspaces().collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = emptyList())` (project's established Flow→Compose-state shape from `MainActivity.kt`, lifecycle-aware unlike `.collectAsState()`), `var showCreateDialog by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }` (survives rotation within a host instance; when `visible` flips false → true the host is re-mounted so saveable state is fresh — dialog starts closed, desired), `val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()` (the on-submit launch scope, cancels on composition exit which encodes "consumer-driven dismiss mid-write cancels the in-flight write silently — `onPicked` is NOT called"; Phase 0 unreachable because `FakeConversationRepository.createWorkspaceFolder` is non-suspending, Phase 4 will revisit if at-least-once write semantics matter, scope choice would change to `applicationScope`). Then a two-child composition tree — `WorkspacePickerSheet(recent = recents, onPick = onPicked, onCreateNew = { showCreateDialog = true }, onDismiss = onDismiss, modifier = modifier)` followed by a conditional `if (showCreateDialog) { CreateFolderDialog(onCreate = { name -> showCreateDialog = false; scope.launch { val path = repository.createWorkspaceFolder(name); onPicked(path) } }, onDismiss = { showCreateDialog = false }) }`. Source order is sheet first, dialog second; M3 `AlertDialog` and `ModalBottomSheet` each manage their own `Popup` / `Window` so source order does not affect Z-order — dialog's window is always above the sheet's window, ordered for readability. Behaviour: tapping a recent row → `onPick = onPicked` direct pass-through (no wrapping, no transformation, the sheet emits `onPick(path)` exactly when the user taps a row); tapping "Create new folder under pyry-workspace…" → `onCreateNew = { showCreateDialog = true }` flips the dialog flag; submitting the dialog → flips `showCreateDialog = false` **synchronously before** launching the coroutine so the dialog leaves composition and a second `onCreate` invocation from the same dialog instance is **structurally impossible** (the AC-2 "exactly once per submit" guarantee is structural, not stochastic — Compose's per-click debouncing rules out the recomposition-fires-twice failure mode, and the synchronous flag flip rules out the rapid-double-tap failure mode by unmounting the button before the second tap can land), then awaits `repository.createWorkspaceFolder(name)` and forwards the returned path to `onPicked(path)`; cancelling the dialog (Cancel button / outside-tap / back-press) → `onDismiss = { showCreateDialog = false }` flips **only** the dialog flag, the sheet stays visible, the host's outer `onDismiss` is NOT called (the load-bearing test `cancelling_create_dialog_keeps_sheet_visible_and_does_not_invoke_host_onDismiss` distinguishes "dialog dismissed" from "host dismissed" — two flag-flips that share the surface verb but route differently); dismissing the **sheet** itself (close icon / scrim tap / drag-down / back-press while the dialog is not open) → `onDismiss = onDismiss` invokes the consumer's outer callback, consumer typically flips its `visible` flag to false, the host re-renders with `visible == false`, returns nothing, the sheet's exit animation runs as `ModalBottomSheet` leaves composition (no `sheetState.hide()` coordination in this ticket — if a future ticket needs the animated-close-then-state-flip ordering the sheet already exposes `sheetState`, the host would gain a `LaunchedEffect` to drive the hide). `onPicked` is a **single callback for both "recent tap" and "newly-created path"** — consumers cannot distinguish; the three near-term consumers (Channel List FAB long-press, [#137](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/137)'s empty-thread chip, [#208](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/208)'s thread overflow item) take the same next action regardless. **`visible: Boolean` is a hoisted prop, not internal state** — each consumer owns its own visibility flag on its `UiState`, the established `pendingPromotion`-style pattern from [`DiscussionListViewModel`](features/discussion-list-viewmodel.md) (and `SaveAsChannelDialog` visibility from [#142](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/142)). The host has no internal "show" state because consumers need to drive visibility from their own state machines (coordinating with other dialogs, reacting to navigation events, reflecting ViewModel decisions). `Modifier` is forwarded **only to the sheet**, not duplicated on the dialog — the dialog is a transient overlay, attaching the same modifier to both would double-tag the test tree; routing the modifier to the structurally primary surface (always present when the host is visible) is the right shape. `modifier`'s default is `Modifier`; tests don't currently use it but it's there for consumer test-tag / semantics attachment. Error handling: `recentWorkspaces()` cannot fail in Phase 0 (`MutableStateFlow`-backed fake); Phase 4 terminal-error emissions would be silently swallowed by `collectAsStateWithLifecycle` (sheet renders last good list) — acceptable for Phase 0, Phase 4 will wire a `catch { }` if surfacing matters. `createWorkspaceFolder`'s `IllegalArgumentException` from blank-name validation is structurally unreachable (`CreateFolderDialog`'s `enabled = isCreateEnabled` gates the Create button on `trimmedName.isNotEmpty()`, the dialog never invokes `onCreate("")`); if it ever fires, the app should crash loudly as a contract violation upstream — the host does NOT catch. Phase 4 network/server-side failures are explicitly out of scope; no `try { … } catch { … }` wrap today, snackbar / error-state slot is the natural extension when the real backend ships. Three Compose UI tests in `app/src/androidTest/java/de/pyryco/mobile/ui/conversations/components/WorkspacePickerTest.kt` (`createComposeRule()` + `AndroidJUnit4`, matching [`WorkspacePickerSheetTest.kt`](codebase/212.md) + [`CreateFolderDialogTest.kt`](codebase/213.md) precedents — no MockK, no Turbine, no shared fixtures; `assertEquals` for callback verification, `hasText` / `hasSetTextAction` / `onNodeWithText` lookups, `performTextInput` + `performClick` for interaction), **all target the `WorkspacePickerInternal` seam** (not the public composable — the seam is precisely the testing-seam shape that skips Koin) with a real `FakeConversationRepository()` instance (not a hand-rolled mock — production-grade fake gives end-to-end fidelity for the host ↔ repo wiring, and `FakeConversationRepositoryTest` already covers the underlying surfaces): `dialog_submit_calls_createWorkspaceFolder_exactly_once_and_forwards_returned_path_to_onPicked` (tap Create-new row, type `"my-workspace"`, tap Create, `composeTestRule.waitForIdle()`; asserts `picked == listOf("pyry-workspace/my-workspace")` for single-invocation + path-shape, **and** re-asserts via `runBlocking { repo.recentWorkspaces().first().first() } == "pyry-workspace/my-workspace"` that the bump landed at position 0 — end-to-end wiring validation, the load-bearing assertion that catches "host called `onPicked` with the right path but did NOT call `createWorkspaceFolder`"; `waitForIdle()` is a forward-compat defence — the current fake is synchronous so it would pass without, but a future suspending `createWorkspaceFolder` would race the assertion), `cancelling_create_dialog_keeps_sheet_visible_and_does_not_invoke_host_onDismiss` (tap Create-new row, tap Cancel; asserts `dismissed == 0`, sheet's `"Choose workspace"` title still `assertIsDisplayed`, dialog's `"Create workspace"` title `assertDoesNotExist` — the load-bearing test for the two-flag-flips contract), `tapping_a_recent_row_invokes_onPicked_with_that_rows_path` (tap `"~/Workspace/pyrycode-mobile"` which is present in `FakeConversationRepository`'s default seed via the `Discussion 3` conversation, assert `picked == listOf("~/Workspace/pyrycode-mobile")` — wiring is one-line `onPick = onPicked` pass-through, the test locks the AC-5c contract; anchoring on seed data is a coupling worth flagging if a future ticket reshapes the fake's seed). Tests intentionally NOT included — **empty-recents rendering** (covered at the sheet layer by `WorkspacePickerSheetTest.recent_header_is_omitted_when_recent_is_empty`, host adds no logic), **sheet dismiss invokes host `onDismiss`** (`onDismiss = onDismiss` one-line pass-through, underlying close-icon wiring covered by `WorkspacePickerSheetTest.close_icon_invokes_onDismiss_on_tap`), **`visible == false` renders nothing** (the seam has no `visible` parameter; trivially-correct early-return guard in the public composable doesn't need a test). **No previews** — the host's behaviour is sequencing, not pixels; both children carry their own previews ([`WorkspacePickerSheet`](features/workspace-picker-sheet.md) two, [`CreateFolderDialog`](features/create-folder-dialog.md) three), a host preview would duplicate what the children's previews already show and would need a fake-repo wrap. **No other files touched** — no `WorkspacePickerSheet.kt` / `CreateFolderDialog.kt` / `ChannelListScreen.kt` / `ChannelListViewModel.kt` / `ThreadScreen.kt` / `ThreadViewModel.kt` / `ConversationRepository.kt` / `FakeConversationRepository.kt` / `AppModule.kt` / `gradle/libs.versions.toml` / `app/build.gradle.kts` / `strings.xml` edit; **no new dependencies** (`koinInject`, `collectAsStateWithLifecycle`, `rememberCoroutineScope`, `rememberSaveable`, `launch` all ship in the existing `org.koin:koin-androidx-compose` / `androidx.lifecycle:lifecycle-runtime-compose` / `androidx.compose.runtime` / `kotlinx.coroutines` artifacts); **no new DI registrations** (`single { FakeConversationRepository() } bind ConversationRepository::class` is already registered in [`AppModule`](features/dependency-injection.md) by [#209](codebase/209.md) / [#210](codebase/210.md), the host's `koinInject<ConversationRepository>()` resolves to the same singleton ViewModels consume via constructor injection). The "host only, no consumer wiring" scope contract held — the three downstream entry points (Channel List FAB long-press, [#137](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/137), [#208](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/208)) are each separate tickets and edit their own screens / ViewModels. Downstream open: **Channel List FAB long-press (next ticket after #220)** edits `ChannelListScreen` + `ChannelListViewModel`, contributes a `pendingWorkspacePicker: Boolean` to the screen's `UiState`, routes `onPicked(path)` to a new-discussion seed action — depends on this ticket landing first; empty-thread workspace chip from [#137](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/137) and thread overflow `"Change workspace…"` item from [#208](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/208) consume this host once the chip / menu wiring lands in their own tickets; Phase 4 error UI for `createWorkspaceFolder` (snackbar / error-state slot for network or server-side failures) deferred until the real backend ships; animated-close coordination (`LaunchedEffect` driving `sheetState.hide()` before `onDismiss`) deferred until a consumer demands exit-animation completion before the consumer's state flip; literal-strings localisation deferred to the first `strings.xml` pass alongside the children's literals. **First consumer shipped in #221** — [`ChannelListScreen`](features/channel-list-screen.md)'s FAB long-press → picker → `repository.createDiscussion(workspace = path)` → navigate. The host's consumer-side conventions were established by this first wiring (see [`codebase/221.md`](codebase/221.md) for the full reasoning): (a) **three event variants** — open (`LongPressFab` / equivalent gesture), confirm-with-payload (`WorkspacePicked(workspace)`), and cancel (`WorkspacePickerDismissed`); the cancel path is distinct and must NOT trigger the side effect (mirrors #78's three-variant split); (b) **clear the visibility flag synchronously before the suspend** in the confirm arm — so the sheet's exit animation starts immediately instead of waiting for the repository call to complete (same discipline as #78's `confirmPromotion`); (c) **host as `Scaffold` sibling**, not inside the Scaffold's content lambda — `ModalBottomSheet`'s window-level scrim would otherwise conflate with the body layout; (d) **`pickerVisible` derived via exhaustive `when (state)` block** in the screen, not a `state is X && state.flag` chain — pays the verbosity for the compiler's completeness check across all sealed UiState variants. [#137](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/137)'s empty-thread chip and [#208](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/208)'s thread overflow item remain open consumers — both should follow these four conventions on `ThreadViewModel` — and post-#137 the empty-thread chip is now wired (the overflow remains the last open consumer).
- [WorkspaceChip](features/workspace-chip.md) — stateless M3 `AssistChip` (#137) rendered at the top of [`ThreadScreen`](features/thread-screen.md)'s body when the conversation is **`!isPromoted && !hasMessages`** (fresh, unpromoted discussion with zero `ThreadItem.MessageItem`s — `SessionBoundary`s don't count, so a workspace-change boundary triggered by the chip itself stays visible until a real message lands). `@Composable fun WorkspaceChip(workspaceLabel: String, onClick: () -> Unit, modifier: Modifier = Modifier)` — single `AssistChip(onClick, label = { Text("Workspace: $workspaceLabel (change)") }, leadingIcon = { Icon(Icons.Outlined.Folder, contentDescription = null) }, modifier)`, no styled spans, no new design tokens, no theme additions — M3 defaults carry it. `workspaceLabel` is already-derived: the ViewModel resolves `Conversation.cwd` → basename at the flow boundary via `private fun Conversation.workspaceLabel(): String = if (cwd.isEmpty() || cwd == DEFAULT_SCRATCH_CWD) "scratch" else cwd.substringAfterLast('/').ifEmpty { cwd }` (the `.ifEmpty { cwd }` guard returns the raw string when `substringAfterLast` returns empty on no-separator inputs like a bare `"X"`; the two-arm `cwd.isEmpty() || cwd == DEFAULT_SCRATCH_CWD` filter is **byte-identical** to `FakeConversationRepository.bumpWorkspace`'s no-bound-workspace check at the data layer — fresh discussions get `cwd = ""` from `createDiscussion(workspace = null)`, the seeded scratch-bound conversations get `DEFAULT_SCRATCH_CWD = "~/.pyrycode/scratch"`, both must collapse to `"scratch"` for the chip and the recents-bumping logic to agree). Tap routes via `onClick = onWorkspaceChipTapped` → `ThreadViewModel.onWorkspaceChipTapped()` → synchronous `pendingWorkspacePicker.value = true` flip on a private `MutableStateFlow<Boolean>`; that flag is combined into `state` alongside `observeConversations(All)` and `observeMessages(conversationId)`, surfacing as `ThreadUiState.workspacePickerVisible`; the screen reads that field and renders [`WorkspacePicker`](features/workspace-picker.md) as a **`Scaffold` sibling** (not inside the Scaffold's content slot — the picker's `ModalBottomSheet` lives in its own window, so source-order placement doesn't affect Z-order, mirroring `ChannelListScreen.kt:180-184` exactly from #221's canonical wiring). On `onPicked(path)` the VM clears the flag *then* launches `repository.changeWorkspace(conversationId, path)` on `viewModelScope` — the returned `Session` is **discarded**, the `Conversation.cwd` update propagates back via the `observeConversations` re-emission to the `combine` arm and `workspaceLabel` recomputes automatically (single source of truth, no risk of label/picker divergence); on `onDismiss` the VM clears the flag only, no repository call. **The chip's visibility predicate lives at the call site as `if (!state.isPromoted && !state.hasMessages)`, not as a derived `showWorkspaceChip` field on `ThreadUiState`** — two reasons: (a) the `&&` of two raw fields is trivially correct to inline; (b) [#208](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/208) (overflow entry point) and [#138](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/138) (empty-state copy) will each read the same raw signals — pre-fusing them would force re-derivation downstream. The call site lives in the existing `Column` slot between the [`ConnectionBanner`](features/connection-banner.md) and the `LazyColumn`, **not inside the `LazyColumn` as a header item**, because the chip doesn't participate in scroll, doesn't get recycled, doesn't need a `key`, and the current empty `items(emptyList<Unit>()) { }` body has no `item { … }` slot to add it to without a structural rewrite that #128's eventual `items(state.messages) { … }` would have to undo. `ThreadUiState` widened from 2 → 6 fields (`conversationId`, `displayName`, `isPromoted: Boolean = false`, `hasMessages: Boolean = false`, `workspaceLabel: String = "scratch"`, `workspacePickerVisible: Boolean = false`) — all four new fields default so the seven existing `ThreadViewModelTest` cases that compare full `ThreadUiState` equality (e.g. `state_initialValue_isConversationIdPlaceholderBeforeSubscription`) compile unchanged; the `stateIn(initialValue = …)` block still constructs `ThreadUiState(conversationId = …, displayName = …)` only. The defaults are conservative — `isPromoted = false, hasMessages = false, workspaceLabel = "scratch"` means the initial-value (pre-emission) frame renders the chip in its visible state with the safe sentinel, which is correct UX for the loading window of a brand-new discussion; wrong-default cases (channels resolving slowly) self-correct on the first emission, which the fake's synchronous `MutableStateFlow.map` chain delivers immediately. `ThreadViewModel.state` flow upgraded from `.map { … }` over `observeConversations(All)` to `combine(observeConversations(All), observeMessages(id), pendingWorkspacePicker) { conversations, items, pickerVisible -> … }`; `combine` over three independent signals was the right shape — collapsing `observeMessages` into the map by calling `observeMessages(id).first()` inside the lambda would have blocked the upstream. `hasMessages` is computed as `items.any { it is ThreadItem.MessageItem }`, **`MessageItem`-only** — a `ThreadItem.SessionBoundary` (including the one the chip itself triggers via `changeWorkspace`) does NOT flip the gate; the chip stays visible until the user sends a real message. `ThreadScreen` signature grew three flat `() -> Unit = {}` / `(String) -> Unit = {}` defaulted callbacks (`onWorkspaceChipTapped`, `onWorkspacePicked`, `onWorkspacePickerDismissed`); all three default to `{}` so the existing two `@Preview`s in `ThreadScreen.kt` and the [`PyryNavHost`](features/navigation.md) destination block (the latter binds method-refs `vm::onWorkspaceChipTapped` / `vm::onWorkspacePicked` / `vm::onWorkspacePickerDismissed`) work without breaking. **No `ThreadEvent` sealed fold yet** — the screen now carries five flat event arms (`onSendMessage`, `onRetry`, plus the three new ones — `onBack`/`onTitleClick`/`onOverflowClick` are pure-nav lambdas, not VM-owned events) and [#208](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/208) adds zero further callbacks (reuses the three picker handlers); the fold to a sealed envelope is deferred to whichever ticket lands the next VM-owned event beyond #208's reuse (likely #128's message-bubble interactions or #145's status row). Six new tests in `ThreadViewModelTest.kt` (now 18 total — 7 from #139, 2 from #188, 3 from #201, 6 here): three label-derivation cases lock the `workspaceLabel()` rule across empty / sentinel / arbitrary `cwd` (the empty-string case is the actual fresh-discussion default, so the test calls `repository.createDiscussion(workspace = null)` directly to exercise the real seam, not a synthetic `fixedRepo` stub); one consolidated case walks both raw chip-gate fields (`isPromoted`/`hasMessages`) across a seeded channel and a `sendMessage`-driven flip on a fresh discussion (locks the two signals that the chip's call-site `if (!isPromoted && !hasMessages)` consumes); two event-handler cases lock the picker-open / picker-write / picker-dismiss state transitions and assert the persisted `cwd` change via `repository.observeConversations(All).first().first { it.id == … }` (end-to-end wiring validation: the VM actually forwarded to the repository and the conversation actually mutated). No new test doubles; the existing `fixedRepo(...)` helper carries the bespoke-`cwd` cases. **AC5 satisfied by previews, not androidTest** — the `app/src/androidTest/java/de/pyryco/mobile/ui/conversations/thread/` directory does not exist (verified) and adding ComposeRule infrastructure for this ticket would be a 100+ LOC sidewinder the AC doesn't request; visual regression artifact is the Light/Dark preview pair in `WorkspaceChip.kt` (`workspaceLabel = "scratch"` in light to exercise the sentinel path, `workspaceLabel = "my-app"` in dark to exercise the basename path, both wrapping in `Surface { }` so the chip renders against the theme's surface color rather than the bare `showBackground = true` white/black). `MainActivity.kt` destination block at `MainActivity.kt:210-212` grew three method-reference bindings; no constructor change, no DI change. Edge cases: empty `conversationId` degenerate path is benign (the chip renders against `hasMessages = false, isPromoted = false, workspaceLabel = "scratch"` defaults; structurally unreachable in production but observable); picker dismiss mid-write does **not** cancel `changeWorkspace` (the launch continues on `viewModelScope`, the `cwd` still updates, the chip label re-renders if still visible — acceptable since the picker's single-invocation guarantee means each `onPicked` corresponds to exactly one in-flight write); `changeWorkspace` on an empty thread creates a `SessionBoundary` immediately (informative but arguably redundant in this UX path — open product/design question, would require either a new repository method or a behavior change on `changeWorkspace` to suppress, the chip's visibility logic already handles this correctly via the `MessageItem`-only filter); chip-to-message-list spacing in the empty state is unstyled — since [`#138`](codebase/138.md) the [`EmptyThreadState`](features/empty-thread-state.md) prompt fills the `weight(1f)` slot below and provides the visual anchor under the chip; no `AnimatedVisibility` on the gate (the chip pops in/out instantly when `hasMessages` flips); `changeWorkspace` failures are handled since [#561](codebase/561.md) — see below (still the `WorkspacePicker`'s own `createWorkspaceFolder` failures are unhandled, same posture as [`WorkspacePicker`](features/workspace-picker.md) from #220). The chip is the **second of three planned trigger points** for the picker host — first was [Channel List](features/channel-list-screen.md) FAB long-press from #221 (set the canonical `MutableStateFlow + combine + flat field + three handlers + Scaffold sibling` shape this slice mirrors), third will be [#208](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/208) Thread overflow "Change workspace…" (reuses `ThreadUiState.workspacePickerVisible` + `onWorkspacePicked` / `onWorkspacePickerDismissed` introduced here; adds only a fourth flat callback for the overflow menu item's tap that routes to the same `pendingWorkspacePicker.value = true` — do not duplicate state or handlers). Pattern-promotion candidate: `workspaceLabel()` / `bumpWorkspace`'s shared filter rule should promote to `Conversation.isScratchWorkspace(): Boolean` when the third caller arrives (likely #208). **[#560](codebase/560.md)/[#561](codebase/561.md)** wired `changeWorkspace` for real and gave it a failure surface: `onPicked(path)` now calls `sendChangeWorkspace(path)` (not the shared `launchGuardedRepoCall`) — success stays exactly as described above (chip re-labels off the projection re-emit, no VM fold); a caught `RelayErrorException` or `IllegalStateException` `trySend(Unit)`s on a new `changeWorkspaceErrors: Flow<Unit>`, rendered by `ThreadScreen` as a fixed-string snackbar (`change_workspace_failed`) — never the caught message. See [Thread overflow menu](features/thread-overflow-menu.md) for the sibling `Archive`/`ChangeWorkspace` surfacing pattern.
- [Thread status row](features/thread-status-row.md) — stateless single-`Row` composable (#145) at `ui/conversations/thread/ThreadStatusRow.kt` rendering `Model · effort · NN% used ▴` (monospace 12sp/16sp, `Modifier.alpha(0.85f)` for the Figma `opacity-85` treatment on `16:58`) inside [`ThreadScreen`](features/thread-screen.md)'s `bottomBar` slot, stacked above [`ThreadInputBar`](features/thread-input-bar.md) inside a wrapper `Column { ThreadStatusRow; ThreadInputBar }` (the `Modifier.imePadding()` already lives inside `ThreadInputBar` so only the input bar lifts with the soft keyboard, the status row stays anchored above). Signature `ThreadStatusRow(model: String, effort: String, tokenPercent: Int, onExpandClick: () -> Unit, modifier: Modifier = Modifier)`. Body is a single `Text` over an `AnnotatedString` with three `withStyle(SpanStyle(color = …))` spans (model → `onSurface`; ` · $effort · ` → `onSurfaceVariant`; `$tokenPercent% used` → threshold color) plus a trailing `Icon(Icons.Filled.KeyboardArrowUp, contentDescription = R.string.cd_thread_status_expand, tint = onSurfaceVariant, size = 14.dp)`; the whole `Row` carries `clickable(onExpandClick) + semantics { role = Role.Button }`. Token-% color via private file-local `@Composable tokenPercentColor(tokenPercent: Int): Color` — `coerceIn(0, 100)` clamp then `< 50 → onSurfaceVariant`, `< 95 → MaterialTheme.colorScheme.warning` (from [#119](codebase/119.md)), `else → error`. `ThreadUiState` widened to ten fields with three new defaults: originally (`model: String = "Opus 4.7"`, `effort: String = "high"`, `tokenPercent: Int = 0`); in [#253](codebase/253.md) `model: String` was retyped to `selectedModel: Model = Model.OPUS_4_7` and the screen-side `model =` arg switched to `state.selectedModel.label()` via a new co-located `fun Model.label(): String` extension in `data/preferences/Model.kt` (the row's own `model: String` parameter shape is preserved). `ThreadViewModel` populates them inside the existing `combine(...)` block — pre-#253 from `companion object { private const val STUB_MODEL = "Opus 4.7"; private const val STUB_EFFORT = "high"; private const val STUB_TOKEN_PERCENT = 73 }`; post-#253 `STUB_MODEL` is deleted and `selectedModel` flows in from a pre-combined `selectedModelFlow = combine(appPreferences.defaultModel, modelOverride) { default, override -> override ?: default }` folded as the **fourth** source into the main `combine`, where `modelOverride: MutableStateFlow<Model?>(null)` is mutated synchronously by a new `fun onModelSelected(model: Model)` (in-memory only, does **not** mutate `AppPreferences` — pinned by `onModelSelected_overridesPerConversationWithoutMutatingPreferences`). `ThreadViewModel`'s Koin binding at `AppModule.kt:36` gains a fourth `get()` (the existing `single { AppPreferences(get()) }` since [#11](codebase/11.md)). The Phase-4 swap point is now the two remaining `STUB_*` references for `effort` and `tokenPercent`. The 0-vs-73 asymmetry on `tokenPercent` between the data-class default and the post-subscription stub is intentional — pinned by a pair of `ThreadViewModelTest` methods renamed in #253 from `*Stub*` → `*Default*` (`state_initialValue_includesDefaultModelEffortAndTokenPercentDefaults`, `state_postSubscription_emitsDefaultModelEffortAndTokenPercent`); four new tests in #253 cover the `selectedModel` plumbing (`selectedModel_followsAppPreferencesDefault`, `selectedModel_reemitsWhenAppPreferencesDefaultChanges`, `onModelSelected_overridesPerConversationWithoutMutatingPreferences`, `onModelSelected_overrideWinsOverSubsequentDefaultChange`). The `ThreadViewModelTest` setup now uses a `TestScope.makeVm(...)` receiver function with a defaulted `prefs: AppPreferences = AppPreferences(newDataStore())` parameter backed by a `TemporaryFolder` rule and `tmp.newFile("prefs_${UUID.randomUUID()}.preferences_pb")` for per-VM prefs isolation; four pre-existing non-`runTest` tests were wrapped in trivial `runTest { }` to absorb the receiver-function shape. `ThreadScreen` grew one new defaulted parameter `onExpandClick: () -> Unit = {}` in #145 plumbing through to `MainActivity` as a no-op with a `// TODO(#146): open Status Sheet` marker; both the placeholder and the parameter itself were **deleted** in [#254](codebase/254.md) when the [`StatusSheet`](features/status-sheet.md) landed — `ThreadScreen` now hoists `var sheetVisible by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }` and binds `onExpandClick = { sheetVisible = true }` internally inside the `bottomBar` block, with a new `onModelSelected: (Model) -> Unit = {}` parameter taking the deleted slot on the signature and bound to `vm::onModelSelected` at `MainActivity`. Eight previews (4 × token-% values 20/60/88/97 × 2 themes) sweep all three threshold bands plus a within-band intermediate; the four existing `ThreadScreen` previews were updated to `tokenPercent = 73` so the row renders in the warning band in screen-level previews. One new string `R.string.cd_thread_status_expand = "Expand status details"` for the trailing-icon `contentDescription`.
- [EmptyThreadState](features/empty-thread-state.md) — stateless centered-prompt composable (#138) rendered into the [`ThreadScreen`](features/thread-screen.md) message-list area whenever `!state.hasMessages` (the same `MessageItem`-only signal added in #137 that gates the chip-presence half of [`WorkspaceChip`](features/workspace-chip.md)). `@Composable fun EmptyThreadState(modifier: Modifier = Modifier)` — single `Box(modifier, contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) { Text(stringResource(R.string.thread_empty_state), style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, textAlign = TextAlign.Center) }`. No icon, no secondary line, no `Modifier.padding` baked in — caller owns size + padding. New string `thread_empty_state = "Send a message to get started"` (joins the `<area>_empty…` family alongside `channel_list_empty`, `discussion_list_empty`, `archived_empty_channels` / `archived_empty_discussions`). Wired inside the `ThreadScreen` body `Column` (post-#201: `ConnectionBanner` → `WorkspaceChip` → list) by **turning the pre-#138 unconditional `LazyColumn` block into the `else` arm of `if (!state.hasMessages) EmptyThreadState(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().weight(1f).padding(horizontal = 24.dp)) else { val reversedItems = state.items.asReversed(); val cutoffChronologicalIndex = remember(state.items) { mostRecentSessionBoundaryIndex(state.items) }; LazyColumn(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().weight(1f), reverseLayout = true) { itemsIndexed(...) { ... } } }`** — the empty arm takes the same `weight(1f)` slot the list would have occupied, so the prompt fills exactly the space between the chip (or banner when the chip is suppressed) and the `ThreadInputBar` in the `bottomBar` slot. The `remember(state.items) { ... }` block (introduced in [#136](codebase/136.md)) moves into the `else` arm **only** — its key is `emptyList()` in the empty arm and the helper returns `-1` for that input, so computing it there is wasted work and would pre-allocate an unconsumed recomposition slot. **Predicate is `!state.hasMessages`, not `state.items.isEmpty()`** — two reasons: (a) symmetric with the chip's `!hasMessages` half (chip and prompt appear/disappear together on the same `combine`-arm re-emission); (b) the rare `SessionBoundary`-only edge case from #137 (user taps chip → `changeWorkspace` emits a `WorkspaceChange` boundary into the stream before any message lands) renders correctly as "still empty" with the prompt visible, rather than as a lonely [`SessionBoundaryDelimiter`](features/session-boundary-delimiter.md) floating above the input bar — matches AC #4 ("Hidden as soon as the message list has at least one entry"; a boundary is metadata, not an entry). The 24.dp horizontal inset is intentionally 8dp wider than the chip's `horizontal = 16.dp` (a centered single line wants more breathing room than a left-aligned chip on the 360dp portrait minimum) and is the one number to tune if a future preview-render review wants different breathing room — the rest of the layout is structurally locked by `weight(1f)` and `Alignment.Center`. **No widening of `ThreadUiState`, `ThreadViewModel`, the `combine` reducer, the destination block, the Koin binding, or any test** — `hasMessages` was already exposed by #137, the branch is a pure UI rendering decision against an existing field. **No new tests** — render-only change, the gate flips on the same `hasMessages` field the four #137 chip-gate tests already cover; AC #5 ("Preview shows the empty state") met by two new `@Preview`s in `EmptyThreadState.kt` (Light + Dark, both wrapping in `PyrycodeMobileTheme(darkTheme = …) { Surface { EmptyThreadState(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(400.dp).padding(horizontal = 24.dp)) } }`, dark with `uiMode = Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES`); previews pass a **finite `height(400.dp)`** rather than the real screen's `weight(1f)` because `weight` has no parent `Column` to weight against inside a `@Preview` and would collapse to zero height — same trade-off any future "centered in the available space" composable's preview faces. No icon and no secondary line are deliberate (AC says one short line; the composable's `(Box → single Text)` signature is stable if a future a11y / UX audit wants a leading icon or hint to land additively). Channels with zero messages also render the prompt (the gate is `!hasMessages` regardless of `isPromoted` — only the chip carries the `!isPromoted` half); if product later asks for channel-specific copy ("This channel has no messages yet — say hi"), the predicate gains an `isPromoted` branch and a second string — out of scope at #138. Figma anchor: there is no separate empty-state node drawn — node `16:8` is the populated thread; AC copy and styling (`bodyMedium` / `onSurfaceVariant`, vertically centered) drive the visual. No `AnimatedVisibility` on the swap — `Crossfade` between `EmptyThreadState` and `LazyColumn` is a one-line follow-up if designer signs off on a duration.
- [ThreadOverflowMenu](features/thread-overflow-menu.md) — stateless Material 3 `DropdownMenu` ([#251](codebase/251.md)) at `ui/conversations/thread/ThreadOverflowMenu.kt` rendering five `DropdownMenuItem`s in this exact order — **New session**, **Rename**, **Change workspace…** (U+2026 ellipsis, M3 "opens further UI" convention; same shape as `save_as_channel_action`), **Archive**, **Channel info** — each dispatching through the new `sealed interface ThreadEvent { data object NewSession; data object Rename; data object ChangeWorkspace; data object Archive; data object ChannelInfo }` co-located at the top of `ThreadViewModel.kt` (placement-next-to-VM rather than the screen-file precedent of `DiscussionListEvent` / `ChannelListEvent` because the VM dispatcher is the only consumer in this slice). Signature `(expanded: Boolean, onDismiss: () -> Unit, onEvent: (ThreadEvent) -> Unit, modifier: Modifier = Modifier)` — single-`onEvent`-sink shape (homogeneous events → one sink; contrast against [`ChannelInfoSheet`](features/channel-info-sheet.md)'s six named callbacks, which serve a heterogeneous sheet of distinct affordances). Each item's `onClick = { onDismiss(); onEvent(ThreadEvent.X) }` — dismiss-before-handler ordering load-bearing for both the Compose-test combined-log assertion `log == listOf("dismiss", "event:NewSession")` and downstream host wiring (dialogs / sheets / nav transitions opened from these events need the menu closed before mounting to avoid Compose layout layering issues; same precedent as `DiscussionListScreen.kt:209-212`'s long-press menu from [#25](codebase/25.md), the only other production `DropdownMenu` call site). Composable is **mounted in production since [#252](codebase/252.md)** inside `ThreadTopAppBar`'s `actions` slot — wrapped in a `Box` alongside the existing `MoreVert` `IconButton` so the M3 `DropdownMenu` anchors directly below the icon (`Box`-as-anchor, not the implicit `actions` `Row`, because the row's bounds would mis-position a single-icon overflow); the screen-owned `expanded` flag lives on `ThreadScreen` as `var overflowExpanded by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }` (mirroring the existing `sheetVisible` line from [#254](codebase/254.md)), `ThreadTopAppBar` gained three parameters (`overflowExpanded`, `onOverflowDismiss`, `onOverflowEvent`), the screen's `onOverflowClick: () -> Unit = {}` parameter was renamed in place to `onOverflowEvent: (ThreadEvent) -> Unit = {}` (defaulted shape preserved so the four `@Preview` composables keep compiling), and `MainActivity` binds `onOverflowEvent = vm::onOverflowEvent` at the `composable(Routes.CONVERSATION_THREAD)` destination block. New instrumented test `ThreadScreenOverflowTest.kt` covers AC #4 with seven `@Test` methods (one render-all-five-items test, five per-item dispatch-then-dismiss tests asserting `events == listOf(ThreadEvent.X)` + `assertDoesNotExist()` on the tapped item — `assertDoesNotExist()` rather than `assertIsNotDisplayed()` is the right shape because `DropdownMenu` removes its content from composition on dismiss — and one `Espresso.pressBack()` dismiss-without-event test paired with `composeTestRule.waitForIdle()` for determinism). `ThreadViewModel.onOverflowEvent(event: ThreadEvent)` is a 10-line `when` dispatcher: `Archive → viewModelScope.launch { repository.archive(state.value.conversationId) }` (same shape as the existing `sendMessage` launcher; conversation id read from `state.value` for one-grep convention with `sendMessage`), the other four cases are an exhaustive `Unit` arm — exhaustive `when` over the sealed surface compiler-enforces that a missing case is a build error, so no TODO comments / no stub bodies needed and the four downstream per-item follow-ups (rename dialog → `Rename`, [#208](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/208) → `ChangeWorkspace`, Channel Info sheet host → `ChannelInfo`, eventual `startNewSession` wiring → `NewSession`) fill them in. **Scope is overflow-only** — the six pre-existing plain handlers on `ThreadViewModel` (`sendMessage`, `retry`, `onWorkspaceChipTapped`, `onWorkspacePicked`, `onWorkspacePickerDismissed`, `onModelSelected`) are **not** migrated to the sealed surface (option (ii) from the convention question on [#203](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/203); option (iii) — migrate all six alongside the overflow work — was rejected as oversized). Five new `R.string.thread_overflow_*` keys (`_new_session`, `_rename`, `_change_workspace`, `_archive`, `_channel_info`). All five `data object`s (not plain `object`) for the auto-generated `toString` that makes Compose-test combined-log assertions readable without a custom matcher; parameterless because the conversation id is sourced from `state.value` at dispatch time, not embedded in the event (a future payload-bearing case migrates to `data class`). No new dependencies, no Koin changes (`onOverflowEvent` adds no constructor parameter), no `gradle/libs.versions.toml` edits, no `strings.xml` edits in [#252](codebase/252.md), no new previews ([#252](codebase/252.md) added no `@Preview` because the menu is system-popup-windowed and Compose `@Preview` doesn't draw popup windows; the four `ThreadScreen` previews cover the surrounding chrome). Tests: two new `ThreadViewModelTest` methods pin `Archive` → exactly one `archive(id)` call with the current `state.value.conversationId` (`onOverflowEvent_archive_callsRepositoryArchiveOnceWithCurrentConversationId`) and the other four cases → zero `archive` calls (`onOverflowEvent_otherCases_doNotCallArchive`) — both use a new private `RecordingRepo : ConversationRepository` test double rather than `FakeConversationRepository` (direct call-shape assertion is one indirection less coupled than projecting the archive's effect through `observeConversations` and asserting on the resulting set; the `flowOf(emptyList())` / `flowOf(null)` defaults on `observe*` methods are non-optional — non-emitting stubs would hang the `combine` and deadlock any `vm.state.first { … }` test). Five `androidTest` Compose tests in `ThreadOverflowMenuTest.kt` mirror [`WorkspacePickerSheetTest`](features/workspace-picker-sheet.md)'s `createComposeRule` + `PyrycodeMobileTheme` + `InstrumentationRegistry.getInstrumentation().targetContext.getString(R.string.thread_overflow_*)` lookup scaffold — one render-order test asserts all five labels are displayed; five per-item tests use the **combined-log recorder pattern** (a single `val log = mutableListOf<String>()` with `onDismiss = { log.add("dismiss") }` and `onEvent = { log.add("event:$it") }`, asserted as `log == listOf("dismiss", "event:NewSession")`) — the combined log is the directly-testable expression of "dismisses the dropdown before firing its `ThreadEvent`" because two separate recorders (a counter + a list) couldn't pin the ordering inside one `onClick` callback. Split from [#203](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/203); the original #140 placeholder rolled into #203's scope and now lives split across [#251](codebase/251.md) (composable + sealed event + VM dispatcher) and [#252](codebase/252.md) (host mount + screen-owned `overflowExpanded` + `MainActivity` binding). The first per-item follow-up — `Rename` → [`RenameDialog`](features/rename-dialog.md) — landed in [#141](codebase/141.md), promoting the `Rename` arm out of the `Unit` branch list and extending `ThreadEvent` with `RenameSubmit(name: String)` (the surface's first parameter-bearing case, cashing in the [#251](codebase/251.md) reservation) + `RenameDismiss`. **Context-aware items added in [#204](codebase/204.md)**: `ThreadOverflowMenu` gained an `isPromoted: Boolean` parameter (between `expanded` and `onDismiss`, no default) driving two mutually-exclusive `if` blocks wrapping the five common items — discussions (`isPromoted == false`) get a leading **Save as channel…** item that emits a new `ThreadEvent.SaveAsChannel` `data object` case (no-op `Unit` arm in `onOverflowEvent`, joining `NewSession`/`ChangeWorkspace`/`ChannelInfo`; the eventual promote-dialog wiring is [#142](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/142)'s scope, parallel to [#141](codebase/141.md)'s rename slice); channels (`isPromoted == true`) get a trailing **Install memory plugin** item that calls `LocalUriHandler.current.openUri(MEMORY_PLUGIN_DOCS_URL)` directly (no `ThreadEvent` — UI-side concern with no VM authority, contrast against `SaveAsChannel`'s eventual VM-owned dialog flow). **`save_as_channel_action` is reused** (the existing key at `strings.xml:11` from `DiscussionListScreen.kt:208`'s long-press promote menu in [#25](codebase/25.md)) — same English copy + same semantic action → one key; only one new string `thread_overflow_install_memory_plugin = "Install memory plugin"`. **`MEMORY_PLUGIN_DOCS_URL` is imported, not duplicated** — the `internal const val` already lives at `SessionBoundaryDelimiter.kt:37` from [#135](codebase/135.md) for the empty-thread install affordance; Phase 3+ swap to a real install endpoint becomes a one-grep edit. `ThreadTopAppBar` gained a fourth parameter `isPromoted: Boolean` (between `onOverflowEvent` and `modifier`); `ThreadScreen` passes `isPromoted = state.isPromoted` down — **no new screen parameter** because `ThreadUiState.isPromoted` already flows in via `state` (projection from `Conversation.isPromoted` is untouched). No `MainActivity` edit. The original render-order test split into `channel_menu_*` + `discussion_menu_*` variants (each asserts the absent-mode item `assertDoesNotExist()`); five existing per-item tap tests gained `isPromoted = true`; two new tap tests — `tapping_save_as_channel_dismisses_then_dispatches_event` (combined-log assertion `log == listOf("dismiss", "event:SaveAsChannel")`) and `tapping_install_memory_plugin_dismisses_and_opens_docs_url` (combined-log `log == listOf("dismiss")` to prove no event fired, plus a `RecordingUriHandler` injected via `CompositionLocalProvider(LocalUriHandler provides fakeHandler)` *inside* `PyrycodeMobileTheme` — the theme stays the outermost frame so future theme-local overrides aren't shadowed). No new `ThreadViewModel` unit tests — `SaveAsChannel` is a no-op `Unit` arm with no observable side effect to assert; the compiler-enforced exhaustive `when` is the contract. **[#540](codebase/540.md)** landed the last remaining per-item follow-up — `NewSession` → a new private `sendNewSession()` calling `repository.startNewSession(conversationId)` (#539's fire-and-forget wire send), **not** through `launchGuardedRepoCall` (that swallows failures, the opposite of this contract): a `CancellationException`-first, then `IllegalStateException` → `newSessionErrorChannel.trySend(Unit)` catch surfaces the not-connected failure via a new `newSessionErrors: Flow<Unit>` one-shot (the `modalSendErrors` idiom cloned, #452), collected by `ThreadScreen` as a second snackbar with the fixed local string `new_session_failed`. Success stays passive — the [#336](codebase/336.md) fold renders the delimiter when `session_transition` arrives. No case remains a no-op `Unit` arm; the item still sits behind the [#508](codebase/508.md) `mutationsSupported` gate in relay mode (deliberately left untouched — a family-wide reachability decision, not a #540-local one). **[#556](codebase/556.md)** then moved `Archive` **off** the shared `launchGuardedRepoCall` (which inertly swallowed every failure and, on a throw, short-circuited the following `PopBack` — a silent no-op that also stranded the user on the thread) onto its own `sendArchive()`, cloning the `sendNewSession`/`newSessionErrors` idiom but with a second caught type: `RelayErrorException` alongside `IllegalStateException`, because archive is request/reply (awaits `conversation_updated`) so a correlated server `error` is reachable, unlike fire-and-forget `new_session`. Adds `archiveErrorChannel`/`archiveErrors: Flow<Unit>` and a third `ThreadScreen` snackbar collector (`archive_failed`, fixed local string, never the caught exception's message); `CancellationException` is rethrown first; `PopBack` sits inside the success continuation only, so on success the thread pops back and the conversation leaves the main list list-driven (via `observeConversations` re-emitting) with no explicit removal call. **[#561](codebase/561.md)** then moved `ChangeWorkspace`'s `onWorkspacePicked(path)` off the same shared `launchGuardedRepoCall` onto `sendChangeWorkspace(path)` — clones `sendArchive`'s two-catch set (`RelayErrorException` + `IllegalStateException`, since `change_workspace` is request/reply per [#560](codebase/560.md)) but `sendNewSession`'s **passive** success continuation (no `PopBack` — the workspace chip re-labels itself list-driven off the confirmed-upsert re-emit, no VM fold needed). Adds `changeWorkspaceErrorChannel`/`changeWorkspaceErrors: Flow<Unit>` and a fourth `ThreadScreen` snackbar collector (`change_workspace_failed`, fixed local string). Same `mutationsSupported` reachability caveat as `#540`/`#556`; e2e sibling #562 stays parked pending the family-wide gate.
- [RenameDialog](features/rename-dialog.md) — stateless M3 `AlertDialog` ([#141](codebase/141.md)) at `ui/conversations/components/RenameDialog.kt`, sibling to [`CreateFolderDialog`](features/create-folder-dialog.md) (its direct structural template) and consumed by [`ThreadScreen`](features/thread-screen.md). Renders Figma `19:14` for the channel/discussion rename flow triggered by the [`ThreadOverflowMenu`](features/thread-overflow-menu.md)'s `Rename` item, replacing the no-op left by [#252](codebase/252.md). `public @Composable fun RenameDialog(initialName: String, onSubmit: (String) -> Unit, onDismiss: () -> Unit, modifier: Modifier = Modifier)` — `public` (not `internal` like `CreateFolderDialog`) because the host lives in a sibling package (`ui/conversations/thread/`). Public composable delegates to a file-private `RenameDialogInternal(initialName, initialValue, onSubmit, onDismiss, modifier)` seam seeded with `TextFieldValue(initialName, TextRange(0, initialName.length))` so the full name is selected on first composition (AC #2's "typing immediately overwrites" comes for free from M3's standard replace-on-keystroke behaviour). The seam exists so three `@Preview`s can demonstrate both pre-filled and "edited" states without going through runtime input — same shape lifted wholesale from [`CreateFolderDialog`](features/create-folder-dialog.md). Three substantive deltas from the template: (i) the pre-selection `TextRange(0, initialName.length)` seed (vs. `TextRange.Zero` on empty input); (ii) `isSaveEnabled` is a two-part gate `trimmedName.isNotEmpty() && trimmedName != initialName` rather than just `trimmedName.isNotEmpty()` — the unchanged-name check is what makes the rename dialog distinct, and the `remember(initialName) { derivedStateOf { ... } }` keying is defensive (the host today gates with `if (state.showRenameDialog)` which tears down between renames, but the key future-proofs against a streaming cross-device rename in Phase 4); (iii) `public` not `internal`. Internal state mirrors `CreateFolderDialog`: `FocusRequester` + `mutableStateOf(initialValue)` + `derivedStateOf { fieldValue.text.trim() }` + `LaunchedEffect(Unit) { focusRequester.requestFocus() }` (fires once after the composition tree commits — when `requestFocus()` becomes safe to call). Same submit-path duality: tap `Save` (`enabled = isSaveEnabled`) **or** `ImeAction.Done` (guarded by `if (isSaveEnabled)` so blank/unchanged input is a no-op), both routing through `onSubmit(trimmedName)`; Cancel / outside-tap / back-press route through `onDismiss` with no emission. **The dialog is the trim authority** — `onSubmit` receives the already-trimmed name; `ThreadViewModel.onOverflowEvent(RenameSubmit(name))` forwards `event.name` to `repository.rename` without re-trimming. Four new `R.string.rename_dialog_*` keys (`_title = "Rename"`, `_field_label = "Name"`, `_save = "Save"`, `_cancel = "Cancel"`) — `rename_dialog_*` namespace (not `thread_overflow_*`) because a future TopAppBar tap-to-rename entry point will reuse the same strings without their key suggesting a wrong origin (different posture from `CreateFolderDialog`'s inline literals, justified by the multi-entry-point reuse case the architect spec called out). `ThreadUiState` widens with `showRenameDialog: Boolean = false` (driven by a private `pendingRenameDialog: MutableStateFlow<Boolean>` on `ThreadViewModel` folded as the **fourth** source into the main `combine(...)` block — five-arity total at the native overload ceiling); the **VM-owned vs. screen-hoisted choice** (vs. `overflowExpanded`/`sheetVisible` which live as `var ... by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }` on `ThreadScreen`) is deliberate — dialog visibility carries semantic content (user invoked Rename, dialog represents in-progress operation against Conversation state, future tap-to-rename TopAppBar entry point dispatches the same `ThreadEvent.Rename`), centralising on the VM keeps the dialog single-sourced. `ThreadEvent` widens with two cases — `data class RenameSubmit(val name: String)` (the surface's first parameter-bearing case, cashing in the [#251](codebase/251.md) reservation that "a future menu item with a payload migrates that case to `data class`") + `data object RenameDismiss` — placed immediately after the existing `Rename` case so the rename family stays contiguous; `onOverflowEvent` replaces the [#252](codebase/252.md) `Rename` no-op with `pendingRenameDialog.value = true` and adds two new arms: `RenameSubmit(name) → pendingRenameDialog.value = false; viewModelScope.launch { repository.rename(state.value.conversationId, name) }` (flag flips **synchronously before** the launch so the dialog dismisses immediately with no flicker, and a re-tap of `Rename` during an in-flight rename has well-defined state — same fire-and-forget shape as `Archive` / `sendMessage`), `RenameDismiss → pendingRenameDialog.value = false`. `ThreadScreen` renders the dialog as a third `Scaffold` sibling alongside [`WorkspacePicker`](features/workspace-picker.md) and [`StatusSheet`](features/status-sheet.md): `if (state.showRenameDialog) { RenameDialog(initialName = state.displayName, onSubmit = { onOverflowEvent(ThreadEvent.RenameSubmit(it)) }, onDismiss = { onOverflowEvent(ThreadEvent.RenameDismiss) }) }`. `initialName = state.displayName` (not the raw `Conversation.name`) so the Save-disabled-on-unchanged check uses the same string the user sees in the title bar — `displayName` already encodes the `"Untitled channel"` / `"Untitled discussion"` fallback for blank names. `MainActivity` is **not** touched — the pre-existing method-reference binding `vm::onOverflowEvent` from [#252](codebase/252.md) handles the two new event variants automatically. `RecordingRepo` (the private test double in `ThreadViewModelTest.kt`) grew a real `rename(...)` body — gained `val renameCalls = mutableListOf<Pair<String, String>>()` and replaced the previous `TODO("not used")` body with a recorder that returns a minimal `Conversation(id, name, ...)`; tests assert on `renameCalls` directly. Same direct-call-shape-assertion idiom from [#251](codebase/251.md) for `archiveCalls`. The `onOverflowEvent_otherCases_doNotCallArchive` test dropped `ThreadEvent.Rename` from its iteration list — `Rename` now mutates state, no longer a no-op case. Tests: nine Compose UI tests in `androidTest/.../RenameDialogTest.kt` mirroring [`CreateFolderDialogTest`](features/create-folder-dialog.md)'s `createComposeRule()` + `PyrycodeMobileTheme` scaffold (`title_and_field_label_and_buttons_render`, `field_prefills_with_initial_name`, `save_disabled_on_first_composition_when_matches_initial_name`, `save_disabled_when_input_cleared`, `save_disabled_on_whitespace_only_input`, `save_disabled_when_trimmed_input_equals_initial_name`, `save_enabled_when_trimmed_input_differs_from_initial_name`, `tapping_save_invokes_onSubmit_with_trimmed_text`, `tapping_cancel_invokes_onDismiss_without_invoking_onSubmit`) — **`performTextReplacement(...)` (not `performTextInput(...)`) is the right primitive** because the field opens with `initialName` already populated and `performTextInput` would append rather than replace (lesson learned: the first three iterations used `performTextInput` and silently passed because `"old" + "new"` trim-equals neither); `hasSetTextAction() and hasText("old name")` disambiguates the editable field node from the static title `Text`. Three new unit tests on `ThreadViewModelTest.kt` pin the VM dispatcher (`onOverflowEvent_rename_setsShowRenameDialogFlag`, `_renameSubmit_callsRepositoryAndClearsFlag`, `_renameDismiss_clearsFlagWithoutRepositoryCall`) — all asserting on `vm.state.value.showRenameDialog` and `repo.renameCalls` directly. **No new dependencies, no Koin changes, no `gradle/libs.versions.toml` edits, no `MainActivity.kt` edits, no preview snapshot infrastructure changes.** First downstream of the four per-item follow-ups [#252](codebase/252.md) left as `Unit` arms; the other three remain open ([#208](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/issues/208) for `ChangeWorkspace`, Channel Info sheet host for `ChannelInfo`, not-yet-ticketed for `NewSession`).
- [SaveAsChannelDialog](features/save-as-channel-dialog.md) — stateless M3 `AlertDialog` ([#142](codebase/142.md)) at `ui/conversations/components/SaveAsChannelDialog.kt`, sibling to [`RenameDialog`](features/rename-dialog.md) (the direct structural template — both share the public-plus-`*Internal`-seam shape lifted transitively from [`CreateFolderDialog`](features/create-folder-dialog.md)) and consumed by [`ThreadScreen`](features/thread-screen.md). Renders Figma `19:24` for the promote-from-thread flow triggered by the [`ThreadOverflowMenu`](features/thread-overflow-menu.md)'s discussion-only **Save as channel…** item ([#204](codebase/204.md)), replacing the no-op `Unit` arm left by #204. `public @Composable fun SaveAsChannelDialog(initialName: String, onSubmit: (name: String, workspace: WorkspaceChoice) -> Unit, onDismiss: () -> Unit, modifier: Modifier = Modifier)` — `public` because the host lives in a sibling package (`ui/conversations/thread/`); the `onSubmit` signature carries the radio-selected `WorkspaceChoice` enum (top-level in `ThreadViewModel.kt`, two variants `DEDICATED` / `SCRATCH`) alongside the trimmed name — keeping the enum at the event boundary lets the dialog stay path-agnostic and makes test assertions read cleanly. Public composable delegates to a file-private `SaveAsChannelDialogInternal(initialValue, initialWorkspace, onSubmit, onDismiss, modifier)` seam seeded with `TextFieldValue(initialName, TextRange(0, initialName.length))` + `initialWorkspace = WorkspaceChoice.DEDICATED`; the seam takes `initialWorkspace` as a parameter so the SCRATCH-selected preview can flip the radio's initial state without runtime input. Three substantive deltas from [`RenameDialog`](features/rename-dialog.md): (i) the `onSubmit` signature carries `WorkspaceChoice` alongside the trimmed name; (ii) **single-gate `isSaveEnabled = trimmedName.isNotEmpty()`** — no `trimmedName != initialName` clause because the dialog has no semantic notion of "unchanged" (submitting the unchanged seeded `"New channel"` is valid); (iii) the `AlertDialog.text` slot is a `Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp))` containing the `OutlinedTextField` *plus* a file-private `WorkspaceRadios(selected, onSelectedChange)` composable rendering two `Row`s wrapped in `Column.selectableGroup()` (M3 accessibility convention), each row using `Modifier.selectable(selected = …, onClick = …, role = Role.RadioButton)` with `RadioButton(onClick = null)` so the row (not the radio glyph) is the click target. The **DEDICATED radio renders a mono-font path caption** `~/pyry-workspace/channels/<auto-slug>/` styled `MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace)` in `colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant` — kept as a **static literal** in the composable (not a string resource) because it's fixed example text matching Figma `19:35`'s Roboto Mono rendering and semantically not localizable in Phase 0; the `<auto-slug>` substring is a literal placeholder marker (not a live preview of `name.toChannelSlug()`). The SCRATCH radio has no caption. Same submit-path duality as `RenameDialog`: tap `Save` (`enabled = isSaveEnabled`) **or** `ImeAction.Done` (guarded by `if (isSaveEnabled)`), both routing through `onSubmit(trimmedName, selectedWorkspace)`; Cancel / outside-tap / back-press route through `onDismiss`. **The dialog is the trim authority** — `ThreadViewModel` forwards `event.name` to `repository.promote` without re-trimming. Six new `R.string.save_as_channel_dialog_*` keys (`_title = "Save as channel"` — no ellipsis, the menu-item key `save_as_channel_action = "Save as channel…"` at `strings.xml:11` stays the menu string; `_field_label = "Name"`; `_workspace_dedicated = "Move to dedicated channel folder"`; `_workspace_scratch = "Keep in scratch"`; `_save = "Save"`; `_cancel = "Cancel"`) — six (not the AC's approximate "four"); placed after the `rename_dialog_*` family. `ThreadUiState` widens with `saveAsChannelDialog: SaveAsChannelDialogState? = null` (nullable sub-state carrier bundling the seeded pre-fill name with the visibility flag per the [#78](codebase/78.md) `PendingPromotion` shape — visible iff non-null; contrasts with #141's `showRenameDialog: Boolean` because this dialog's `initialName` must be captured at *open* time while rename reads `state.displayName` at render time), driven by a private `pendingSaveAsChannelDialog: MutableStateFlow<SaveAsChannelDialogState?>` on `ThreadViewModel`. The outer `combine(...)` **stays at the native 5-arity ceiling** via a new private `data class TransientDialogs(val renameVisible: Boolean, val saveAsChannel: SaveAsChannelDialogState?)` plus a pre-combiner `transientDialogs: Flow<TransientDialogs> = combine(pendingRenameDialog, pendingSaveAsChannelDialog) { … }` that folds both transient flags into one source — cashing in #141's ceiling-note warning; the main combine's `pendingRenameDialog` arm became `transientDialogs`, the lambda destructures `dialogs.renameVisible` / `dialogs.saveAsChannel`, no other call-site churn. **Patterns established**: (a) nested pre-combiner for transient-dialog sources when the outer combine sits at arity ceiling — semantic naming on the bundle class (`TransientDialogs`) beats anonymous tuple types; (b) nullable sub-state vs. boolean-plus-siblings for dialogs — discriminator is whether the seed value needs to be captured at *open* time (nullable carrier) or read at *render* time (boolean flag); (c) enum payload on a `ThreadEvent` data class — keeping the enum at the event boundary lets the dialog stay path-agnostic and makes test assertions read cleanly; (d) file-scope `private` extension functions for VM-local helpers (`resolveWorkspace`, `String.toChannelSlug`); (e) workspace caption renders as a literal, not a string resource — applies to any fixed example-text caption (filesystem paths, URL templates) whose content is structurally fixed. `ThreadEvent` widens with two cases — `data class SaveAsChannelSubmit(val name: String, val workspace: WorkspaceChoice)` + `data object SaveAsChannelDismiss` — placed immediately after the existing `SaveAsChannel` so the family stays contiguous; `onOverflowEvent` replaces the [#204](codebase/204.md) `SaveAsChannel` no-op with `pendingSaveAsChannelDialog.value = SaveAsChannelDialogState(initialName = AUTO_SUGGESTED_CHANNEL_NAME)` (where `AUTO_SUGGESTED_CHANNEL_NAME = "New channel"` is a file-scope `private const val` Phase-0 stub; Phase 4 replaces with a generator that reads the first user message off `state.value.items`) and adds two new arms: `SaveAsChannelSubmit(name, workspace) → pendingSaveAsChannelDialog.value = null; viewModelScope.launch { repository.promote(state.value.conversationId, event.name, resolveWorkspace(event.name, event.workspace)) }` (flag flips **synchronously before** the launch — same dismiss-then-suspend ordering as #141's `RenameSubmit`), `SaveAsChannelDismiss → pendingSaveAsChannelDialog.value = null`. **File-scope `private` slug helper**: `resolveWorkspace(name, choice): String?` returns `"pyry-workspace/channels/${name.toChannelSlug()}"` for `DEDICATED` and `null` for `SCRATCH` (preserve existing cwd per `repository.promote(..., workspace = null)` contract);
