- [Queued backlog](features/queued-backlog.md) — the per-conversation **thread-observable list of messages waiting while claude is busy** ([#460](codebase/460.md), split from #429; epic pyrycode#597 Phase 3 — the **data substrate**, render is consumer #461, outbound drop is #466 / its UI affordance #467). Decode the capability-gated v2 `queue_state` snapshot (`{conversation_id, queued:[{queued_msg_id, text, ts}]}` — the wire form of `msgqueue.Snapshot`, FIFO order; server SSOT pyrycode#705/#720, ADR 025) into `observeQueue(conversationId): Flow<List<QueuedMessage>>`, **on the [`ConversationRepository`](features/conversation-repository.md) interface with a `flowOf(emptyList())` default** so it reaches the thread through the [`StableConversationRepository`](features/stable-conversation-repository.md) facade — the same surfacing decision as [`observeStall`](features/stall-state.md) (#395), of which this is a **near-exact structural twin**. The new public element type `QueuedMessage(id: Long, text: String, timestamp: Instant)` is **co-located with the interface** (like `ThreadItem`); `id` is the daemon's `queued_msg_id` — a wire **`uint64`** decoded as a `Long` (a `String` is the #720 trap), a plain ordinal not a nonce. Rides the **single existing** `pump.inbound` collector in [`RemoteConversationRepository`](features/remote-conversation-repository.md): a fifth connection-scoped `queuedByConversation: MutableStateFlow<Map<String, List<QueuedMessage>>>` (single-writer → no race), updated by one **full-replace** `TYPE_QUEUE_STATE` arm (`it + (conversationId to queue)` — each snapshot is authoritative, overwrites that conversation's entry, preserves wire order verbatim; AC #1/#3). **Unlike #395 there is NO clearing hook** — a snapshot is whole-truth state replaced by the next (possibly empty) snapshot, never cleared by forward-progress events, so the live-session arm is untouched (the simpler full-snapshot shape). Gated on `CAPABILITY_INTERACTIVE in negotiatedCapabilities()` (fail-closed, **rides the already-negotiated `interactive`** — no new capability advertised); `decodeQueueState` drops a malformed payload without tearing down the collector, **one bad item drops the whole snapshot** (AC #4). `observeQueue = queuedByConversation.map { it[id].orEmpty() }.distinctUntilChanged()` (empty-until-first-snapshot, AC #2); facade `switchToLive(emptyList())` + fresh repo per connection (#351) → a backlog never survives a reconnect. `QueuedMessageDto`/`QueueStatePayloadDto` + `toQueue()` in `InteractivePayloads.kt`; **`queued` is the one non-strict field** (nullable-defaulted + `.orEmpty()` to tolerate a wire `null`/`[]` empty backlog — `MobileJson` has no `coerceInputValues`). `security-sensitive` — `text` is user content carried verbatim, never logged; memory bounded by replacement; architect + code-review **PASS** (1 NIT on the `QueuedMessage` placement = the documented architect call). Test-first RED→GREEN (8 remote + 2 facade tests); all gates green ([PR #463](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/pull/463)). Stall/queue interaction flagged to #461 (exposed independently). **Render shipped #461** → [Queued backlog section](features/queued-backlog-section.md). **Outbound drop shipped #466** → [`dropQueuedMessage`](features/queued-backlog.md) (sends `dequeue_message {conversation_id, queued_msg_id}` via `sendAndAwaitReply`; `Long → uint64` number, the #720 trap; no projection mutation — backlog updates via the next `queue_state`; defaulted-interface-method precedent like `requestScreenSnapshot`, [codebase/466](codebase/466.md)). **UI drop affordance shipped #467** → the [Queued backlog section](features/queued-backlog-section.md) trailing close button → `ThreadViewModel.onDropQueued` fires this send inert-on-failure ([codebase/467](codebase/467.md)).
- [Queued backlog section](features/queued-backlog-section.md) — the **UI half of the queued-message backlog** ([#461](codebase/461.md), split from #429; epic pyrycode#597 Phase 3 — the **render** of data substrate [#460](codebase/460.md)). A new **stateless** `fun QueuedBacklog(queued: List<QueuedMessage>, modifier: Modifier = Modifier)` at `ui/conversations/components/` renders [`ThreadUiState.queuedMessages`](features/thread-screen.md) (the [`observeQueue`](features/queued-backlog.md) backlog) as a **de-emphasized foot-of-list section** below the message list and above [`ThinkingIndicator`](features/thinking-indicator.md): a "Queued" caption + one row per entry **in wire order verbatim** (FIFO, no sort/dedup; AC #1), each an end-aligned user-side bubble (same `primaryContainer` shape/colour family as [`UserMessageBubble`](features/message-bubble.md)) at `Modifier.alpha(QUEUED_ALPHA = 0.6f)` with a leading decorative `Icons.Outlined.Schedule` glyph + plain `Text` (**never `MarkdownText`** — un-sent user input). `if (queued.isEmpty()) return` makes it a pure function of the hoisted list (the [`ThinkingIndicator`](features/thinking-indicator.md)/[`StallPromotionBanner`](features/stall-promotion-banner.md) early-return idiom), light+dark previews. Each row also carries a **per-row drop affordance** ([#467](codebase/467.md)): a trailing `IconButton`+`Icons.Outlined.Close` (un-queue, not delete; `cd_thread_queued_drop`) whose `onDrop(id)` hoists the row's `QueuedMessage.id` through a new defaulted `ThreadScreen.onDropQueued` param → `MainActivity vm::onDropQueued` → `ThreadViewModel.onDropQueued(id)`, which fires #466's `repository.dropQueuedMessage(conversationId, id)` on the already-injected facade (**no new ctor param** — drop is a `ConversationRepository` interface method, the #466/`requestScreenSnapshot` placement). **No optimistic removal** — the row leaves only on the next `queue_state` (`observeQueue`); the VM mutates no state. The handler mirrors `sendInterrupt` exactly (rethrow `CancellationException` first, then swallow `RelayErrorException`/`IllegalStateException` inert — AC #4, no error surface); `IllegalArgumentException` (`conversation.not_found`) deliberately uncaught (VM always passes its own valid id). The clickable `IconButton` forms its own semantics merge boundary, so the drop nodes stay addressable in the unmerged tree despite the section's `mergeDescendants` group. **Hoisted onto `ThreadUiState.queuedMessages` (a defaulted field), NOT a sibling `StateFlow`** like `isStalled`/`isThinking` — the deliberate divergence from the two render twins: the queue is **thread content** (an ordered list, same category as `items`), not a transient scalar (AC #4). Folded with `threadItems` into a file-private `ThreadContent` so the 5-arm typed `state` combine keeps one content arm; the queue **content** needs **no `ThreadScreen` param** (rides `state`), while the drop **action** added one defaulted `onDropQueued` param + a single `vm::onDropQueued` `MainActivity` line (#467). Separate wrap-content `Column`, **not** a `LazyColumn` row (list keying/alpha/auto-scroll untouched — the foot-of-list-affordance pattern). **Not** `security-sensitive` — resolves #460's forwarded UI-leakage flag: `entry.text` is the same user-content class sent bubbles already render **without** `FLAG_SECURE`, no new capture surface, logs nothing. Visual is **design-owed** (no Figma 16-8 backlog treatment **or** drop affordance drawn yet; M3 default — both #461 render and #467 drop button). Lesson: a `mergeDescendants` a11y group defeats per-row text asserts → read `useUnmergedTree = true` (a clickable child like the drop button keeps its own merge boundary, so it stays addressable too). Render (#461): code review **PASS**, zero blocking; 3 VM + 4 screen tests RED→GREEN ([PR #464](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/pull/464)). Drop affordance ([#467](codebase/467.md), size S, not security-sensitive, blockedBy #458): code review **PASS**, zero blocking (1 non-blocking note on the uncaught `IllegalArgumentException`); 4 VM + 2 Compose tests RED→GREEN, the swallow test captures via `Thread.setDefaultUncaughtExceptionHandler` (count-alone false-greens), the activate test taps the *second* row and asserts its id ([PR #470](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/pull/470)).
- [Live tool-call](features/live-tool-call.md) — the correlated, **status-carrying tool row** in the thread ([#387](codebase/387.md), split from #368; blocked by [#385](codebase/385.md)): turn the decoded `tool_use` (start) / `tool_result` (completion) [`LiveSessionEvent`](features/live-session-events.md)s into **one evolving `Role.Tool`** [`Message`](features/data-model.md) carrying a status. A `tool_use` opens a `Running` row (name + input); the matching `tool_result` (correlated by `toolUseId`, **not** by position) updates **that same row in place** to `Done`/`Failed` (`isError ? Failed : Done`) and attaches the output. **Model:** a new public `enum class ToolCallStatus { Running, Done, Failed }` + a **trailing defaulted** `status = ToolCallStatus.Done` on `ToolCall` (`Done` = the pre-existing finished-call semantics → zero fixture cascade, `ToolCall(a,b,c) == ToolCall(a,b,c, status=Done)` stays green; the file already had 3 public top-level types so no [[ktlint-filename-rule-single-class]] trip). **Correlation:** two private folds in [`RemoteConversationRepository`](features/remote-conversation-repository.md) — `applyToolUse` (append if absent, leave untouched on repeat id → no second row, no status reset) + `applyToolResult` (update in place if present, **no-op if absent** → no orphan; idempotent on duplicate) — matched `id == toolUseId && role == Role.Tool` (namespaced so a `toolUseId` can't clobber a real `message_id`). Both ride the **existing** #385 live-session demux arm (a `when (event)` dispatch alongside the #395 stall-clear + the `tryEmit` — **no second subscription, no new gate/collector**) and write the **existing** `messagesByConversation`, so tool rows **interleave by arrival order** in `observeMessages` for free (AC #4 — no merge, no second flow, no sort; a ViewModel merge can't satisfy it because ordering is a repository surface). In-place flip re-emits via `distinctUntilChanged` (`ThreadItem.MessageItem` is a `data class`). Single writer (the lone inbound collector) → insert-then-update never races; `Clock.System.now()` timestamp (arrival-order, never a timestamp sort; can't throw on a malformed `envelope.ts`), result preserves the use's timestamp; **not** folded into `lastMessages` (a tool call is never a list preview). Tolerant of missing / duplicate / out-of-order `tool_result` + malformed payload (dropped at the existing `decodeLiveSessionEvent` `catch`, collector survives) — AC #3/#5. Inside the existing `CAPABILITY_INTERACTIVE` gate (fail-closed). `security-sensitive`: **no new parse point** (consumes the already-typed events), `name`/`inputSummary`/`resultSummary` carried **verbatim** into the `ToolCall` fields (output-encoding is #388's job), **zero `Log`/`println` touch tool-derived data** incl. drop/no-op branches (grep-verified). The **visible** status affordance is sibling **#388** (extends [`ToolCallRow`](features/tool-call-row.md), Figma 16-28 design-owed). Deliberate inverse of [#395](codebase/395.md)'s separate `Set` projection: a tool row *is* a thread row → fold into the message list; stall is state *beside* it → its own flow. 11 new tests, test-first RED→GREEN; all gates green; code review **PASS**, zero findings ([PR #409](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/pull/409)).
- [Session-transition fold](features/session-transition-fold.md) — the **data-layer wire→thread fold** ([#336](codebase/336.md), split from #313) that decodes the capability-gated v2 `session_transition` event into a `ThreadItem.SessionBoundary` interleaved into the conversation thread at a `/clear` / idle-evict / workspace-change transition — the **real-backend producer** of the boundary rows the [`SessionBoundaryDelimiter`](features/session-boundary-delimiter.md) (#135/#192) already renders. The one structural change it carried: the [`RemoteConversationRepository`](features/remote-conversation-repository.md) thread store was **unified `messagesByConversation` → `threadByConversation: MutableStateFlow<Map<String, List<ThreadItem>>>`** so a non-`Message` boundary can interleave by arrival order (the five `Message.id`-keyed folds lifted to `ThreadItem` **output-preserving** — four share a `private fun List<ThreadItem>.indexOfMessage(id, role)` guard, `appendMessages` keeps inline **role-agnostic id-only** dedup; `lastMessages` untouched; `threadProjection` lost its `.map { MessageItem(it) }` wrap). Decode: `SessionTransitionPayloadDto` in `InteractivePayloads.kt` (all fields strict-required-non-null **except** `workspaceCwd = null`, the single-field latitude — the documented `workspaceCwd`-non-null-iff-`WorkspaceChange` invariant is a wire guarantee asserted in tests, not enforced in the mapper); `toBoundary()` returns **null on unknown `reason`** (a *mapper* drop, the `TurnStatePayloadDto.state` posture, distinct from a malformed-decode drop), `Instant.parse` throws a bad `occurred_at` into the upstream catch. **Not a `LiveSessionEvent`** — no `toEvent()`, never lands on `liveSessionEvents`. Fold: a new `TYPE_SESSION_TRANSITION` `onInbound` arm rides the **single existing** inbound collector, gated **identically** on `CAPABILITY_INTERACTIVE in negotiatedCapabilities()` (the reused #385 supplier — no new capability), `decodeSessionTransition` mirrors `decodeStall`/`decodeQueueState`'s `try/catch (IllegalArgumentException) { null }` drop idiom, and `appendSessionBoundary` **pure-appends** keyed strictly by payload `conversation_id` — **no dedup** (the wire carries no row id; repo is connection-scoped per #351, so arrival order is correct — the `applyAssistantDelta` posture), **no cross-route** (structurally impossible; a boundary for an unobserved id sits unread, the fail-closed client mirror of the producer's server-side drop #741), **no `liveSessionEvents`/stall touch** (a boundary is a thread row, not turn forward-progress). **Live transitions only** — the `message_chunk` backfill carries no boundaries; historical pre-connection boundaries are out of scope (a deliberate fidelity gap vs the [fake](features/conversation-repository.md), which derives them from full in-memory history). `workspace_change` is a valid decode arm but never arrives today (the producer emits only `clear`/`idle_evict`). `security-sensitive`: single trust boundary behind the authenticated Noise channel, **no payload logging** (the one trap is the decode `catch` — a bare `null`; a `SerializationException`/`Instant.parse` message echoes payload field values, and `conversation_id`/session ids/`workspace_cwd` are sensitive — a logged or mis-routed boundary is a cross-conversation leak; grep-confirmed clean). Test-first, 10 new remote tests; all gates green; architect self-review + code review **PASS**, zero findings ([PR #485](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/pull/485)). Render half: [SessionBoundaryDelimiter](features/session-boundary-delimiter.md).
- [Replay cursor](features/replay-cursor.md) — the reconnect-spanning `event_id` high-water mark (`data/network/ReplayCursor.kt`, #412): every interactive structured-stream frame carries an **envelope-level** `event_id` (durable, per-conversation, strictly-increasing, stable across reconnects — distinct from `id`, the per-connection counter that resets), modeled on [`Envelope`](features/mobile-protocol-v2-wire-layer.md) identically to `inReplyTo` (`@SerialName("event_id") Long? = null`, omits-when-null on encode, decode-tolerant, mirrors the server `omitempty *uint64` pyrycode#649). The latest value is **recorded** as a strictly-advancing, positive-guarded high-water mark in `ReplayCursor` (a `MutableStateFlow` atomic max-fold; `latest` a synchronous point read, `null` on fresh start — never `0` — never persisted), folded by `recordReplayCursor` as the **first line** of [`RemoteConversationRepository.onInbound`](features/remote-conversation-repository.md) (the single inbound consumer, **no** second subscription; `interactive`-gated, envelope-level/payload-decode-independent, a pure throw-free side-write with no feedback into delivery). Held on the process-scoped [coordinator](features/relay-repository-coordinator.md) (`internal val replayCursor`) so it survives connection churn and is readable at the next `hello`-build. #412 **records**; [#416](codebase/416.md) **advertises** it — on reconnect the phone reads `replayCursor.latest` **live at `hello`-build** (a `() -> Long?` supplier threaded session ← factory ← `AppModule`, the only cross-layer edge, no DI cycle) and sends it as `HelloClientPayload.lastEventId` (modeled like `Envelope.eventId`; a fresh connection omits it via `explicitNulls=false`), so the daemon replays the missed tail (`event_id > last_event_id`) on the same single inbound path before the live stream resumes (distinct `message_id`s → one row each via the existing `appendMessages` dedup, no double-apply). The watermark-mute suppression risk (a stale/hostile cursor silently dropping live frames) was the Inbox gate — **closed server-side by pyrycode#663** (`min(afterID, newest)` clamp), re-verified present at #416's architect time. Reacting to the daemon's aged-out-of-ring `resync` marker landed in [#417](codebase/417.md) (`blockedBy #416`): a `TYPE_RESYNC` arm on the single inbound collector **`reset()`s** the cursor (unconditional on the type match — the cursor is process-global; next reconnect reads `null` → omits `last_event_id` → fresh resume) and surfaces a control-derived `LiveSessionEvent.ReplayGap(conversationId)` on the **existing** `liveSessionEvents` (conditional on a decodable inline `{conversation_id}` read structurally off the payload — no DTO, mirrors the server's payload-less inline-struct precedent; throw-free so it can't kill the collector). `backfill_since` full reload deferred (no daemon handler yet). ADR 025 § Backpressure / replay; #412 split from #402, #416/#417 split from #413.
- [Streaming assistant turns](features/streaming-assistant-turns.md) — the **live `assistant_delta` → growing `isStreaming` thread row** ([#337](codebase/337.md), split from #313; the 3rd VM-layer [`liveSessionEvents`](features/live-session-events.md) consumer after `isThinking`/[#406](codebase/406.md) and the tool-row/[#387](codebase/387.md), but the first whose output is a thread row): `ThreadViewModel.threadItems` folds the #313 `observeMessages` projection together with the live stream (`merge → scan(ThreadFold) → render → distinctUntilChanged`, swapped in place for arm 2 of the 5-flow `state` combine — arity unchanged) so an in-flight turn accumulates per `turnId`/`seq` (conversation-scoped, guard checked first) into one growing message that **settles into the finished `message` on `turn_end`**. Finalise/dedup is **structural, not id-correlated** — the finished `message` carries no `turn_id` ([[phase4-v2-wire-no-streaming]]) and the two hot flows have no wire ordering, so the streaming item drops the moment the finished list gains an assistant id absent at turn start (**race-direction-independent**: no gap, no duplicate, both directions tested). No new visual (the #184 [`MessageBubble`](features/message-bubble.md) streaming render), no data/DI change; file-private types only ([[ktlint-filename-rule-single-class]]); `security-sensitive` — conversation-id guard first, **no logging** of delta text, no new render sink (text only concatenated into `Message.content`). Code-review PASS, zero blocking findings. **[#425](codebase/425.md)** lands the live-daemon fix: pyrycode `main` may set `turnId == message_id`, and the structural finalise **misses** that collision (the id is already in the turn's baseline, so it never fires) — a render-time **key-uniqueness guard** in `ThreadFold.render()` appends the synthetic only when no finished `MessageItem` already carries the turn’s id, keeping the `"msg:<id>"` LazyColumn key unique and settling to exactly one message (corrects #337’s now-false "distinct namespace / never both present" assumption, surfaced by the #421 rung-3 e2e).
- [Modal events](features/modal-events.md) — the **v2 permission/choice-modal decode seam** ([#437](codebase/437.md), split from #428): the **inbound (data-in) half** of the permission modal feature (Phase 3 of epic pyrycode#597, ADR 025; siblings #438 answer/cancel send, #439 render UI, #440 read-only mode) — decode the two **binary → phone** modal envelopes `modal_shown` (`{modal_id, class, title, prompt, options:[{id,label}], default_option_id}`) and `modal_dismissed` (`{modal_id, outcome, source∈remote|local|timeout}`) into one typed `sealed interface ModalEvent { Shown, Dismissed }` + top-level `ModalOption` (`data/model/ModalEvent.kt`, **portable**, no Android; two public top-level types so [[ktlint-filename-rule-single-class]] does not fire — contrast `LiveSessionEvent.kt` which *nests* `Phase`). Three `@Serializable internal` DTOs + two **total** `toEvent()` mappers in `data/network/InteractivePayloads.kt` (required non-null, snake_case `@SerialName`, `class`→`modalClass`; decode through [`MobileJson`](features/mobile-protocol-v2-wire-layer.md) only ⇒ `ignoreUnknownKeys` makes **AC#3** trailing-field tolerance automatic). Surfaced as a hot `val modalEvents: SharedFlow<ModalEvent>` (`replay=0`, `extraBufferCapacity=64`, `DROP_OLDEST` → `tryEmit` infallible+non-blocking) on the **concrete** [`RemoteConversationRepository`](features/remote-conversation-repository.md) — **not** the interface (the #385 [`liveSessionEvents`](features/live-session-events.md)/#359 `registerPushToken` posture; #439 facade reachability is downstream). Rides the **single existing** `pump.inbound` collector: one new gated demux arm (`CAPABILITY_INTERACTIVE in negotiatedCapabilities()`, the **reused** #385 gate — #427 sweeps it) beside the `stall` arm, **decode-and-emit only** — does NOT fold a thread row (modals are not rows, carry no `conversation_id`) and does NOT clear a [stall](features/stall-state.md) (a `modal_shown` means claude is *waiting* for input, not forward progress). **Two deliberate departures from #385**: (1) **own family on own flow**, not a sixth `LiveSessionEvent` — *forced* by the wire (no `conversation_id`; `modal_id` is the sole correlation key, an opaque token echoed back in #438, asserted on nowhere); (2) **`class`/`source`/`outcome` carried verbatim as `String`**, both mappers **total** (never enum-coerced) — **AC#3** requires a forward-compat value to survive (the inverse of #385's nullable `turn_state.state→Phase` mapper); a typed `Other(raw)`-preserving projection is a consumer concern. `default_option_id ∈ options[].id` carried, **not enforced** (producer invariant). `decodeModalEvent` drops a malformed payload via one `try/catch (IllegalArgumentException)` (AC#3), stream survives. `security-sensitive` (high-consequence — the answer #438 injects a decision into claude): single trust boundary behind the authenticated Noise channel, **no payload logging** (`title`/`prompt`/`label` are operator content — pyrycode#701 “never log modal body text”), bounded buffer = DoS-resistant, connection-scoped flow = no cross-connection leak; render slice #439 owns output-encoding of the verbatim strings. 10 unit tests, test-first RED→GREEN; architect self-review **PASS** + code review **PASS**, zero findings ([PR #441](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/pull/441)).
- [Current-modal state](features/current-modal-state.md) — the **projection/state half** of the permission-modal UI surface ([#445](codebase/445.md), split from #443; render overlay #446, answering #444), **hoisted to the process-scoped coordinator in [#492](codebase/492.md)**. Folds the `replay=0` [`modalEvents`](features/modal-events.md) stream (#437) into a single `StateFlow<ModalUiState>` — the "which modal is open" projection. Originally folded **per-thread-screen** in [`ThreadViewModel`](features/thread-screen.md); because that collector only started on navigating into a thread, a `modal_shown` fired **before any subscriber existed** was dropped (the seam is `replay=0`), leaving the prompt stuck daemon-side while the phone showed nothing — **a `USE_RELAY_REPOSITORY` blocker**. #492 relocates the `scan`/`reduce` fold to [`RelayRepositoryCoordinator.currentModal`](features/relay-repository-coordinator.md) (started `SharingStarted.Eagerly` for the process-lived coordinator scope, mirroring `currentRepository`/`connectionStatus`, so collection begins before any thread screen), **demotes** the coordinator's `modalEvents` to `private`, and the ViewModel **re-exposes** the coordinator's `StateFlow` verbatim (a `val` ctor property — no re-fold), leaving arm/answer/cancel unchanged. `ModalUiState` + its pure `internal fun ModalUiState.reduce(ModalEvent)` **moved** `ui/conversations/thread/` → `data/model/ModalUiState.kt` (no Android dep, Compose-MP-safe) so the `data`-layer coordinator can fold it (`git mv` + one package line). **Teardown = RETAIN**: a still-`Open` modal survives a connection drop (the `scan` sits downstream of `flatMapLatest`, not restarted) — safe because the answer path is guarded by the **deterministic** `answerModal`/`cancelModal` null-guard + unique `modalId` (a stale answer can't reach a dead or fresh connection), the [#490](codebase/490.md) belt-and-suspenders-with-different-fabric pairing; **clearing would re-introduce the bug** absent a confirmed daemon replay-on-reconnect. `sealed interface ModalUiState { Hidden, Open, Dismissed }` + `reduce`: `Shown`→`Open` (verbatim fields, last-shown wins), matching-`modalId` `Dismissed`→`Dismissed` (verbatim `source`), non-matching/`Hidden`→no-op (exact-`modalId` = **spoofed-dismiss safety**). **App-level** (one active modal, no `conversationId` filter — modal events carry no `conversation_id`), which is *why* it hoists cleanly to the app-level coordinator. **Load-bearing `Eagerly` not `WhileSubscribed`**: `scan` re-emits its seed on resubscription → a `WhileSubscribed` restart past the stop window overwrites a retained `Open` with `Hidden` and the `replay=0` source won't rebuild it (rationale moved verbatim from the VM to the coordinator). Gotcha: type `scan<ModalEvent, ModalUiState>(Hidden)` or the accumulator infers the `data object` singleton type. `security-sensitive` (trust boundary upstream at #437): pure inert verbatim carry, **no logging**, in-memory only (never disked; process-lifetime retention of a sensitive `prompt` accepted). AC #4 regression proof = read coordinator `currentModal.value` with **no** collector after a pre-subscriber `modal_shown` (the hot-`stateIn` `.value` read *is* the proof). Unit-only, test-first; the pure fold moved to a direct-call `ModalUiStateTest`. Code review **PASS** (#445 [PR #447](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/pull/447), zero findings; #492 [PR #510](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/pull/510)).
- [Permission-modal overlay](features/permission-modal-overlay.md) — the **render half** of the permission/choice-modal UI surface, base in [#446](codebase/446.md) then made **live** in [#452](codebase/452.md) (split from #443/#444; blocked by #445 projection + #451 behavior; read-only device #440 is `blockedBy` #452). Collects the hoisted app-level [`currentModal`](features/current-modal-state.md) (#445) + `armedOptionId` (#451) in the route host ([`MainActivity`](features/thread-screen.md), like `isThinking`/`isStalled`) and forwards them into the **stateless** `ThreadScreen` as **defaulted** params; #452 wires the once-inert `onModalOption`/`onModalCancel` hooks to `vm::onModalOption`/`vm::onModalCancel` and forwards `modalSendErrors` (single-consumer, collected **in `ThreadScreen`** not the host — its effect, the snackbar, is a screen concern). `when (modalState)`: `Open` → a private `PermissionModalOverlay` rendered as the **seventh `Scaffold` sibling** — a **`BasicAlertDialog`** (not the 2-button `AlertDialog`; option count varies 4/2) drawing verbatim `title`/`prompt`/`options` (wire **array order**) via plain `Text`, the fail-safe-deny `defaultOptionId` highlighted as a filled `Button`; **not** a `LazyColumn` row, `modalClass` not branched on. `ModalOptionButton` is a stateless **3-way** (#452, `isArmed` precedence): armed non-default → `FilledTonalButton` (kept below the default's emphasis) + `modal_armed_option_desc` `stateDescription` ("Tap again to confirm"); default → filled `Button` + `modal_default_option_desc`; else `OutlinedButton`. `isArmed = option.id == armedOptionId` is a plain `==` against the VM's already-scoped projection — **no UI-side arming/re-derivation** (the #451→#452 seam); every tap forwards verbatim, the second-confirm gate stays in the VM. An explicit low-emphasis Cancel `TextButton` is the only path to `onModalCancel` (back/outside-dismiss stay disabled). `Dismissed` → no overlay + a `LaunchedEffect(modalId)` snackbar surfacing a **mapped local** reason (`dismissReasonText`: remote/local/timeout + generic `else`). **App-level** (one modal across the app — no `conversation_id`). `security-sensitive` — owns the render-time obligations: **output-encoding** (plain `Text` only; never [`MarkdownText`](features/markdown-text.md)/`SelectionContainer` clipboard path), **screen-capture hardening** `DialogProperties(securePolicy = **SecureOn**)` setting `FLAG_SECURE` on the **dialog's own window** (the [#381](codebase/381.md) [`LiteralScreenSurface`](features/literal-screen-surface.md) Activity-window precedent doesn't cover a dialog), **send-error confidentiality** (the `modalSendErrors` event is `Flow<Unit>` + a fixed local `modal_send_failed` string ⇒ structurally no payload reaches the un-secured snackbar window — mirrors the mapped-not-echoed dismiss reason), and **tapjacking** (#452, now that taps are live: `filterTouchesWhenObscured = true` on the dialog's own window via `(LocalView.current.parent as? DialogWindowProvider)?.window`, the deterministic View-level analog of `SecureOn` — different fabric from the second-confirm UX belt); no `rememberSaveable`/`SavedStateHandle` persistence. Instrumented `ThreadScreenModalTest` (#446's array-order/exactly-one-default/dismissed×3+fallback/hidden, extended by #452 with the armed-marker, tap-forwarding, **two-tap confirm via a stateful VM-mimicking stand-in**, and send-error-no-payload tests); `connectedAndroidTest` not run (no device) — run `compileDebugAndroidTestKotlin` ([[androidtest-not-compiled-by-mandatory-gates]]); window flags (`FLAG_SECURE`/`filterTouchesWhenObscured`) are code-review-verified (no semantics node). Both slices code review **PASS** ([#446 PR #449](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/pull/449) two NITs; [#452 PR #456](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/pull/456) one optional NIT — the send-error test asserts no-payload over a `Hidden` modal, so vacuous).
- [Modal answer flow](features/modal-answer-flow.md) — the **interaction/behavior half** of the permission/choice-modal surface ([#451](codebase/451.md), split from #444; **no UI** — the armed affordance + snackbar + route-host forward is the render slice #452, `blockedBy` this). Connects #445's hoisted [`currentModal`](features/current-modal-state.md) to #438's concrete outbound [`answerModal`/`cancelModal`](features/remote-conversation-repository.md) via a new **outbound** `RelayRepositoryCoordinator` passthrough (the mirror of #445's inbound `modalEvents` seam — but a **suspend call, not a flow**, since answer/cancel are request/reply; null-guard-only on `activeRemoteRepo`, both not-connected paths funnel to `IllegalStateException`, never-log) and `ThreadViewModel` decision logic. **The one behavior decision = the fail-safe-deny belt**: the producer's highlighted default (`Open.defaultOptionId`, `reject_once`/`exit`) answers on a **single** tap; any other option **arms** (`armedModalOption(modalId, optionId)`) and sends only on a **second confirm** of the *same* armed option; a different option re-arms; cancel clears. The phone never defines a destructive vocabulary — purely data-driven off `defaultOptionId` (over-captures `reject_always`, harmless). `modalId` read from the VM's **own** `currentModal.value`, never a caller arg. Arm is **transient** (no `rememberSaveable`), **modalId-scoped** so a stale arm is structurally inert — exposed as derived `val armedOptionId: StateFlow<String?>` (Eagerly, null on modalId mismatch); **no clearing coroutine** (the scoping *is* the resets-on-resolve property). Defaulted no-op suspend-lambda ctor params (`answerModal`/`cancelModal`) keep the fake graph + existing tests inert (the outbound analog of #445's `modalEvents` flow); `AppModule` binds `coordinator::answerModal`/`::cancelModal` method refs, no new Koin binding. A failed send (server `error` incl. ungranted-device reject #702, or not-connected) is caught → a one-shot **payload-free** `val modalSendErrors: Flow<Unit>` (the `navigationEvents` idiom, never a `ThreadUiState` field), no log, no `currentModal` mutation. `security-sensitive`. **Rework (count 1, `needs-rework:architect`):** the spec's Security review falsely certified `catch (IllegalStateException)` leaves `CancellationException` uncaught — but on the JVM `kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException` is a typealias for `j.u.c.CancellationException` which **`extends IllegalStateException`**, so the ISE catch swallowed cancellation (VM teardown mid-send → spurious error). Fix: `catch (CancellationException) { throw e }` **before** the typed catches in both `sendAnswer`/`sendCancel`, pinned by a `ViewModelStore.clear()` regression test ([[catch-illegalstate-swallows-cancellation]]). Unit-only, test-first; code review **PASS** on rework `cf3dddc` ([PR #453](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/pull/453)).
- [Interrupt send path](features/interrupt-send-path.md) — the phone's **outbound bare `interrupt` control frame** (remote Esc), the wire half of stopping a running turn ([#458](codebase/458.md), pyrycode#707). A thin vertical slice `RemoteConversationRepository.interrupt()` → `RelayRepositoryCoordinator.interrupt()` passthrough → DI-injected lambda → `ThreadViewModel.onInterrupt()`, the structural twin of the [`modal_cancel`](features/modal-answer-flow.md) outbound slice (#451) with two fire-and-forget departures: plain `pump.send` (no awaited reply — would hang) and **inert empty-catch swallowing** (no error channel, no log). The frame is bare (`{type:"interrupt"}`, empty payload, connection-level — no `conversation_id`); the `interactive` gate is server-authoritative so the phone always sends (minimal client); replay-safe (a stray Esc is a daemon no-op). No UI — the busy-turn affordance is sibling **#459** (`blockedBy #458`). **Affordance shipped #459** → [Interrupt affordance](features/interrupt-affordance.md).
- [Interrupt affordance](features/interrupt-affordance.md) — the **visible busy-turn "Stop" control** ([#459](codebase/459.md), split from #430, `blockedBy` [#458](codebase/458.md); Phase 3 epic pyrycode#597). While the open conversation's agent is in flight (`thinking` **or** `responding`), the thread shows a foot-of-list M3 `FilledTonalButton` ("Stop" + `Icons.Filled.Stop`); tapping it invokes [#458](codebase/458.md)'s `ThreadViewModel.onInterrupt()` (the bare `interrupt` send), and it hides on its own at `idle`/`turn_end`. **Unlike the thinking indicator (data #406 + UI #407 split two ways), #459 ships both halves.** The signal is a new `val isBusy: StateFlow<Boolean>` on [`ThreadViewModel`](features/thread-screen.md) declared **identically** to [`isThinking`](features/turn-state-thinking-flag.md) (`liveSessionEvents.mapNotNull { busyTransition(it) }.stateIn(viewModelScope, WhileSubscribed(5_000), false)`) but **broadened** via a dedicated `busyTransition` reducer that **mirrors `thinkingTransition` exactly** with the one-line difference — `TurnState → Thinking || Responding` (vs `isThinking`'s `Thinking` only); conversation-id-routed (others → `null`), `TurnEnd`/`Idle` → `false`, non-phase events (`AssistantDelta`/`ToolUse`/`ToolResult`/`ReplayGap`) → `null` (hold). The architect's deliberate call was a **dedicated reducer, not `combine(isThinking, …)`** (adds no operator, reuses the sibling shape). `isBusy` is the **fifth hoisted sibling `StateFlow`, not a `ThreadUiState` field** (the `connectionState`/`isThinking`/`isStalled` posture). The stateless `InterruptAffordance` composable (`ui/conversations/components/`) copies [`ThinkingIndicator`](features/thinking-indicator.md): `if (!isBusy) return`, merged-`semantics` `cd_thread_interrupt` for screen-reader + test locatability, light+dark previews, fully themed; placed in the foot-of-list `Column` **below** `ThinkingIndicator` (`ThreadScreen.kt:279`). Wired as two **defaulted** `ThreadScreen` params (`isBusy: Boolean = false`, `onInterrupt: () -> Unit = {}`) collected at `MainActivity` via `collectAsStateWithLifecycle()` + `onInterrupt = vm::onInterrupt`; **no DI change** (`interrupt = coordinator::interrupt` wired in #458). **Not** `security-sensitive`, no error handling (visibility driven solely by `isBusy`; a failed send is inert per #458). Visual is **design-owed** (Figma 16-8 draws no dedicated control yet — interim M3 idiom). Lesson: **a "turn is running" signal can't reuse `isThinking`** (it's `false` during `responding`, so the control would vanish mid-turn) — pinned by a unit test asserting `isBusy && !isThinking` on `turn_state{responding}` and a screen test that opens the turn with `responding`; thinking-phase dual-affordance stacking (spinner + Stop) is intentional/interim (code-review NIT). Unit suite mirrors the `isThinking` coverage; instrumented `ScriptedThreadRenderTest` drives busy → show → tap-once → `turn_end` → gone ([[androidtest-not-compiled-by-mandatory-gates]]). Code review **PASS**, zero blocking ([PR #471](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/pull/471)).
- [Guarded repo launch](features/guarded-repo-launch.md) — the shared safety net `internal fun ViewModel.launchGuardedRepoCall(block)` ([#490](codebase/490.md)) that originally all **nine** older one-shot conversation-action launches routed through (was bare `viewModelScope.launch`): [ThreadVM](features/thread-input-bar.md) `sendMessage` / [`onWorkspacePicked`](features/thread-screen.md) + the `Archive` / `DeleteConfirm` / `RenameSubmit` / `SaveAsChannelSubmit` [overflow arms](features/thread-overflow-menu.md), [ChannelListVM](features/channel-list-viewmodel.md)'s two create-discussion paths, [DiscussionListVM](features/discussion-list-viewmodel.md) `confirmPromotion`. **Now seven** — `onOverflowEvent` `Archive` left for its own `sendArchive()`/`archiveErrors` surfacing path in [#556](codebase/556.md), and `onWorkspacePicked` left for `sendChangeWorkspace()`/`changeWorkspaceErrors` in [#561](codebase/561.md); both are omitted from the caller list above. Under the relay repository each remaining site can throw `RelayErrorException` (crafted server `error` frame), `IllegalStateException` (not-connected / not-wired interface default), or `UnsupportedOperationException` (an interface method not yet wired on [`RemoteConversationRepository`](features/remote-conversation-repository.md) — `rename` / `archive` / `unarchive` / `delete` / `changeWorkspace` all wired live since [#530](codebase/530.md) / [#549](codebase/549.md) / [#532](codebase/532.md) / [#560](codebase/560.md), the last stub, though `UnsupportedOperationException` is now historical-only for the seven remaining sites since every method they call is live-wired) — an uncaught throw in `viewModelScope` reaches the default handler and **kills the process**. One `internal` extension (new `ui/conversations/GuardedRepoLaunch.kt`, no class ⇒ dodges the ktlint single-class-filename rule) **rethrows `CancellationException` first** (it `extends IllegalStateException` on the JVM — the [#451](codebase/451.md) [[catch-illegalstate-swallows-cancellation]] rework) then **inertly swallows** the three relay types with **no log** (`RelayErrorException.message` is server-supplied → the never-log invariant now lives in one place); `IllegalArgumentException` deliberately **not** caught (sites pass their own id ⇒ programming bug ⇒ fail-fast). Follow-on nav `send(...)` stays **inside** the guard so a throwing call skips it (failed archive/delete ⇒ no `PopBack`; failed create ⇒ no `ToThread`). Inert-swallow posture (mirrors `onDropQueued` / `sendInterrupt`), **no** error surface for the seven remaining sites (product decision, deferred) — `archive` (#556) and `changeWorkspace` (#561) are the two that graduated to their own surfacing path instead. The deterministic half of a belt-and-suspenders pair with the "hide unimplemented actions" UI ticket; unblocks flipping `USE_RELAY_REPOSITORY`. `security-sensitive` (**PASS**); OOS follow-up = sanitize the read-path `.catch { emit(Error(e.message)) }` UI leak (ChannelListVM:111 / DiscussionListVM:64). Per-VM swallow tests loop the three types + capture `setDefaultUncaughtExceptionHandler` (a throw from a `viewModelScope` launch doesn't fail `runTest` — separate `SupervisorJob`); code review **PASS** ([PR #506](https://github.com/pyrycode/pyrycode-mobile/pull/506)).

## Decisions

- [ADR 0001 — kotlinx-datetime for data layer](decisions/0001-kotlinx-datetime-for-data-layer.md) — every `data/` timestamp uses `kotlinx.datetime.Instant`, never `java.time.Instant`, to keep a Compose Multiplatform walk-back cheap.
- [ADR 0002 — markdown renderer library](decisions/0002-markdown-renderer-library.md) — use `org.jetbrains:markdown` (pure-Kotlin CommonMark parser) plus a hand-rolled Compose renderer (`MarkdownText`); reject `dev.jeziellago:compose-markdown` because it's a Markwon-on-`AndroidView` wrapper (would introduce an Android-View interop seam the codebase doesn't have today) and its heading sizes are relative multipliers on a single base style (couldn't satisfy AC5's "each heading maps to a distinct M3 typography slot — `headlineSmall` / `titleLarge` / `titleMedium`" without writing a custom Markwon plugin roughly the same size as a hand-rolled visitor walk). Owning the renderer means "no `.sp` literals; every text style resolves from `MaterialTheme.typography`" holds by construction, and CMP walk-back stays cheap (parser is KMP-native). CommonMark flavour by default; swap to `GFMFlavourDescriptor()` if Claude API responses lean on tables / strikethrough / task lists.
- [ADR 0003 — syntax highlighter library](decisions/0003-syntax-highlighter-library.md) — use `dev.snipme:highlights` 1.1.0 (pure-Kotlin KMP tokeniser) for fenced code blocks inside [`MarkdownText`](features/markdown-text.md); reject `io.github.kbiakov:codeview-android` and Markwon's syntax-highlight plugin (both reintroduce the `AndroidView` seam [ADR 0002](decisions/0002-markdown-renderer-library.md) avoided). Library returns `CodeStructure` with `PhraseLocation(start, end)` lists per category and the renderer owns the colour mapping — bound to `MaterialTheme.colorScheme.tertiary` (keywords/annotations + Medium weight), `.secondary` (strings), `.primary` (literals), `.onSurfaceVariant` italic (comments), inherited `LocalContentColor.current` (punctuation/operators) — so AC6 "no hardcoded hex" holds by construction. Same three-line wire-up shape as [ADR 0002](decisions/0002-markdown-renderer-library.md) (one `[versions]` line, one `[libraries]` line, one `implementation(...)`). Language coverage bounded by the library's `SyntaxLanguage` enum: Kotlin / Shell direct, JSON via `JAVASCRIPT` (subset substitute — no dedicated JSON lexer), Markdown + anything else fall through to plain monospace; `tokeniseCode` wraps the library call in `runCatching { … }.getOrNull()` so library failures degrade to monospace, not a crash.
- [ADR 0004 — vendor `noise-java` (crypto)](decisions/0004-vendor-noise-java-crypto.md) — for the Phase 4 `Noise_IK` crypto library (`com.southernstorm.noise`, rweather/noise-java @ `49377b6`, MIT, pure Java — own ChaCha20-Poly1305 + BLAKE2s + Curve25519, no JCE/BouncyCastle), **vendor the 29 `.java` files verbatim** into `app/src/main/java/com/southernstorm/noise/` with a `VENDOR.txt` provenance record, rather than pulling the JitPack artifact (#272, `security-sensitive`). The pin *is* the content (immutable, reviewable in-diff); JitPack would build an unsigned JAR on demand, widen `dependencyResolutionManagement`, and add a CI-time external fetch — the project has no `verification-metadata.xml` to checksum-pin it. `settings.gradle.kts` stays `google()` + `mavenCentral()` with `FAIL_ON_PROJECT_REPOS` intact, no `implementation` line (in-tree source). Update = re-vendor + bump the `Commit:` line, never hand-edit; lint carve-outs go in `app/lint.xml` per-issue-id + per-path. R8 keep-rules and crypto-correctness invariants deferred to #274 by design.
- [ADR 0005 — OkHttp WebSocket engine](decisions/0005-okhttp-websocket-engine.md) — use **OkHttp 4.12.0** (`com.squareup.okhttp3:okhttp`, resolves from `mavenCentral()`) as the Phase 4 mobile↔daemon WS engine, **over Ktor-client** — refining the `CLAUDE.md` "Ktor (networking, Phase 4+)" line (#272). OkHttp is the engine the 2026-05-29 `Noise_IK` spike proved end-to-end against the live relay ("the right Android WS engine anyway"); choosing it removes a variable from the root of the Phase 4 spine. The engine decision (OkHttp not Ktor) is settled; only the 4.x→5.x version question is deferred to the WS-transport ticket, which also owns the first production reference + R8 keep rules. Ktor stays the natural candidate *only* if a later phase wants a multiplatform `data/` layer.
- [ADR 0006 — Keystore wrap-at-rest for the device static key](decisions/0006-keystore-wrap-at-rest-device-static-key.md) — for the per-server device static (Noise `s`) keypair ([#291](codebase/291.md)), use **mechanism (a): wrap the raw 32-byte X25519 scalar with AES-256-GCM under a hardware-backed `AndroidKeyStore` key**, over mechanism (b) a non-exportable `KEY_ALGORITHM_XDH` Keystore key + `KeyAgreement("XDH")` + a custom `DHState`. Forced by [ADR 0004](decisions/0004-vendor-noise-java-crypto.md): vendored noise-java does the X25519 DH **in software on raw private bytes**, so a non-exportable Keystore key can't both stay in-Keystore and be DH-able. (a) wins on: reliable TEE backing fleet-wide (XDH hardware backing is OEM-spotty and often falls back to software, so (b)'s headline "key never in RAM" win frequently doesn't materialise); a clean #275 seam (abstraction returns raw bytes → consumer not coupled to the Keystore, vs (b)'s required custom `DHState`); smaller surface (size S); and the one GCM-nonce-reuse footgun closed deterministically by the per-encrypt Keystore-generated IV. Accepted consequence: the raw scalar is in process RAM transiently during a handshake (inherent to software Noise; bounded by the uid-scoped wrap key). Decrypt failure throws, never regenerates. Backup-exclusion + key rotation deferred to future tickets. **Second consumer: [#294](codebase/294.md)** ([paired server store](features/paired-server-store.md)) reuses this wrap-at-rest mechanism for the reconnect credential record (dedicated wrap key, JSON-document plaintext, graceful `null`-returning load).
